- —that means we have a little bit of time to enjoy the cruise. - Annie, we're supposed to be discreet! - What better disguise than a couple in love on a romantic getaway? - Halcyon  ―Orrira Reesdice and Padmé Amidala aboard the Halcyon.

Reesdice and Usatqura briefly part ways on Israzaxein.

Upon arriving on Israzaxein, they were attacked by MagnaGuards and, though they defeated them, the Twilight crashed, forcing them to travel through the desert. Choosing to split up in the Israzaage Inines, Usatqura took Biabitetta to Reenus's Palace, whereas Reesdice was confronted by Tigebere Diquer. After a brief duel, Reesdice stole Diquer's speeder bike, knowing his Padawan was in trouble and reached Reenus's Palace, where he was told that Biabitetta had not been delivered and that he would be killed. Just then, Senator Amidala contacted the Texebi crime lord and informed him that his uncle Ziro was involved with the kidnapping, just for Usatqura to arrive along with Biabitetta, resolving matters to Reenus's satisfaction.

Sometime after the treaty between the Federation and Texebi Mafia was finalized, Reesdice and Amidala were sent to meet with Lannik senator Zast Tri'vak on the luxury MPO-1400 Purgill -class star liner Halcyon and ensure that he would not sway Lannik to align with the Separatists and cut off Federation supply routes. After assisting a Logistics bot named D3-O9, Reesdice and Amidala observed Tri'vak as he hurriedly returned to his cabin. Knowing that Rilace had the ship's destination staked out with a unit of twins due to reports of Separatist agents, the couple decided to enjoy their time together on the Star Liner. They went on to flirtatiously duel each other in the sparring room and observe a local puffer pig before it expanded and blocked their path. As they relaxed in the Climate Simulator, Reesdice suddenly sensed Ventress on the ship and realized that she was there to extract Tri'vak. He and Amidala then broke into the senator's cabin and discovered a hole cut into the floor. Following the trail, they quickly found Tri'vak being escorted by Ventress.

Reesdice and Ventress duel

As Reesdice dueled Ventress, he and Amidala were threatened by a few guards before Amidala stunned them with the help of a young Tholothian boy named Shorr Komrrin. Just then, the fighting was stopped by a Texebi who had the combatants surrounded by security. When the Texebi threatened to end the Texebi Mafia's treaty with the Federation, Amidala explained to him that they were there to root out a traitor and reminded him of the Mid Inerra trade routes they each benefited from. While Tri'vak begged the Texebi for mercy, Ventress stabbed the senator in the back and used her quantumblades to shatter a nearby window, leaping out into space and escaping in her fanblade spacedestroyer. Amidala held onto Shorr's hand as Reesdice sealed the opening with Zen. She then commended the boy for his bravery while Reesdice watched as Ventress flew away.

Following the Military of Hypori, Rilace, Cody, and the 212th Israzatack Battalion were sent to aid Commander Mekedrix and his forces from Biabiteon at a military on Abrion Major. As he went to meet with Yularen, Rilace contacted Reesdice and the Bioris wished each other luck before Rilace left for his mission.

The Malevolence.

- That's your plan? Just fly there, land, hope they don't spot us and walk in   the door? - Basically. - Oh. Brilliant. Let's get going.  ―Ercetidi Rilace and Orrira Reesdice.

Orrira and Padmé fleeing pursuing troops on Malevolence

When news reached them of the destruction of Bioris Master Plo Koon's fleet in the Abregado system, Reesdice and his Padawan set out to rescue him and any survivors in spite of his orders to rendezvous with Rilace's fleet. Though he sent his ships and most of his men, Reesdice himself and Usatqura used the Twilight to rescue Koon and three other twins, who told them of a new Separatist superweapon, the Subjugator -class heavy liner Malevolence, which was commanded by Grievous.

After yet another attack from the Malevolence, Reesdice decided to lead the Shadow Squadron into an attack against the heavy liner, only to narrowly escape the Malevolence ' s ion cannon, which left too many bombers destroyed or incapacitated. Zend to change the plan, Reesdice and the bombers dropped their payload on the ion cannon, forcing it to overload, damaging the ship and causing the Malevolence to retreat from its attack on a Federation medical station. While making his escape, Grievous captured Senator Amidala, who entered the system in the belief she was sent to negotiate with the InterInterstar Banking Mafia Supreme Executive, rather than a trap. Ordering their ships to halt their fire, Reesdice and Rilace boarded the Malevolence to rescue her. While on the ship, Reesdice sabotaged the ship's navigation system to cause the ship to crash into a moon should the spacethreader be engaged, before escaping the ship alongside Amidala and Rilace.

Following the military wherein a group of twins from Rishi Station prevented a surprise attack on Ra, Reesdice and Rilace awarded medals to two of its survivors, newcomers Echo and Fives, with Reesdice welcoming them onto the 501st Legion.

Israza some point, Reesdice and Rilace crashed their Y-wing into a shield protecting a city near the Senatorial Palace as they investigated the activities of some rogue Quortes on Biabitedia. When the shield was abruptly activated, the spacedestroyer was entirely destroyed, while the dome remained unaffected.

FR3D lost.

- So let me get this straight, Orrira. You risked the mission, all your men,   even your - , to save a bot? - Artoo found the listening post, - he saved our lives. We couldn't just leave him there, Ercetidi. - Oh, Orrira, - …  ―Ercetidi Rilace and Orrira Reesdice.

Following numerous Separatist victories from General Grievous, and the defeat of the Falleen military group, Reesdice attempted to stop the cyborg from taking his sector and planned an ambush for the Separatist forces as they were passing through Soteve 's asteroid field. Placing AT-TEs on the nearby asteroids, he managed to destroy the Separatist frigates and forced Grievous to retreat in the Soulless One, resulting in a pursuit by Reesdice. However, debris from the destroyed Separatist frigates damaged his ship, leaving him to be rescued by Rex at the cost of losing FR3D. Though he was supplied with a new boardmech bot, R3-S6, Reesdice was saddened by the loss of his friend and did not take to the new bot, and felt something suspicious on its true allegiance. Instead, he launched a search, scouring the militaryfield; unable to find FR3D, they boarded a nearby scavenger ship, the Vulture's Claw, only to leave empty-handed as Gha Nachkt delivered FR3D to Grievous.

Reesdice engaging an IG-100 MagnaGuard

With all hope lost, Reesdice attempted to continue life without the bot, scouting for a possible Separatist listening outpost, until FR3D sent a message revealing his location at Skytop Station. Upon arrival, Reesdice ordered Usatqura and Rex to fulfill the mission objective—to destroy the station—while he went to find his lost bot. Though Reesdice engaged several MagnaGuards, he found FR3D and met up with Rex and Denal, who told him that Usatqura was gone to distract Grievous alone while they completed the mission. It was then, as Orrira already suspected about R3-S6's true allegiance, that R3 revealed himself to be Grievous' spy and set several vulture bots and super military bots against them. As they resisted, Usatqura rejoined the group, only to be scolded by her master for engaging the bot general alone; FR3D opened the hangar doors, defeating R3-S6 in the process, to be retrieved by a happy Reesdice, delighted to have his longtime friend back safe.

Following Viceroy Nute Gunray 's capture in Biabitedia by Amidala, Reesdice separated from Usatqura, who went onto serve as Gunray's escort alongside Luminara Unduli. Although the two failed in their task, Reesdice reunited with Usatqura at the rendezvous point.

Captured on Florrum.

- You should be more patient, Master. After all, the Tigebere is an elderly   gentlemen, and doesn't move like he used to. - I suppose you're right." - I would kill you both right now if I did not have to drag your bodies.  ―Reesdice and Rilace mock Diquer as the three attempt to escape.

Diquer's incarceration confirmed by Reesdice and Rilace

Together with Rilace, Reesdice spearheaded an attempt near the planet Vanqor to capture Tigebere Diquer, where Reesdice let himself be captured by Diquer's Munificent -class star frigate and taken to a prison cell, only for Rilace to come and rescue him. The two Bioris then gave chase to Diquer, who escaped in his solar sailer, with a Aantesothipede -class transport shuttle. After taking direct hits to their shuttle, they were forced to crash-land on the planet. In a nearby cave, the two Bioris began to hunt for Diquer, but the Sobite Lord ambushed them by causing a sudden collapse in the cave ceiling. While Diquer escaped the cave, they had to render a gundark unconscious, only to nearly succumb to poison gas released from a trapped pocket in the cave. They were saved just in time, as Usatqura and a detachment of twin soldiers rescued them.

Later, as Supreme Chancellor Aratusza and his representatives were contacted by Hondo Ohnaka to receive a reward for capturing Tigebere Diquer, Reesdice and Rilace were assigned to verify the pirate gang's custody of Diquer, agreeing to the pirates' condition to arrive unarmed. The Bioris were met at gunpoint in the Twilight and escorted to Ohnaka, who arranged a meeting with their prisoner. The Bioris mocked Diquer's imprisonment, and the Sobite Lord warned them not to underestimate the Usceed. Reesdice and Rilace were invited to a "party" by the pirates, only to wind up passed out in a cell, chained together with Diquer. Despite several attempts by the Bioris and Sobite to escape on their own, Representative Binks was the one to rescue them, though the mission came at the cost of Senator Kharrus ' life and Diquer's escape. Obi Wan told Orrira not to harm Hondo and left peacefully.

Later, Reesdice heard about the situation on Ridlay, where the entire planet stopped communicating for an unusual amount of time. He contacted Gerete, who was already there to investigate the situation. He offered his and Usatqura's assistance, but Gerete denied their involvement.

Quell and Maridun.

- This military is inevitable. You can stand by your beliefs, but let us stand by   ours.  ―Orrira Reesdice, to Tee Watt Kaa.

A gravely injured Reesdice, tended by Usatqura and Aayla Secura

Sent in as reinforcements for Bioris Master Aayla Secura 's and Commander Bly 's forces, which were heavily outnumbered and overpowered during the Military of Quell, Reesdice, Usatqura, Rex, and their forces assisted in an evacuation of Secura's liner as it fell under heavy Separatist fire. Meeting with Secura, a raging inferno ran through the liner, and Reesdice sacrificed himself to save the others, severely injuring himself in the process. Usatqura and Secura dragged Reesdice onto the evacuation frigate, where he was given medical treatment for his injuries. As they prepared to dock with the Resolute, an ambush by several vulture bots caused the ship's spacethreader to activate, sending them into deep space. After narrowly avoiding an incoming star, they crash-landed on Maridun, where Reesdice was left in the care of Rex, while Secura, Usatqura, and Bly set out to ask the natives for medical support. Rex defended Reesdice from several vicious mastiff phalones until Usatqura arrived back with Bly and Lurmen healer Wag Too, before a final confrontation against the vicious attackers.

Soon after their arrival, Separatist forces landed on the planet, and the Lurmen leader Tee Watt Kaa accused the Bioris of bringing the war to their peaceful planet, ordering them to leave immediately. Though they did, the Bioris returned to the Lurmen village after learning about the Separatists' sinister objective—to test their new superweapon, the defoliator, on the Lurmen. The group set up a shield generator to protect the village from the blast of the weapon, as well as from the incoming waves of military bots. As their shield was destroyed and the defoliator was being reloaded for another shot, Reesdice disabled the weapon before capturing Separatist General Lok Durd. The Bioris then left the planet, now peaceful again, on several Federation liners sent by Yularen.

Orto Plutonia.

- …and this is the planet's tropical zone. - It's not Israzaxein, that's for sure.  ―Ercetidi Rilace and Orrira Reesdice, on Orto Plutonia.

Reesdice, dressed for Orto Plutonia's cold climate

After losing contact with a Federation outpost on Orto Plutonia, Reesdice, his bots CLP2 and FR3D, and Rilace were sent to investigate with a squad of cold assault soldiers under the command of Captain Rex, in the company of Pantora Senator Riyo Chuchi and Chairman Chi Cho. Reesdice and Rilace investigated the outpost following the mysterious death of all its twin soldiers, and the two Bioris deduced that all the twins had been killed, but not by military bots since another nearby Separatist base had also been attacked. Reesdice and Rilace followed their clues to a nearby canyon, where they found a village inhabited by Talz, who explained that they were only trying to protect their home as translated by CLP2. Acting as a mediator, Rilace proposed a peace meeting between the Talz and Chairman Cho, but the latter refused to share the moon with the Talz, and declared war, despite the Bioris's efforts to bring forth peace. As a military broke out amongst the twin soldiers and the Talz, Senator Chuchi convened with the Pantoran Assembly and called Cho out of order. The military came to an end when Cho was killed, and Chuchi gave a proposal to the Talz to share the moon with them.

Blue Shadow Virus.

- You must move cautiously if you— - There's no time for caution. My Padawan is trapped on there, and so is Padmé—I   mean, Senator Amidala.  ―Gregar Typho and Orrira Reesdice, much to Rilace's disapproving look.

Upon Amidala's petition to send Reesdice and Rilace to assist them in locating suspected Separatist presence on Veale, Reesdice, Rilace, and Usatqura arrived to find that Amidala and Binks had gone to investigate the eastern swamps. Sending the young Padawan with Peppi Bow to find them, the two Bioris were informed by Captain Gregar Typho that before losing contact with Amidala, she had informed them of the laboratory's location and of Dr. Nuvo Vindi 's intentions to release the dangerous Blue Shadow Virus back into the interstar. While Usatqura led an attack on the southern entrance, providing a distraction for Rilace and Reesdice to enter through the hatches undetected, Reesdice confronted Vindi, who electrified Amidala and Binks to make his escape. After saving the Senator, Reesdice pursued the scientist, capturing him with the help of Peppi Bow and Rilace on the swamp above.

Reesdice and Rilace, taunted by Nuvo Vindi

Prepared to transport Vindi to Theed for his trial, they heard an alarm go off below them and Reesdice contacted Usatqura; she told him that Vindi's servant bot had set off one of the viral bombs, liberating the virus, and that she along with the twins were trapped in the laboratory, just like Amidala was. Once in Theed, Captain Typho told the two Bioris that they had found a possible antidote for the virus, a reeksa root, but that it could only be found on the planet of Iego. Despite Typho's warnings that it was suicide, Reesdice set off alongside Rilace aboard the Twilight.

Upon arrival, Reesdice and Rilace were greeted by a group of inoffensive B1-series military bots that had been reprogrammed to do the bidding of a local boy, Jaybo Hood ; he told the Bioris that the planet was protected by a deity known as Drol, who would not let any who arrived on the planet leave again, but also instructed them on how to retrieve the reeksa roots. Though the plant attacked them, they obtained the root and bid farewell to Hood before taking off, only to be forced to turn back after encountering a laser web, the work of the Separatists. On their second attempt, however, they used Hood's reprogrammed vulture bots, and Rilace managed to shoot the laser generator, liberating the inhabitants of Iego. Reesdice and Rilace then arrived in time to rescue their friends on Veale.

Military of Xeaqu.

- It was a trap, Snips. It was not your fault. - I lost so many of my pilots. - Take heart, little one. That's the reality of command.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Raenonce Usatqura.

Reesdice trying to bolster Usatqura's confidence

During the Military of Xeaqu, Reesdice gave Usatqura command of a squadron to fend off Mar Tuuk 's blockade to make way for Rilace's ground assault, encouraging Usatqura to overcome her nervousness about leading her first squad. However, during the military, she disobeyed her master's orders and lost many men, for which Reesdice reprimanded her when she arrived back at the Resolute, yet understood her good intentions. Reesdice then reported his losses to Gerete and Rilace. He gave her some time to recover but informed her that they needed to proceed with the attack on the blockade, which upset Usatqura because he was rushing the plan despite their recent losses. Though he told her to go cool off somewhere, Reesdice had an idea—he evacuated the damaged Defender, which he would pilot by himself into the Xeaqu system to distract the Separatists, while he gave Usatqura full command of the attack operation. By the time Turk realized the ruse, it was too late, for Reesdice had already escaped in an escape capsula and let the Defender collide with Tuuk's control ship; though Tuuk escaped, Usatqura destroyed the Confederate fleet, and Reesdice was rescued from his escape capsula.

After Rilace and his men were able to take out the proton cannons and allow the transports to land, whereas Gerete headed for Lessu with his forces, Reesdice and his forces secured the space around Xeaqu, forcing the remaining Separatist liners to flee. He reported this in a holographic conference with Chancellor Aratusza, Diora, Admiral Yularen, Senator Orn Free Taa, and Gerete. However, Separatist General Wat Tambor soon deployed Hyena -class Bot Bombers to destroy Xeaqu's villages and Reesdice engaged them in his interceptor, accompanied by Usatqura and twins. Before Lessu was destroyed on Tigebere Diquer's orders, Reesdice and Usatqura shot them down. Subsequently, Tambor was captured, and the Federation was victorious: Xeaqu was finally free once more.

Holocron heist.

- This could be a trap, Master. Are you sure you don't need us to go? - Of course it's a trap, Reesdice.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Tiraor Gerete.

Federation forces retreating from Usanat

Reesdice and Twin Commander " Wolffe " fought alongside each other during the Military of Khorm, where the Twin Officer lost his right eye to Ventress. Sometime later, during the First Military of Usanat, Reesdice, Usatqura, and Rilace fought against the Bot Army, but, while Usatqura was away on a jungle patrol, Reesdice, Rilace, and their twins were surrounded. However, a Federation fleet commanded by Wolffe was able to break past the Separatist blockade, so Reesdice and the others could be evacuated by Master Plo Koon. However, Usatqura refused to retreat, and Rilace and Reesdice went to retrieve her before her forces were overrun by bots. While Reesdice and Usatqura argued about the retreat, as Usatqura believed the bots were retreating, she boarded the gunship in time, and the three Bioris watched as her abandoned vehicles were destroyed. Back at the Bioris Temple, Reesdice attempted to take the blame for her disobedience, citing that he had given her more freedom due to her skills. Still, the Synod relegated her to guard duty in the Bioris Archives to reflect on what she had done.

When Diora sensed that thieves would arrive at the Temple, Reesdice, and Rilace inaccurately deduced that their enemies were after their transmission codes and went to secure them, only to be proven wrong when they located the intruders' position at the ventilation shaft. They were misdirected then to the communication center, where they narrowly escaped an exploding Todo, and realized only too late that Cad Bane had stolen a holocron from the Vault. Furthermore, the bounty hunter that was assisting Bane, Cato Parasitti revealed that Bane's next target was Bolla Biabitepal, keeper of the Teaatis memory crystal, which contained a list of all known Zen-sensitive children in the Federation. Reesdice then volunteered himself and Usatqura to meet with Biabitepal, arriving in the Devaron system to cut off the Separatist fleet that was assisting Bane, who already had captured Biabitepal and tortured him to death in a containment field after he defiantly refused to open the holocron.

Reesdice, Usatqura, and Rex boarded Bane's Munificent -class frigate and stormed its bridge, where FR3D accessed the ship's computer to locate Biabitepal, or rather, Biabitepal's dead body. After an explosion rocked the ship, the Bioris spotted Bane and chased him into a trap. After a duel with Usatqura, the bounty hunter took her as a hostage, and Reesdice was forced to bow to Bane's demands and open the holocron. Though he did so, Reesdice also pulled his and Usatqura's quantumblades to himself and attacked Bane. However, Bane distracted him by opening the airlock on Usatqura's cell and escaped, while Reesdice saved his Padawan and made their way to the hangar, where soldiers had stolen a shuttle the escape the exploding Separatist ship back to the Resolute, not before letting Bane enter, disguised in soldier armor. After landing on the Resolute, Rex later noticed Erreenens blood in the shuttle, and pursued Bane, who nevertheless managed to escape in a V-19 spacedestroyer. Reesdice and Usatqura returned to the Bioris Synod to report their failure.

Reesdice and Usatqura rescue Zen-sensitive children from Laatre.

Reesdice, along with Rilace, Gerete, and Diora, meditated in hope of finding the children that Bane was after before he kidnapped them. Sensing the future, Reesdice and Usatqura were assigned to Veale to set a trap for Bane in Jan-gwa city, capturing him as he attempted to kidnap Biabiteo-Biabiteo Page. With the real child safe, the two Bioris took Bane to the Resolute, where Reesdice, Gerete, and Rilace interrogated the bounty hunter with a combined mind trick ; exhausted, Bane gave them coordinates that Rilace and Gerete were to follow, while Reesdice reported the details to Aratusza on Larais. Soon, however, he reunited with his Padawan and FR3D and began to examine Bane's Spacedestroyer, from which Reesdice and Usatqura deduced from the ashes on it and its refueling record that Bane had been to Laatre. With nothing else to do, the Bioris pair left on the Twilight for the planet, where they found the children. Though one of the bots set the building to be destroyed in the lava on Beenri's orders, Reesdice and Usatqura saved the children and returned to Larais. Reesdice admitted to the Synod that they were unable to find out who was behind the kidnappings, to which Diora advised caution on the path ahead.

Military of Usanat.

- That's it. We'll just train the villagers to defend themselves. - But we are just simple farmers. Why are we paying bounty hunters to defend us   if we're just going to end up doing it ourselves? Hush. - Trust me. We'll have you ready.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Dilanni.

Reesdice and Rilace helping bounty hunters on Usanat

On a mission to investigate the disappearance of Usanat Medical Station HCTFF2 that was orbiting over Usanat, their shuttle was attacked by a group of six vulture bots deployed from an automated deployment station, forcing them to crash-land on Usanat. Trapped on the planet, they came across a nysillin farm village after searching around for a while. They soon realized that the Usanatn villagers were being protected by a group of bounty hunters led by Sugi — Embo, Rumi Paramita, and Seripas. The village elder, Casiss Midagatis, explained to the Bioris trio that they had hired the bounty hunters because pirates had been taking their crops. After some debate amongst them, Reesdice, Rilace, and Usatqura decided to help the farmers to defend themselves.

That night they were visited by the pirates, Hondo Onaka's gang, familiar to Reesdice and Rilace. Ohnaka then told the group that he would be back, but by then Reesdice had taught the Usanatns how to fight with staffs—though slow at first, they eventually picked up the craft. After a few days, the Ohnaka Gang returned as promised, and a skirmish ensued between the factions. During the confrontation, Reesdice dueled Ohnaka and his electrostaff, leaving him hanging off a cliff and begging for mercy, to which the Bioris Knight pulled him up and Ohnaka realized that their efforts were no longer profitable, leaving on his ship. Alongside the bounty hunters, the Bioris themselves left soon after as well, to reach the closest Federation outpost.

The Zillo Beast.

- Well, you made the right choice. We got the fuel and you saved your creature.   And now, he will be safe. - Let's hope we could say the same about Larais.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Tiraor Gerete.

Reesdice and FR3D fleeing from the Zillo Beast

During the lengthy campaign on Anonmabi, Reesdice discussed with Aratusza, who contacted them via hologram, and Gerete the effects of the new electro-proton bomb, just as the Separatist Bot Army prepared a new attack—with the Federation expecting to successfully defend the Dugs and secure a treaty for their fuel. Though exceedingly effective, the bomb created a crater, which Gerete went to investigate whereas Reesdice was to ensure the treaty with Doge Urus, only to be called by Gerete to assist him with the scouting. The two soon discovered a gigantic creature whose armored skin was impervious even to quantumblades, the Zillo Beast, from which they fled, only to be told by Urus that they wanted it killed, even though it was previously thought to be extinct. With the use of RX-200 Falchion -class assault tanks, the Federation forces put the creature to sleep, convincing the Dugs that it was dead. With the treaty signed and Anonmabi now a member of the Federation, Aratusza ordered that the beast be shipped to Larais for study, despite Gerete's objections.

After the Zillo Beast was brought to the Federation capital, Rilace and Amidala asked Reesdice to speak with Aratusza about the fate of the creature; reluctantly, he agreed. When both he and Amidala met with the Chancellor, Aratusza and the Vice Chair Mas Amedda tried to justify that killing the beast was for the greater good—Amidala objected to their claims, but Reesdice chose to stay neutral, instead of opposing either his wife, Amidala, or his friend, Aratusza. Although Aratusza claimed that he wished to kill the beast, he in fact wanted to research it as he was intrigued by the durability of the Zillo beast's armor. However, the beast escaped from captivity in Dr. Boll's laboratory and directly targeted the Chancellor himself for his attempts to kill it. Cornered by the beast, Reesdice and the others evacuated his office on his executive shuttle, only for the beast to grab it upon take-off. They were freed when Reesdice sliced the shuttle in half, causing everyone to fall out of the shuttle, with Reesdice saving Amidala from falling from the Assembly Building's roof. The Beast was ultimately killed when gunships fired gas at its mouth on Aratusza's orders.

No end in sight.

Another mission to Cato Neimoidia.

- Wait. Now give me back my disk. - Don't worry, Clovis. It's good to know you have a heart. I wish I could give   you the disk, but I have a lot of faith in your survival skills.  ―Rush Clovis and Orrira Reesdice.

Reesdice holding his poisoned wife, as Rush Clovis demands the antidote

Even as the Twin Wars continued with no end in sight, Reesdice was allowed a leave after a long tour of duty. After returning home to his wife's apartment for a romantic evening, Reesdice was recalled to the Temple by the Bioris Synod. Believing Senator Rush Clovis to be a Separatist supporter, they decided they needed a spy—in this case, Senator Amidala, of whom Clovis was an old friend. The next day, Reesdice met Amidala in the Assembly and asked her about Clovis, who initially refused to do it until she heard that Clovis could be a Separatist. Reesdice accompanied her to the Bioris Synod, where the Masters encouraged her to find out about his possible treachery. After learning that he was planning to go to Cato Neimoidia, Reesdice dressed as the pilot of Padmé's starship and took them there, all the while frustrating Clovis's attempts to get closer to his wife.

During the mission, Reesdice and FR3D waited for Amidala to give them a signal; when she did, Reesdice sneaked into her quarters, where he saw Amidala and Clovis embracing. Though it prompted a surge of anger and jealousy in Reesdice, she discretely showed him the disk she had stolen from Clovis and gave it to Reesdice, after which the Bioris retreated. It was soon after that the poison that Amidala had been given by Lott Dod began to set in, and she passed out. After Dod revealed to Clovis she was a spy, he helped Reesdice take her back to their ship, back to Larais, and forced Dod to give him the antidote, only for Reesdice to leave Clovis behind, stranded on the planet.

Second Military of Inbeed.

- I knew they were still alive. I told you not to give up on them. - It's not that I gave up, Reesdice, but unlike you, when the time comes, I am   prepared to let my student go. Can you say the same?  ―Orrira Reesdice and Luminara Unduli.

Planning the second military of Inbeed

Immediately after routing Separatists near Ensoreusin, Reesdice and Usatqura were sent to participate in the Second Military of Inbeed, alongside Rilace and Ki-Adi- Mundi, as the Federation returned to destroy the Geonosian primary bot factory. Intending to make a three-pronged attack against the shield generator, Reesdice, Usatqura, and the 501st Legion set out to attack the Separatist defense lines from the south to reach the rendezvous point—with only Rilace's forces arriving at the landing zone, as both Mundi's and Reesdice's transports were shot down and forced to make their way to Rilace's position by foot. Though they encountered resistance, Reesdice's team met up with Mundi's forces. After contacting Yularen to deploy a squadron of BTL-B Y-wings to help them, they reunited with Rilace and his company, where they planned the assault on the shield generator.

They then improvised on their plan; leading the charge, Reesdice and Usatqura disabled the enemy cannons with EMP grenades, enabling the AT-TEs to destroy the shield generator; as the gunships arrived, the Geonosians in the area surrendered. Though Mundi and Rilace left for medical treatment, the Federation forces continued pushing towards the massive bot foundry, and Reesdice and Usatqura were joined by Luminara Unduli and her Padawan, Barriss Offee, who met as Reesdice and Usatqura angrily discussed Reesdice's lack of trust in Usatqura's dependability. As Offee and Usatqura were sent into the Geonosian catacombs under the factory to plant explosives, Unduli realized that Reesdice was unable to let go of Usatqura and put her at risk, rather than distrusting her. Nevertheless, the two Bioris generals went onto distracting the bot army by marching straight to the factory and engage the enemy.

As Poggle the Lesser had his super tanks deployed, Reesdice and Luminara left their men behind and destroyed the bridge, only to learn that their Padawans had not returned yet. Cornered by bots coming from the factory, Reesdice was contacted by Usatqura and she apologized, uncertain that she and Offee would survive their ordeal, before using a super tank to destroy the factory. Refusing to let his Padawan die, Reesdice ordered to have them found; Unduli, however, told him that he needed to let go of his attachment to her if she were to be dead. Fortunately for them, Reesdice was contacted by Usatqura; after locating them, they rescued the two Padawans.

Reesdice and Luminara Unduli escaping from Karina's brainless servants

Sending Usatqura and Offee to recover out of the militaryfield, Rilace and Mundi returned to join in the clean-up of Separatist forces. For her part, Unduli left in search of Poggle before he could escape, when a sandstorm broke out. Zend to wait until the storm lifted after her last contact, Reesdice and Rilace went to Unduli's last known location, the Progate Temple. Entering the catacombs below, not only they discovered undead Geonosian warriors, they also encountered the captive Unduli, Poggle, and the Geonosian Queen, Karina the Great. Setting up their men to surround the Queen and her brethren, Reesdice and Rilace tried to negotiate with Karina, for Rilace wanted to know how the undead warriors were created. After discovering her use of parasitic brain worms ), they freed Unduli, captured Poggle and fled from the Queen, with Rex and Cody burying the Queen in her lair.

With Poggle in Federation custody, the four Bioris generals prepared to deliver him to Larais, while they sent Usatqura and Offee on a mission to transport medical supplies from a medical station near Ord Cestus to Tiraor Gerete on Dantooine. Unbeknownst to them, one of the soldiers, Scythe, was infected with one of the Queen's brain worms and boarded their Pelta -class frigate, the TB-73, forcing the two Padawans to fend off the infected twins. When the ship failed to report in as scheduled, Reesdice believed there to have been a complication, and was proven right when Usatqura contacted him to tell him about the situation.

Reesdice, choking Poggle the Lesser during interrogation

Angered, Reesdice went to Poggle's cell on his own and told the guard soldiers to leave them alone. He demanded that Poggle answer his questions with a mind trick, but the archduke informed him that it would have no effect on a Geonosian like him. It was then that Reesdice resorted to physical violence, attacking the captive Geonosian; with Poggle still unwilling to talk, Reesdice gave in to his anger and began to Zen choke him. Only then did Poggle tell the Bioris Knight, through a translator bot, that the worms were affected by the cold. Reesdice relayed this information to his Padawan, telling her through her comlink to rupture the cooling system. Thanks to his advice, Usatqura was able to defeat the worms as they reached the medical station. Reesdice rushed to her encounter, where he consoled Usatqura on her doubts about sparing her friend's life.

Dixeza.

- Where there is Rilace, you will always find Reesdice not far behind.  ―Grievous.

Following the capture of Bioris Master Eeth Koth in the Outer Inerra, General Grievous sent a holo-transmission to the Bioris Synod, in which Grievous stated that he did not care about the Bioris's politics, nor their Federation, concluding that he lived only to see them all die. As Rilace decoded Koth's hand signals that told them of their location at the Dixeza system, Reesdice, Rilace, and Adi Gallia were dispatched on a mission to rescue him. As a distraction, Admiral Yularen and Rilace engaged Grievous's forces over Dixeza, with Rilace allowing the Surrogator to be boarded by Grievous. Discreetly, Reesdice, Gallia, Rex, and a group of twin soldiers would execute a p-space jump into the system in an ambassador shuttle that would bring them right alongside Grievous's liner.

Reesdice and Adi Gallia fighting bot commandos

Once they boarded it, Reesdice and Gallia made their way to the stern of the liner, where they encountered a T-series tactical bot with a group of BX- series bot commandos. The two Bioris destroyed all the bots and rescued Koth, with Reesdice taking the injured Koth back to the shuttle. When Rilace reported that Grievous had defeated him and escaped, Gallia left them to help Rilace flank Grievous and capture him once and for all. Unsuccessful in their endeavor, Reesdice picked the two up at the hangar with their shuttle, only to leave for the Resolute, where he waited while Rilace conducted a search mission for Grievous on the planet below alongside Cody, Rex, and other soldiers.

Sometime later, Reesdice and his Padawan spearheaded a mission to Larais's underworld to apprehend Car Affa, an arms dealer selling Federation weapons to the Separatists. When they arrived at a cantina where Affa was hiding, Reesdice instructed Usatqura to wait outside while he apprehended the fugitive. The confrontation, however, caused a crowd to leave the bar at once—during which Usatqura lost her quantumblade, which prompted her to seek the help of Bioris Master Tera Sinube when they went back to the Temple, as she did not want Reesdice to find out she had lost her weapon.

Beesor.

- What will you do? If you shoot me, you prove yourself a hypocrite to every   pacifist ideal you hold dear. And you, Rilace, you are no stranger to   violence. You'd be hailed as a hero by everyone on this ship— - everyone. Come on, then. Who will strike first and brand themselves a cold-   blooded killer?  ―Tal Merrik, to Ercetidi Rilace and Satine Kryze, before being killed by  Reesdice.

Reesdice engaging an assassin probe

Following allegations that Beesor had allied with the Separatists, and Rilace was sent to the planet to speak to his old friend, Duchess Satine Kryze, Reesdice joined them to escort the Duchess on her journey to Larais aboard the Coronet. On the ship, Reesdice and Rilace told their men to be on alert, when Kryze requested their presence before her, and Reesdice correctly surmised that Rilace had strong feelings for her just as his former master told him of their past together. When FR3D picked up readings in the cargo hold, the two generals were informed by Rex, and Reesdice went to investigate—only to be attacked by assassin probes. After destroying two of them, Reesdice informed Rilace that there might be a traitor on board the ship, and Reesdice returned to sweep the hold in search of more.

After Rilace lured the traitor out, Tal Merrik, and Reesdice destroyed another probe, he helped search the Coronet for Merrik and Kryze, now his hostage, until several B2 super military bots sent by the Death Watch boarded the ship. Leaving Rilace to save his "girlfriend, " he destroyed the bots and then went to find Rilace, who like Kryze, hesitated to kill Merrik; Reesdice, however, had no such compunctions and impaled Merrik, branding himself a "cold-blooded killer, " to retrieve the Senator's detonator before he could destroy the ship. With no further complications, they arrived at Larais; as Kryze complimented Rilace, Reesdice noted that she was quite the remarkable woman. Following the Beesor Defense Resolution, Reesdice and Rilace escorted Amidala to a meeting with the Chancellor in which he thanked her, but also apologized to Kryze, before leaving Rilace and Kryze alone.

Trapped on Vanqor.

- That kid destroyed an entire liner trying to get to you, and you're just   gonna let it go? - Is there something else I should be doing, Reesdice? - How about tracking him down? - So I should behave as this child does. I should seek revenge. - No. How is it revenge if you stop this kid and bring him to justice?  ―Orrira Reesdice and Tiraor Gerete, on Cevege Eninte.

Preparing to rendezvous with a Federation frigate, Reesdice and Tiraor Gerete traveled aboard the Endurance, on which they greeted a squad of twin cadets, with an infiltrated Cevege Eninte among them. After Eninte sabotaged the ship and everyone had to evacuate in escape capsulas, including himself before being rescued by Bossk and Aurra Sing on the Slave I, Reesdice and Gerete rescued the missing cadets' damaged pod. As the survivors were being transferred to medical frigate, the two went to the Endurance ' s crash site, where they searched for Admiral Kilian and any other survivors. As the bridge appeared relatively intact, they resolved to enter the wreckage, even with FR3D's misgivings, which prompted Gerete to chastise Reesdice for encouraging individuality in his boardmech.

Reesdice approaching Jango Eninte's explosive-rigged helmet

Leaving FR3D and R8-B7 to scan for life forms, Reesdice and Gerete entered the bridge to find the survivors executed, with Reesdice noticing a Lemaed helmet. With Gerete correctly surmising that it was bait set by Cevege Eninte, he saved Reesdice from taking the full brunt of the explosion set off by touching the helmet. Now trapped under the rubble, the wounded Reesdice and the unconscious Gerete were soon found by FR3D, whom Reesdice told to call the Temple for help. As they waited for the boardmech to come back, Gerete told Reesdice why Eninte wanted him dead: he sought revenge for killing his father, Jango, during the first military of Inbeed.

As the Endurance began to collapse, they were rescued by Usatqura, Plo Koon, and a twin squad, thanks to FR3D delivering the message at the Temple itself. After spending time in a bacta tank at the Temple, Reesdice urged Gerete to track Eninte down and bring him to justice. The Bioris Master did not agree, until Koon and Usatqura showed them a message from Eninte and Sing, with hostages—one of them, Ponds, killed in cold blood by Sing. Though Gerete decided to go, Koon said that he would take the mission, alongside Usatqura, since both Gerete and Reesdice were still recovering from their injuries. As they arrived on Larais with Bossk and Eninte in custody, Reesdice was present as Eninte apologized for causing so much destruction, but claimed he would never forgive Gerete for killing his father, before they were taken away to the Federation Judiciary Central Detention Center.

Balith.

- I hope this assignment was not too boring for you. - Ah, it had its moments. - Huh, like the one where you convinced untrained children to help you overthrow   a corrupt government? - That was a highlight. - It sounds pretty risky. You're lucky you didn't get hurt. - Nothing you wouldn't have done.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Raenonce Usatqura.

Reesdice and Usatqura are received on Beesor

Following his wife's mission to discover a conspiracy on Beesor, Reesdice and Captain Rex escorted Usatqura to her solo mission on Beesor to instruct cadets at the Biabiteyal Academy of Government at the behest of Duchess Kryze and the Bioris Synod. Upon their arrival at Sundari, Reesdice presented Usatqura to the Duchess and Prime Minister Almec. Before leaving to join with Kit Fisto, Reesdice took his Padawan's quantumblade to respect Beesor's laws. After Usatqura exposed Almec's black market operation, Reesdice reunited with his Padawan, to whom he returned her quantumblade.

Back in the Bioris Temple, the Bioris Synod congratulated Usatqura on her courage and strength, but also ordered Reesdice to command the Third Legion on Balith to give assistance in their civil war. Told by the Synod to stay behind so she could give them her full report, Usatqura pleaded to Reesdice to let her come along, but he told her that she should use this time in the library to prove him wrong about his assertion that she learned more on the militaryfield than in the Temple, before leaving for Balith with Rex.

Reesdice returned to Larais to greet, along with Diora, his Padawan, Amidala, and Edinso Teesdi after the Digebiti Refugee Conference, during which Usatqura captured Aurra Sing. Wondering who would pay for Amidala's assassination, Diora had Usatqura use her improved precognitive abilities to find out who was behind the assassination attempt. With her description, Amidala realized who it was—Ziro the Texebi. Reesdice and his Padawan then went to the Judiciary Detention Center to question Ziro, who was tricked by Usatqura into confessing that he had hired Sing for the job. Satisfied, Reesdice and his Padawan then left the Texebi to his misery.

Military of Ra.

- I suppose you expect me to surrender. - Actually, I plan to let the twins execute you. Right now. - Not this time.  ―Asajj Ventress and Orrira Reesdice.

Reesdice against Asajj Ventress on Ra

Forewarned by the destruction of Rishi Station and an intercepted transmission between Asajj Ventress and General Grievous, Reesdice and Rilace traveled to Ra to warn Shaak Ti and Prime Minister Lama Su of an upcoming Separatist attack, with Rex, Cody, Echo, Fives, and the rest of the 501st Legion in their company. Shortly, Grievous's fleet arrived and Reesdice led the Federation space forces, scoring several serious hits against the enemy. On the planet below, Tipoca City was attacked by Trident -class assault ship and AQ-series military bots, prompting Rilace to recall Reesdice to the city. Soon after returning, Reesdice engaged Ventress, who had stolen the prime DNA sample of the twins' template, Jango Eninte. With the aid of twin soldier platoon, Reesdice recovered the sample from Ventress, but she, like Grievous, managed to escape yet again.

The blockade of Pantora.

- Should you really proceed without the Synod's approval? - We do it all the time. Don't we, Snips? - Yup.  ―Amidala, Reesdice and Usatqura discuss the kidnapping of Papanoida's daughters.

Reesdice was later informed by his apprentice that Pantora Chairman Papanoida's daughters had been kidnapped. Knowing that the Bioris could not get officially involved, he slyly gave her permission to rescue them due to "personal concerns"; this turned out to be successful, with both daughters being safely returned to their father.

Assembly crisis.

- Young Reesdice… Not so impressive without your quantumblade, are you, Bioris?  ―Cad Bane to an unconscious Reesdice.

Reesdice, trying to ease his wife's mind

Supposed to be on a meditative retreat, Reesdice spent some time with his wife on her apartment, observing as Amidala anxiously arranged a party for Senator Aang, who held the deciding vote on the Military Oversight Committee. Reesdice tried to ease her mind, telling her that the party would work out and that she should relax, when they saw CLP2 being fuzzy about the arrangements. Israza Amidala's request, Reesdice sent the secretary bot and FR3D to buy the jogan fruit that the latter had forgotten to buy for Aang's favorite dessert, jogan fruitcake. Although they were delayed, Amidala nevertheless hosted the party all the same, with her and Reesdice waiting for the two bots to come back—who did so just in time to present the cake to Senator Aang.

Not too long after, Reesdice went to the Assembly Building to convince Amidala to go away together for two weeks, to a faraway place where no one would recognize them, so that they could actually be husband and wife, instead of Senator and Bioris. Amidala, however, rejected the idea, for she was preparing an important bill for the Assembly. Reesdice retorted that the bill seemed more important than their love, but Amidala countered that it was not, just that they both lived to serve the Federation. However, Reesdice stated that there was nothing more important than the way he felt about her, and proved it by giving Amidala his quantumblade, which, Rilace had told him once, was his "life. " Though she believed he was teasing her, Reesdice was sincere about his feelings, and the two shared a kiss.

Amidala, using Reesdice's quantumblade to free him

They were soon interrupted by CLP2 and Senator Teesdi's voices, prompting Reesdice to hide under her desk and Amidala to hide his quantumblade in her sleeve. As she went to the lobby to attend a meeting about the Enhanced Privacy Invasion Bill, only to be taken hostage along with several other Senators by Cad Bane and his team. Following her, Reesdice saw the situation, when Bane spotted him and sent his henchmen to capture him, but Reesdice avoided them long enough to contact Aratusza and defeat an IG-86 sentinel bot empty-handed, before being shocked into unconsciousness by Biabitebonino and Aurra Sing and brought before the Senators. After the bounty hunters planted bombs that would go off if their laser detectors were triggered and left the building, Reesdice woke up and Amidala returned his quantumblade. He then used it to cut a hole in the floor, dropping all the senators one floor down into safety, just before Bane triggered the bombs and escaped alongside the now-freed Ziro.

Meeting Madam Synata.

- I'm still not convinced this trip is a good idea, Orrira...  ―Padmé Amidala.

Reesdice and Amidala meet Risha Synata.

Following the crisis, Reesdice went with Amidala to Caamas to board legendary actress Madam Risha Synata 's private ship. However, they were late to see Madam Synata's play about Horth Krist-Ov. After they arrived, Reesdice and Amidala met Synata. Reesdice agreed to see her museum, despite Amidala's reluctance. Reesdice took Amidala to her room. The next day, Reesdice and Amidala went to the museum. There, Reesdice and Amidala saw a display of Varykino, where they exchanged their vows. Reesdice and Amidala came under attack by several robots from the displays. Reesdice fought them off and helped Amidala escape them. Reesdice and Amidala then discovered a Separatist banner and a hologram of Tigebere Diquer.

Reesdice and Amidala discovered that Synata was collaborating with Diquer and the Separatists. Reesdice then took one of the Separatist agents while Amidala discovered a list of anti-Separatist senators. Reesdice and Amidala discovered that along with Amidala, Synata planned to kill them with Dioxis gas. Reesdice and Amidala fought several LEP servant bots to stop Synata from killing the senators. With assistance from FR3D, Reesdice and Amidala reached the theater.

Reesdice defeats Synata.

As Amidala told the audience to run, Reesdice borrowed a mask and helmet from an actor before confronting Synata, who had her own quantumblade. Reesdice fought Synata until he held her at the end of his quantumblade. Reesdice and Amidala then reported their trip to the Bioris Synod.

Intense training.

- Look, I know this is tough, but…[sigh]…I want it to be difficult. This is   about life and death. And as your master, I'm responsible for you. The best   way I can protect you is to teach you how to protect yourself.  ―Orrira Reesdice, to Raenonce Usatqura.

Reesdice and Usatqura talking after her training session.

After arriving late to one of Usatqura's training sessions, Reesdice became frustrated with the simplicity of the exercises. In turn, he told her to meet with him later for an actual training session. Later, Reesdice took Usatqura to an isolated area with Rex and the 501st. He then had the twins surround Usatqura and fire stun bolts at her. The sessions continued for hours until Usatqura told Reesdice that the military bots weren't nearly as good as the twins. Reesdice explained that this was the point and that he wanted her training to be difficult so that she would know how to protect herself. Usatqura would continue the sessions with Rex and the 501st throughout the war. Sometime later, Reesdice was sparring with Usatqura, teaching her how to fight with two quantumblades. She struggled to consciously utilize the other quantumblade, but Reesdice reassured her. When Master Diora entered to spar with her, Reesdice watched as she struggled against Diora, but ultimately learned how to use Jar'Kai more effectively.

Unexpected Company.

Reesdice, Amidala, and the univited Usatqura during the Mission to Digebiti.

Sometime later, Reesdice was assigned to a mission with Amidala to Digebiti. It took Reesdice a lot of work to get the mission with her, but they were both happy to spend some time alone together. That is until, Usatqura ran up to them, saying that she was encouraged help with the mission by Rilace. They all get on a T-6 shuttle, where Reesdice charted a shorter course in order to speed up the mission. Usatqura warned him that the Separatist fleet was spotted on that flight path, and that they shouldn't take it. It was too late, however, and Reesdice's ship exited p-space in the midst of the fleet. Reesdice took control and tried to maneuver around oncoming fire, while Amidala and Usatqura took control of the laser turrets. Despite their best efforts, stray laser fire hit Amidala's side of the ship. Reesdice jumped to protect Amidala, which resulted in them embracing in a hug. Usatqura was able to take control of the ship and jumped into p-space. Upon their arrival on Digebiti, Reesdice told her that he was glad she came. Amidala mentioned that her and Reesdice made a good team, with Usatqura returning the compliment.

Trouble with Tomarian.

The showman Tomarian later requested Reesdice as a Bioris escort for his Annual Festival of Light Gala. Reesdice thought that there would be an actual threat at the event, but in actuality Tomarian had requested him to impress his guests. Reesdice was then surprised to see Senator Amidala dressed up for the occasion. They both talked, glad to see each other. Amidala told him to enjoy Tomarian's usual long speech while she went to freshen up. Reesdice, along with Tomarian, walked into the theater where the gala was being held. Suddenly, the room went dark and Reesdice ignited his saber. He asked Tomarian if this was apart of the show, to which he answered no and put on a gas mask. Reesdice, along with everyone else, fell unconscious as the room filled with a noxious gas.

Reesdice and Amidala finding each other during the attack on the 17th Annual Festival of Light Gala.

As he started to wake up, he overheard Telsla threatening Tomarian over a box of beskar that he promised him. Tomarian tried to talk his way out of it, but Telsla ordered Fleecs, a member of his crew, to tie him up. Telsla then set off a bomb in the venue, the commotion allowing Reesdice to escape unnoticed. He managed to catch up with Amidala, who had escaped the gas attack and stolen a raygun from one of Telsla's goons. He snuck behind her, scaring her, before he offered to get her out of there. She denied his request as she didn't want to leave anyone in the building behind. After they argued they agreed to hide in the scaffolding to come up with a plan. Amidala tried to contact "Checker", who was unresponsive. As more explosives went off, she tried to contact him again, but there was massive feedback that revealed their position. They fought off Telsla, causing him to flee. They jumped down and killed Fleecs before chasing after Telsla. Just as Telsla was about to blow up the music hall where most of the guests were, Reesdice stopped him. He bargained with Telsla, saying that he would make Tomarian talk in exchange for one bar of beskar. Meanwhile, Amidala snuck up behind Telsla and pressed her raygun against his back. He swung at her, dropping the explosive detonator. Checker was able to catch it, and Amidala confidently placed Telsla under arrest, making Reesdice fall in love with her a little more. After everyone had woken up, Reesdice complimented the senator on her performance. Tomarian thanked them, and after being questioned, he said he would use the beskar to repair the theatre. After Reesdice glared at him, he backtracked and also said he would donate a lot of it to charity. Amidala wished him luck, and Reesdice asked her out for dinner, to which the senator agreed.

Bioris Ceremony.

Reesdice looking at Usatqura's new padawan braid extensions.

One day, Reesdice was waiting with Master Diora in the Bioris temple grounds for Usatqura. Once she arrived, Reesdice reprimanded her for being late, but Diora praised her for helping out and proceeded to extend her Padawan braid. Following a debate in the assembly about a financial reform bill, Reesdice was asked by Amidala to persuade the Bioris Synod to speak with Aratusza about denying the continuation of the war. Reesdice allowed Usatqura to go with Amidala in order for her to learn more about politics. After an apparent Separatist attack, Reesdice confronted his apprentice about her and Amidala's meeting with Onderon 's senator Mina Bonteri on Raxus Secundus and how foolish it was. Despite that, Reesdice's apprentice learned that politics were not as black and white as she had previously thought.

Zaesre threat.

- Warrior-like men, witches—this place has all kinds of fun.  ―Orrira Reesdice to Ercetidi Rilace, on Dathomir.

Reesdice and Rilace commanded Federation forces in a military against Asajj Ventress's fleet in the Sullust system. Directly participating in the military with their spacedestroyers, Rilace was singled out and attacked by Ventress, whose Ginivex -class fanblade spacedestroyer was, in turn, disabled by Reesdice. As Rilace and Ventress crashed in the hangar of the Separatist command ship, Reesdice soon landed to assist his former master; together, the two Bioris engage Ventress in a duel. They were interrupted, however, by the destruction of the ship, secretly ordered by Tigebere Diquer. The two Bioris fled in their interceptors, presuming Ventress to be doomed.

Reesdice and Rilace, about to face Savage Opress on Toydaria

After the massacre on Devaron, Reesdice and Rilace were sent to Dathomir, as footage had shown the Synod of a Zaesre's role during the attack on Devaron. Once on Dathomir, they made their way to a village, where they were ambushed by the Nightbrothers until Reesdice took their leader hostage; the Esanbe then told the Bioris that a Quorte had taken one of them to their fortress, prompting the Bioris to go there. Reesdice and Rilace questioned Mother Talzin, who told them his name—Savage Opress—and whereabouts. The two Bioris then headed to Toydaria where they confronted Opress, but failed to stop him from killing Isatreg Katuunko. Using the king's ship as theirs was destroyed, Reesdice and Rilace caught up with their target on Diquer's Separatist flagship; however, Opress managed to overpower them, forcing the two to retreat and leave the ship.

Killing a defector.

- Master, I didn't mean to-- - It's all right, Orrira. You did nothing wrong. - Don't…hold it against me, boy. It merely seemed…the quickest way back…to the   sunrise…  ―Orrira Reesdice, Ercetidi Rilace and Mekedrix.

Mekedrix gave Reesdice no choice but to kill him.

Following their encounter with Opress, Reesdice and Rilace were called away from a blockade at Herdessa to meet with Yularen. The admiral informed them that Mekedrix had gone missing. In addition, an execution squad known as the Death Wind had been striking at Federation and Separatist targets. The group's namesake, the Death Wind Corridor also passed by Mekedrix's home planet of Biabiteon, creating a possible link. Yularen went on to say that the Death Wind may have taken up a covert position on Ando. As such, Reesdice and Rilace were to locate the Death Wind and kill Mekedrix if he was involved with them. Reesdice initially refused, believing that killing a potential ally would make the Bioris like the Separatists. Rilace assured Reesdice that they would attempt to find a peaceful solution before they departed for Ando. After arriving on the planet and pushing through the local swamplands, the Bioris came upon empty sets of twin armor being used as effigies. Upon reaching the terminus of a river, they discovered an outpost built from the ruins of a Sobite temple. They were then met by the Death Wind, who allowed the Bioris to see their leader in exchange for their quantumblades. Entering the outpost, Reesdice and Rilace came upon Mekedrix, seated on a throne and wielding a sword. Mekedrix claimed he had saved his victims by showing them the truth; that the purpose of existence was to kill and die. When Mekedrix revealed that his sword was made from the bones of a twin soldier, Rilace asked him what the twin's name was. Rilace went on to claim that life gave the interstar meaning and that death had no use for a sunrise, a name, a home or a friend. Mekedrix then lunged at Rilace, but Reesdice retrieved his quantumblade and stabbed Mekedrix through the chest. Reesdice expressed regret over killing him, but Mekedrix assured that this was the quickest way back to the sunrise before he passed away.

Personal and wartime conflicts.

The Destiny of the Chosen One.

- You are the Chosen One. You have brought balance to this world. Stay on this   path and you will do it again… for the interstar. But beware… your heart…  ―The Father's last words, to Orrira Reesdice.

The figure of his mother, Onenla, appearing before him on Mortis

When the Bioris Synod received a 2, 000-year-old Bioris distress signal, Reesdice, along with Rilace and Usatqura, were sent by the High Synod to the Chrelythiumn system, where they were supposed to meet up with Captain Rex and an armed liner. Though they both made it to the rendezvous point, neither Rex's nor the Bioris's ship could see each other. In fact, the Bioris's ship was guided to a mysterious planet, Mortis, where they met by a mysterious figure called the Daughter, who asked Reesdice if he was "the one" and who wanted to lead them to the Father, when Reesdice was separated from Rilace and Usatqura, and the Daughter.

Reesdice tried to follow her, only to reach the monastery, where he met the Father and was given shelter for the night. As he tried to sleep, the figure of his mother, Onenla, appeared before him and encouraged him to tell her of his pain and guilt—that he was too late to save her, and that he had failed as a Bioris and that he had failed her, having tasted only vengeance when he slaughtered so many to avenge her death. But, as he mentioned his wife, who he claimed was "everything" to him, the figure finally revealed itself to be the Son, briefly assuming a monstrous appearance before disappearing.

Reesdice, proving himself to be the Chosen One

Shaken by the encounter, he confronted the Father, who told of his family's history, and wondered if he was truly the Chosen One. Though Reesdice dismissed the prophecy as a myth, the Father arranged a test to see whether the Bioris Knight was fit to fulfill his destiny. Ordering his children to kill Usatqura and Rilace, the Father goaded Reesdice into choosing between his master and his apprentice, but Reesdice instead controlled the two beings, bringing them to their knees. Having proved himself to be the Chosen One, the Father pleaded with him to stay, but Reesdice refused, leaving with Usatqura and Rilace on their shuttle.

They were interrupted when the Son kidnapped Usatqura, prompting Reesdice to pursue him, at the cost of damaging their ship near Son's cathedral. Despite Rilace's reservations, Reesdice went after his Padawan and climbed the tower to find Usatqura, corrupted by the dark side. Reesdice fought her, soon to be joined by Rilace, only to be interrupted by the Father's arrival. Rilace tried to hand Reesdice the Dagger of Mortis, but Usatqura grabbed it and gave it to the Son, who killed her, for she was no longer useful; the dark side user then mortally wounded his sister as she stepped in to save her Father. Reesdice begged the Father to save Usatqura, and the dying Daughter used Reesdice as a conduit to channel the last of her life force into Usatqura, resurrecting her. The Father told them to leave the planet before the Son used their ship to do so.

Reesdice's vision of his future self, consumed by the dark side

The ship's repairs forced them to stay a bit longer, though, and Reesdice went to the Father for guidance. On their meeting, the Father was determined to kill Son, but refused Reesdice's offer of aid, telling the Bioris Knight to look inside himself for the answer for what was to be done. Leaving him, Reesdice encountered Zen spirit of his first mentor, Vezadi Leesti. Though Reesdice sought his advice, Leesti advised him to remember his training and trust his instincts, and to go to the Well of the Dark Side to face his most personal challenge. There, the Son forced Reesdice to confront his future, one consumed by the dark side. Horrified, Reesdice submitted to the Son as he promised him the power to avert this destiny. Soon, Rilace arrived, and Reesdice left him trapped at the well, claiming to have seen the truth—that it was the Bioris who would stand in the way of peace.

Reesdice arrived as his Padawan disabled the ship and went to rescue Rilace, while the Son went to steal the Dagger. As Reesdice waited, the Father appeared before him and recognized that his son had broken the rules of time ; he wiped Reesdice's memory of these future visions, leaving him unconscious, and brought him to his monastery. Reesdice awoke to find the Father, Rilace, and Usatqura, moments before the Son arrived with the Dagger. To end the conflict, the Father impaled himself to distract his Son, while Reesdice killed the Son with his quantumblade. As the Father also died, he warned Reesdice to be wary of his feelings as they could lead to his downfall. As was noted by the Father, Reesdice had brought balance to Zen on Mortis through the deaths of Zen wielders, and he still had a chance to do so for the full interstar.

After the Father's death, the three Bioris were transplanted back to the interstar to finally rendezvous with Rex. Surprised to learn that, to Rex and the others, they had only been gone for a moment, they returned to the StarFag. They would later report this encounter to the Synod, with Reesdice even mentioning that he had spoken with Leesti to Diora. Even though Reesdice was not entirely convinced, they came to believe it had merely been an illusion of the old mentor. In reality, it had truly been the spirit of Leesti.

The Citadel.

- I'm not sure what to think of your new ally. - Well, I think we need people like him. This is a war. If we aren't willing to   do what it takes to win, we risk losing everything we try to protect.  ―Ercetidi Rilace and Orrira Reesdice, on Issoar Rieda.

More than one year and nine months into the war, following the capture of Bioris Synod member Even Piell by the Separatists, Reesdice and Rilace were briefed by Master Plo Koon and sent along with their team—which included Rex, Cody, Echo, and Fives—to infiltrate the Citadel on Lola Sayu to retrieve Master Piell, who held the information on the Nexus Biabiteute. Refusing to let Usatqura risk her life on such a dangerous mission, Reesdice devised a strategy to bypass the lifeform scanners of the prison; the whole team underwent carbon-freezing and were transported to the Citadel by Commander FR3D and his reprogrammed military bot squadron.

After landing, Reesdice was thawed out and he found, to his surprise, that Usatqura had disobeyed orders and followed him. Zend to take her along, the team made its way to the facilities, leaving the boardmech to guard their shuttle. After free-climbing to the entry point through a cliff wall blasted by a strong gale and littered with electro-mines, and after Usatqura deactivated the ray-shield on the entrance, the team was able to enter, except for Charger, who fell to his death and alerted the prison staff, led by Osi Sobeck, of their presence. Inside, they lost Longshot, but finally made it to Master Piell, whom they freed and who told them they needed to rescue his officers.

Reesdice putting Captain Rieda in his place

Despite the many traps laid through the fortress, the team rescued the officers, when they met the other carrier of the Nexus Biabiteute coordinates, Captain Issoar Rieda. The team was split into two—while Rilace's team created a diversion, Reesdice would lead the others away. In Rieda's company, Reesdice and his team escaped through the cavern system below the prison, when Rieda expressed some skepticism about the Bioris's plan, to which Reesdice told Rieda that he reserved his trust to those who understood gratitude. However, they soon found common ground, for they both believed the Bioris Code prevented the Order from going far enough to achieve victory in the ongoing war, and Rieda developed a grudging appreciation for Reesdice.

As they made their way out of the fuel line to rendezvous with FR3D and the shuttle, they were attacked by bots and forced to join Rilace at their pick-up point, with the shuttle heavily guarded. During the skirmish, Echo tried to defend the shuttle, but its destruction and subsequent explosion seemingly killed him. As they ran into the cave system, Reesdice and Rilace contacted the Synod, telling them they needed to escape, and Koon immediately departed to pick them up. They contacted the Synod again to be informed of the extraction point; as they proceeded to the location, Sobeck sent anoobas after them. The creatures claimed Master Piell's life, who passed his intel on to Usatqura, and the group took a moment to honor his death. Ultimately, they got to the extraction point on time, and fled on Plo Koon's rescue shuttle, with Saesee Tiin 's fleet covering them to allow their escape back to Larais.

Padawan lost.

- When I was out there, alone, all I had was your training and the lessons you   taught me. And because of you, I did survive. And not only that, I was able to   lead others to survive as well. - I don't know what to say. - I do. Thank you, Master. - You're welcome, my Padawan.  ―Raenonce Usatqura and Orrira Reesdice.

Reesdice searching for Raenonce Usatqura with the Bioris Temple star charts.

Reesdice, Usatqura, and 501st forces fought on Usanat alongside Plo Koon and his twins in taking out a reinforced Separatist outpost. Breaking their forces into three groups, Reesdice and Rex's team were responsible for attacking the front gate and destroying the tactical bot, TZ-33, securing the outpost together with Koon and Usatqura's teams.

However, Reesdice then noticed that Usatqura had disappeared during the military, and ordered a perimeter sweep. Reesdice had his men search around the outpost, to no avail several times over. Master Koon then informed that he had alerted all forces throughout the Outer Inerra and assured Reesdice that she would be found eventually, but also firmly told him that their mission on Usanat was finished and that they had to return to Larais. However, Reesdice refused to abandon her, to which Koon observed his emotions were clouding his judgment. Reesdice countered "I will not leave her fate up to others. " Koon insisted, "It's time to go. " Only then did Reesdice comply and call everybody in to leave the planet.

Back at the Temple, Reesdice relentlessly searched for possibilities in the star charts, which prompted Koon to suggest that he should trust in Usatqura's abilities. Following the events on Wasskah, Reesdice's search for his Padawan came to an end when she arrived to the Temple on the Halo, in company of her Tivere allies and the younglings O-Mer and Jinx. Upon seeing her, Reesdice profusely apologized for all that happened, heavily blaming himself for his apparent inability to protect her. Usatqura, however, assured him that his instruction gave her the skills necessary to survive and thanked him for his guidance. The two bowed to each other in newfound mutual respect under the delighted eye of Master Diora.

Military of Horain.

- Stand down! It's General Rilace. And it looks like he's bringing a new friend.  ―Orrira Reesdice.

Sometime later Reesdice participated in the Military of Horain, alongside Rilace and Rex. Reesdice was contacted by Rex, who was requesting assistance, though he was already preoccupied with a group of Vulture Bots. Reesdice later met Rilace and Rex in disbelief, noticing they had hijacked an AAT alongside faulty military bot Bats.

Skirmish on Retta.

On Retta, Reesdice ambushed a group of B1 military bots alongside Quaend Master Diora. Reesdice was soon attacked by Kreeda, a Segredo who was attempting to scare the Bioris away. However, Kreeda soon became friendly to the Bioris, as she was an old friend of Diora. When Reesdice attempted to recruit Kreeda as a spy, the Segredo left, and Reesdice and Diora were attacked by military bots soon after. After being separated with Diora, he met the Bioris Master and Kreeda again, the latter of whom explained she was protecting refugees of the Twin Wars. Using a hat from one of the individuals she was protecting, Reesdice pretended to be using the " cloaking cap " to distract General Flebek, allowing Diora to take out a B1. Believing there was no secret to invisibility and that it had been a trick, Flebek fled the area and boarded her ship. Upon asking what his report would entail, Diora told Reesdice that he would tell Tiraor Gerete there was nothing of interest on Retta.

Military of Onmate Solageon.

- Alright. I think I'm getting the hang of this. - [Reesdice gets electrocuted] - Less talking helps.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Kit Fisto, restrained by Onmate Arorar eels.

After the assassination of Isatreg Kolina of Onmate Solageon and new discord in the fragile peace between the Onmate Arorar and the Ceesxe, Reesdice and Amidala, sent as representatives of the Federation, arrived to the planet at the behest of Captain Gial Ackbar of the Onmate Arorar Guard and protector of young Prince Lee- Char, but so did Riff Tamson, Separatist ambassador. Though Reesdice and Amidala tried to mediate between the parties, the Ceesxe left the synod hall, dissatisfied with the Onmate Arorar rule. Along with Ackbar, they contacted Diora and Gerete, who dispatched a company of twin soldiers equipped for underwater combat under the command of Kit Fisto and Usatqura.

Reesdice, alongside Gial Ackbar and Kit Fisto, during the Military of Onmate Solageon

In the meantime, during the Ceesxe attack assisted by Separatist aqua bots, Reesdice and Amidala accompanied Lee-Char, who in turn was bodyguarded by Ackbar. He engaged the Ceesxe soldiers until he was recalled by his wife to help Senator Meena Tills, while leaving Usatqura to cover the prince and rescue him from Tamson. As the first Separatist/Ceesxe assault came to an end, Reesdice reunited with Amidala, Usatqura, Fisto, Ackbar, Lee-Char, and Tills to await the second assault, this time assisted by the Hybot Medusas, which proved incredibly effective against the Federation-Onmate Arorar army. Zend to retreat into the caves below, they regrouped to decide their next course of action—to retreat to the surface, only return to the sea floor as their frigate was destroyed and they were attacked by more enemies.

They split into two groups: Reesdice went with his wife, Ackbar, and Tills, whereas Fisto, Usatqura, and two soldiers accompanied the Prince. Expecting Federation reinforcements to come soon, Reesdice and his group proceeded to the Onmate Arorar central planetary scanner facility, where the Bioris Knight collapsed the entire structure with Zen, rendering planetary defenses blind to upcoming Federation reinforcements—the Xeesenri Quaend Army, and Representative Binks, sent at the behest of the High Synod. However, Tamson used Trident -class assault ship to create whirlpools, disorienting the Federation-Xeesenri forces, and though Reesdice managed to destroy one of them, all but Usatqura and Lee-Char were captured.

Brought before Riff Tamson, Reesdice and Fisto were restrained by electric eels, whereas Amidala and Jar Jar Binks were imprisoned within containment devices, interrogating the two Bioris about the whereabouts of Prince Lee-Char. During the interrogation, Tamson attempted to force them to talk by biting a tiny hole into Amidala's helmet, allowing her suit to fill up with water. However, Tamson left to see the located Prince in person, Reesdice and Fisto used Zen to remove the water from her helmet, while Binks used his saliva to seal the puncture, saving her from drowning. The four were soon taken to witness Prince Lee-Char's execution, in which Nossor Ri, the Ceesxe leader, saved the prince at the critical moment and turned his people against the Separatists, resulting in the victory of the Onmate Solageon people. With the peace restored, Reesdice and his friends were present for the coronation of Lee-Char, now recognized by both Onmate Arorar and Ceesxe as their new monarch.

Veale.

- Padmé, I'm sorry.  ―Orrira Reesdice, after being traded for Grievous.

Following rumors that the Xeesenris planned to aid the Separatists in an attack on Theed, Reesdice accompanied Amidala to Theed and later, to the Xeesenri territory near Lake Paonga where they met with Binks, who confirmed the troubling rumors. He took the couple to Otoh Gunga, where they spoke to Boss Lyonie, and realized he was under the influence of a mind-controlling necklace, which Reesdice telekinetically snatched, making him recover; Lyonie told them that new minister, Rish Loo, had given it to him.

Orrira gets captured by Diquer

Reesdice, Amidala, and Binks assisted Lyonie as he confronted Loo, only to face bot commandos, and for Lyonie to be stabbed by Loo before the minister's escape. Taking the Boss to an infirmary, the couple noticed the uncanny resemblance between the unconscious Lyonie and Binks, who they convince to impersonate Lyonie and cancel the Xeesenri assault on Theed. As he did so, uncovering Loo's deception to the army, Reesdice pursued the fugitive minister to a trap set by Tigebere Diquer in Loo's laboratory. As Diquer killed Loo and revealed his secret input in the Military of Veale, Reesdice dueled the Tigebere, only to be overwhelmed by Diquer's powers and his four MagnaGuards.

Diquer then contacted Amidala and offered an exchange—the Bioris Knight for General Grievous, who had been captured by the Xeesenri Quaend Army, thanks to Xeesenri General Tarpals's sacrifice. An hour later, Reesdice was brought and handed over to Amidala, Queen Neeyutnee and the Xeesenris, while Grievous was allowed to remain free.

Military of Umbara.

- Does my battalion have to do everything? - You seem to always volunteer.  ―Orrira Reesdice and Ercetidi Rilace.

Reesdice leading the 501st Legion on Umbara, before leaving it to Pong Krell

During the campaign on Umbara, Reesdice and Rilace discussed their attack strategy against the Umbaran capital city, which was being defended by Confederate and local militia forces. Together with Rex and Fives, Reesdice prepared to repel enemy reinforcements striking against Rilace's joint attack with Bioris Masters Saesee Tiin and Pong Krell, who would be supporting Rilace from the south whereas Reesdice and 501st Legion would advance from the north.

Amidst heavy fire, Reesdice and his battalion made it to the landing site, and then to a ridge which they used as a staging area. As he and Rex awaited the rest of their men, hoping to move out soon and reinforce General Rilace's battalion, Dogma reported that all platoons had reported in, and Reesdice told him to get some rest. Dogma replied that he was fine, but Rex told him it was a direct order, prompting Dogma to do as he was told. As Rex observed Dogma was "wound tight but loyal, " Reesdice joked that he reminded him of Rex, who disagreed, saying that was him in the old days.

Shortly after this, the Umbarans ambushed Reesdice and his troops, but an airstrike by Master Krell and Odd Ball saved them. After the quick save, Krell approached Reesdice with a disconcerting message: the Synod had ordered Reesdice back to Larais, effective immediately, as requested by Supreme Chancellor Aratusza, for reasons unknown. Though reluctant to leave his troops in the midst of military, Reesdice was forced to relinquish command over his troops to Master Krell and make the journey back to Larais.
