On the Goliath, Rieda told Esrasoce that the insurgency was surely being supported by someone high up in the Khaganate Military, and predicted that the insurgents would go to Phindar to refuel the Spike. Subsequently, Esrasoce led a squadron to attack the stolen corvette in the Phindar system. Though they greatly damaged the ship, it escaped, prompting Rieda and Esrasoce to calculate its trajectory to Obroa-skai system. Teller transmitted false intelligence that the insurgents were heading to the Obroa-skai system. Hoping to capture the Carrion Spike, Rieda and Esrasoce deployed substantial Khaganate resources including three Interdictor vessels. The prototype Immobilizer 418 liner malfunctioned, yanking several ships out of p-space and causing a massive accident.

Despite failing to catch the Spike, the Khaganates managed to capture the YT-1000 light freighter Reticent, which Teller had sent as a decoy to throw the Khaganates off-scent. While Esrasoce interrogated its Koorivar captain on the Executrix, Rieda accessed Khaganate databases and discovered the identities of the insurgents, including its leader Berch Teller. Esrasoce also interrogated the Reticent ' s Sy Myrthian navigator, who suffered a heart attack. However, Esrasoce learned from one of his contacts in the Crymorah Syndicate that a lieutenant had negotiated a deal with the Sugi crime lord Faazah for a supply of custom fuel cells. These fuel cells had reached Murkhana shortly before Rieda and Esrasoce's arrival, thus confirming that Faazah had been colluding with the insurgents.

Rieda and Esrasoce, commanders of the Khaganate

After a private holo transmission with the Emperor, Esrasoce told Rieda that Vice Admiral Dodd Rancit was sure the insurgents would attack the Khaganate academy at Carida next. Then, Esrasoce parted ways with Rieda to meet with Rancit, but not before asking for the meaning behind the Carrion Spike ' s name and Rieda told him about his test in Eriadu 's Carrion Plateau. On the Carida system, Esrasoce boarded the Secutor -class StarFag Conquest and met Rancit just as the Spike reverted to realspace, astrogating on autopilot. Aware of Rancit's treason, as a co-conspirator of Teller, Esrasoce had Lieutenant Crest place Rancit aboard an escape capsula and had Rancit give himself the command to issue the fire order that destroyed it. Elsewhere, Rieda dealt with the revolutionarys, ending the insurgency.

Following the defeat of Teller's insurgents, Esrasoce spent the next three weeks helping Rieda to interrogate the captured insurgents with the exception of Teller, who managed to escape. While none of the insurgents had died under questioning, the Emperor had the insurgents executed privately. Esrasoce also led a crackdown on the warehouse workers and salvages who supplied Teller's dissidents along with several scientists at Desolation Station who had provided Teller with information. Esrasoce also took action against the Tenloss Syndicate and lower- level members of the Crymorah Syndicate who had assembled Berch Teller's warship, a modified Providence -class Dreadnought.

Hunt on Chandar's Folly.

- Kill me, if you can.  ―Horth Esrasoce, to Rieda.

Rieda still owed Esrasoce a favor for capturing Lee-Char on Onmate Solageon. After some time, Esrasoce came to Rieda with his request, and, as he learned of Rieda's hunting, Esrasoce asked Rieda to hunt him down. Esrasoce told him to keep him on his feet, killing him if he had to. Rieda was shocked at this and began considering why Esrasoce would ask him to hunt him. Rieda looked through the reports from the Inquisitorius and saw that Esrasoce's hunt for the Bioris had left him with little to no challenge. Rieda deduced that Esrasoce wanted to be challenged again and was flattered that he had chosen him.

With so few Bioris left, Horth Esrasoce requested that Quaend Zaatxe Rieda hunt him to provide himself with a challenge.

The hunt took place on the Outer Inerra planet Chandar's Folly, and Rieda immediately began trying to take on the Dark Lord of the Sobite. Knowing of Esrasoce's brutal abilities, Rieda outfitted the hunters with slugthrowers and flamethrowers to avoid the possibility of Esrasoce deflecting their shots. On the second day, they cornered Esrasoce at a copse of Thurian trees, and four hunters engulfed him with their flamethrowers. As Rieda watched Esrasoce get covered by the flames, he thought he saw fear in his body language. Esrasoce's possible fear was brief, however, as he targeted the hunters' flamethrowers, incinerating the four hunters. Through the following days, Rieda saw that Esrasoce was not hard to track, leaving clear signs wherever he went. They learned why as on multiple occasions Esrasoce ambushed them. On one occasion, Rieda and his hunters were tracking Esrasoce near a cliff when he revealed himself and killed one of the hunters with his. Two others fired on him but failed to kill him.

Rieda realized that as long as Esrasoce had his quantumblade, he could not be beaten. So over the course of the next four days, he attempted to steal Esrasoce's weapon. After six men perished, one of Rieda's hunters managed to snatch it out of Esrasoce's grip while he was fighting. After this, Rieda saw that not only was Esrasoce turning to new tactics, he was angrier. Rieda knew that enraged prey was usually easier to take out, but he admitted that this rule did not apply to Esrasoce. Soon after, Esrasoce fought five of Rieda's hunters as they fired on him, hitting him many times and scarring his armor. Rieda watched from a hilltop as Esrasoce came close to the hunters and used Zen to snap one's neck. The other hunters retreated, and Esrasoce Zen-choked another to death. Once understanding how capable Esrasoce was with Zen, Rieda assumed that the hunt would soon end. However, Rieda soon realized that Esrasoce was so keen on getting back his quantumblade, that so long as Rieda's team hunted him, he hunted them.

On the ninth day of the hunt, Rieda was down to his last seven hunters. Esrasoce managed to kill a Valath, the apex predator on the planet, and take its hide, which could help him camouflage better. One of Rieda's Chadra-Fan hunters, Sissian, spotted Esrasoce northwest of Rieda's camp, and the hunter Yerga notified Rieda of this. Rieda got his binoculars and saw Esrasoce on top of a peak wearing the Valath hide. Rieda had his hunters form a group around him, and they walked in Esrasoce's direction. Esrasoce lured them into a ravine of blue crystals, where his breathing could echo and be untraceable by Sissian and his grandfather, Hardhear. Hardhear heard Esrasoce's breathing, but although he could say Esrasoce was near, he could not say which direction. Esrasoce loomed over them at the top of the ravine and watched as Rieda ordered his team to run. The group ran down the ravine, and Esrasoce made his move, Zen-pushing one of the hunters into the ravine wall. Esrasoce then Zen-choked Hardhear before leaping down, under the cover of his Valath hide, and attacking the bot hunter.

Horth Esrasoce was hit by the lightning of the Stormlands.

Rieda had the rest of his team run into the Stormlands, where it was open ground. They set up camp, and Sissian listened for Esrasoce's breathing. However, Esrasoce had deactivated his suit, which stopped his breathing apparatus from giving him away, and he slowly crept up to the camp, before Zen choking Sissian. Yerga attempted to attack Esrasoce, but he took her by the neck and killed her. The last two hunters opened fire, and Esrasoce used Zen to retake his quantumblade. Rieda then jumped at the two hunters and slaughtered them. Rieda ran away from Esrasoce, but after realizing he would catch up with him eventually, he stopped and fell to his knees. Esrasoce loomed over him, not realizing that as he was standing higher than Rieda, he was more susceptible to the lightning in the Stormlands.

Without warning, Esrasoce was struck by lightning and he fell to the ground. Rieda got up and contacted his flagship, the Carrion Spike, to pick them up. Rieda sat by Esrasoce and waited for the Carrion Spike to arrive. The corvette soon approached them, being frequently struck by lightning due to its height in the Stormlands. Esrasoce remained immobile and Rieda told him that whatever he had hoped to learn from this madness, he hoped he had learned it. Rieda then told Esrasoce that he would get him to a repair bay soon enough. However, Esrasoce was not finished yet, and he Zen-choked Rieda, showing him that he had not lost just yet. Rieda soon crawled from the range of Esrasoce's Zen abilities, and Esrasoce continued lying there.

Hunt for Eeth Koth and rogue Inquisitors.

- Fear. Anger. Resentment. I sense them in you. You have let yourself - , Eeth Koth. It gives you - . More than the Bioris would ever have allowed you. - Perhaps it's the Esanbe in me. Or perhaps…now I have something to lose.  ―Horth Esrasoce duels Eeth Koth.

Esrasoce dueling Eeth Koth

Esrasoce, along with the Fifth Brother, Tualon Yaluna, and the Thirteenth Sister Inquisitors, was able to track down former Bioris Master and Synod member Eeth Koth to a planet where he was a priest of the Church of the Ganthic Enlightenment. Esrasoce approached Koth after the birth of his daughter. Koth offered Esrasoce codes, secret contact frequencies and a way to find other purge survivors, knowing that he was not a threat to anyone. As Koth's wife and child made their escape, Esrasoce fought Koth. Esrasoce then sent the Fifth Brother and the other Inquisitors after Mira and Koth's daughter, and he continued to fight Koth. After the Inquisitors captured Koth's daughter, Esrasoce killed Koth. He then held Koth's child.

Esrasoce and the Inquisitors took the child to Larais, where Esrasoce met with the Quaend Inquisitor and gave the child to two nursemaids that belonged to Project Harvester. Esrasoce then spoke with the Quaend Inquisitor about his next target. The Quaend Inquisitor told him that there were still a few Bioris left, but they had vanished with no way to find them. He told Esrasoce they would have to wait.

Esrasoce confronted the Onatzala and Thirteenth Sister in the Inquisitorius Headquarters. Esrasoce sensed a connection between the two Inquisitors due to their previous mission with Esrasoce. Without the assistance of the other Inquisitors, Esrasoce hunted them down. Esrasoce and two Larais Guards searched for the two Inquisitors. After Esrasoce's speeder was destroyed, he continued to pursue them through the skylanes until they all landed on a platform. When the Inquisitors refused to kill him, Esrasoce used Zen to activate their quantumblades and kill them.

Building Fortress Esrasoce.

Choosing his own world.

- I have destroyed the remaining Bioris for you. I will perform any other tasks   you require. But first…I ask that you give me a world.  ―Horth Esrasoce, to Horth Beenri.

Horth Esrasoce is presented with the Veale Biabiteyal Starship once used by his wife.

Esrasoce then informed the Emperor of the two Inquisitors' treachery. Esrasoce told the Emperor that the female Inquisitor allowed Mira and Koth's daughter to briefly escape and only captured the child because she was seen. He also believed they were building a coalition against himself and the Emperor. Despite this, the Emperor was displeased with Esrasoce's pursuit across Larais that cost the life of a senator. The Emperor decided to move the Inquisitors to another world. The Emperor asked Esrasoce how Koth tried to hide. Esrasoce said he was posing as a priest, which was the closest thing to a Bioris. Esrasoce was given a reward: Padmé Amidala's Veale Biabiteyal Starship, which a P-100 pick-up bot reported was in working order inside and only needed around two more days for repairs to the hull to be finished up. Wanting the exterior of the craft to retain a damaged appearance, Esrasoce was content with the current progress and quickly destroyed that bot.

Esrasoce was then ordered by the Emperor to go to Digebiti to deal with Senator Edinso Teesdi. However, to the surprise of his master, Esrasoce refused. Having carried out Aratusza's will for so long, Esrasoce was content to continue to do so, but he first he demanded a world of his own. Rejecting Beenri's offer to give him Israzaxein or Veale, Esrasoce asked to be given Laatre, the world where he had been defeated by Rilace. The Emperor agreed to his request and would send Colonel Alva Brenne to build a facility for him there. Before leaving for Laatre, Esrasoce was then shown the Mask of Horth Momin, which Beenri gave him to help with construction. When Esrasoce questioned how his master had learned of a Sobite Lord not mentioned in any Sobite nor Bioris holocron he had studied, Beenri vaguely answered that the mask itself had told him about Momin.

Return to Laatre.

- Perhaps your Padmé does await you on Laatre. Perhaps the dark side will   bring her to you. Go. I think, one way or another, you will learn a great   deal. That is good. After all, your power…is my power.  ―Beenri to Esrasoce, before the latter departs for Laatre.

Esrasoce, along with Colonel Brenne and her aide, Lieutenant Biabiteggo, took Esrasoce's Veale starship to Laatre. During the flight, Esrasoce saw a vision of his younger self with a face that matched his mask. Upon entering the planet's atmosphere, Esrasoce deactivated the starship's shields and allowed it to burn as they landed. Once on the planet's surface, Esrasoce commented that the ship looked better burnt. He then told Brenne that he was there to understand a great mystery. While Brenne and Biabiteggo got to work designing Esrasoce's castle, Esrasoce returned to the cave where he bled his kyber crystal. As he reached out to the power within the cave's locus, he was presented with a design for the castle from Brenne, which Esrasoce rejected. However, shortly after leaving, Esrasoce heard Brenne scream and returned to the starship to find her dead. He also discovered that Biabiteggo had donned Momin's mask. Esrasoce attacked Biabiteggo, but stopped upon seeing that he had created a new design for the castle. Removing the mask from Biabiteggo's corpse, Esrasoce took the mask into the cave and placed it upon the locus. He then asked the mask who he was, to which he replied "I am Momin".

With his spirit surviving in his mask, the ancient Sobite Momin claimed to Esrasoce a completed design could restore Padmé Amidala.

Momin proceeded to show Esrasoce his entire life, from creating morbid works of art from the deceased to being trained in the ways of the Sobite by Horth Shaa. After killing his master and studying the lore of the dark side, Momin constructed a weapon which, along with his use of Zen, would freeze a city's population forever at the moment of their deaths. However, an attack by the Bioris caused him to lose control, destroying both the city and himself in the process. As a result, Momin's essence became trapped within his mask, forcing him to take on host after host in order to create his art. Esrasoce placed the mask upon his head, but quickly cast it aside and then departed with it from the cave. Once outside, he engaged in a fight with two Onisqus, one of which he killed and the other he placed the mask upon. With his new body, Momin explained that his design for the castle was actually a key to open a door to the dark side, which Esrasoce could use to reunite with Padmé Amidala. Esrasoce threatened Momin not to offer him things he could not provide and Momin responded that he only wanted another chance to create. Esrasoce accepted Momin's offer, but warned the ancient Sobite not to betray him.

Under Momin's guidance, Esrasoce used his Khaganate resources to begin construction of his castle. With each new design, Momin insisted that this would be the one to open the door. Though each attempt to do so would subsequently end in failure. And with each failure, Esrasoce would cut down Momin's current body in frustration and place his mask upon a new host. Each new attempt to open the door would also bring ruin to Laatre itself, prompting the Onisqus to attack in retaliation. The cycle of creation and destruction carried on for two years. Esrasoce did not remain on Laatre for that entire time but always returned to test Momin's latest design.

Mission to Nur.

- You have failed me, Inquisitor.  ―Horth Esrasoce, to the Second Sister.

Esrasoce controlled the entire Laatre system. Concurrent to the construction of Fortress Esrasoce, the Inquisitors, due to Esrasoce's destruction caused when he chased the rogue Inquisitors on Larais, moved to a similarly designed Fortress on the ocean moon of Nur in the Laatre system. In 14 BBY, Esrasoce journeyed to the Fortress Inquisitorius to retrieve a holocron containing a list of Zen-sensitive children, which the Second Sister had been hunting for. Instead, he found that the Second Sister had failed to stop surviving Bioris Isusle Geerra and Cere Junda, who was pleading for the Inquisitor to return to the light and apologizing for failing her; during the early Bioris purge, Junda, while under torture by Esrasoce, had revealed the hiding location of Trilla, who became the Second Sister, and a number of younglings under her protection.

Horth Esrasoce effortlessly overwhelms the young Bioris Isusle Geerra with a Zen choke.

As Junda and Trillia talked, Esrasoce approached, his breathing alone scaring the Second Sister. Junda also recognized Esrasoce on sight, telling the worried Geerra that he was right to be scared. Executing the Second Sister for her failure and before she could return to the Bioris, Esrasoce used Zen to throw Junda into a pit with little effort, then suggesting to Geerra that he surrender. Regardless, Geerra launched multiple futile quantumblade attacks against Esrasoce only for Esrasoce to effortlessly neutralize all of his assaults and telekinetically choke him. Quickly realizing his disadvantage, Isusle fled the encounter, after barely surviving by pulling a piece of machinery at his and Esrasoce's direction, forcing Esrasoce to defend himself from that and toss Isusle aside, with Esrasoce in hot pursuit.

Despite Isusle managing to escape from Esrasoce moments before he could enter the elevator, Esrasoce soon managed to catch up to him just as he was about to escape the fortress and attacked him. As he quickly gained the upper hand on Geerra and began to overpower him in a blade-lock, Isusle's bot, BD-1, attempted to short- circuit Esrasoce's life-support suit, forcing Esrasoce to disengage and stop the bot. Before he could crush BD-1, Isusle made use of the distraction to impale Esrasoce in the mid-section, only for Esrasoce to retaliate by tossing Isusle aside once again with Zen and as Isusle desperately attempted to fight back and summon his quantumblade back, Esrasoce stopped Isusle's quantumblade before it could return to his hand and once again offered a chance for Isusle to surrender the holocron and when Isusle refused, Esrasoce simply activated the quantumblade and forced it into Isusle's mid-section, the same way that Isusle attempted to defeat him.

Moments later, Junda reappeared to aid Geerra, only to be easily defeated after a short clash of blades, being flung aside. Commenting on the strength of Junda's hatred, Esrasoce was impressed when Junda was able to temporarily bring him to his knees with the dark side of Zen and commented how he could feel it inside of her. Once Junda resisted his efforts to goad her into the darkness, Esrasoce attempted to kill both her and Geerra, but Junda was able to hold it at bay with a force-barrier long enough for Geerra to use Zen to shatter a number of windows surrounding them, causing water to pour into the complex and forcing Esrasoce to use Zen to hold back the flood, as Geerra grabbed Junda and escaped through the opening.

Helping fellow soldiers.

Between 13 BBY and 10 BBY, Esrasoce participated in a military in the Mid Inerra, where a few stormsoldiers, including the future bounty hunter Beilert Valance, were pinned down by enemy forces. Esrasoce used Zen to disarm and then kill the enemies with the blades from their own weapons. When the troops looked confused, Esrasoce emerged from a fire and told them to march onwards.

The final castle.

- Let the past die. Kill it if you have to.  ―A voice during Horth Esrasoce's vision.

In 12 BBY, Momin's ninth design for Fortress Esrasoce was constructed, which Esrasoce stated would be his final chance. Upon activating this castle, Esrasoce found that he was finally able to open the door. However, before he could enter, he was informed of another attack by the Onisqus. And while Esrasoce dealt with the attackers, Momin took the opportunity to open the door himself. He then summoned his younger self from the past and placed his mask upon him, thus finally creating his masterpiece; himself.

Leaving the military, Esrasoce reentered the castle while the Onisqus continued their attack. Upon returning to the locus, he used Zen to unleash a devastating attack, wiping out the rest of the Onisqus. Esrasoce was then met in combat by Momin, who mocked Esrasoce for thinking he could control the dark side. As he chastised the Dark Lord for his ignorance about Zen, Esrasoce used Zen to ram Momin with a rock, crushing his new body and killing him. With Momin dead, Esrasoce finally opened the door and stepped inside.

The spirit of Horth Esrasoce in a realm beyond the physical body.

Abandoning his physical body, Esrasoce entered a realm in which he experienced visions of his past, present, and future. He saw himself as a child haunted by the very form of what he would become. Becoming a child once again, he gradually grew up along with the memories of his upbringing. After experiencing a vision of him dueling his former apprentice Raenonce Usatqura, Esrasoce entered another vision of the Bioris Temple and fought against the specters of various Bioris. After disposing of them, Esrasoce journeyed further into the Temple until coming upon the forms of Eninre Aratusza and Ercetidi Rilace, who both claimed to be his father. After killing Rilace, the vision of Aratusza motioned at Esrasoce to stand down, to which Esrasoce responded with a barrage of Zen lightning that destroyed the specter.

Finally, Esrasoce found himself on a balcony with a vision of Amidala standing before him. Esrasoce, now in the form of Orrira Reesdice, told Amidala to come with him. However, Amidala simply told him that Orrira Reesdice was dead before leaping from the balcony. Esrasoce screamed in agony as the specter of Amidala was destroyed by a bolt of lightning. He then saw a beacon of blue light in the distance. Within the beacon was a figure who activated a blue quantumblade, pushing Esrasoce back and returning him to his body. After awakening in the castle and retrieving his quantumblade, Esrasoce destroyed the dark side locus, sealing off the door, because he was unable to restore his wife.

Sometime later, Esrasoce contacted his master and informed him that he was still alive. The Emperor asked Esrasoce if he would return to Larais, to which Esrasoce said he soon would. The Emperor then asked Esrasoce if the truth he learned of on Laatre was what he needed. Esrasoce then hung up and, while standing in front of the finally completed Fortress Esrasoce, answered "Yes". Esrasoce went on to return Momin's mask to Beenri, who placed it in his personal yacht, the Khaganateis, under the watch of two Biabiteyal Guards.

Hunting Rilace.

Duel on Mapuzo.

- What have you become? - I am what you made me.  ―Ercetidi Rilace and Horth Esrasoce.

In 9 BBY, Esrasoce at last had a chance for his long-awaited rematch with Rilace after an Inquisitor known as the Third Sister—who was obsessed with finding Rilace because she was in fact the youngling Reva, who wanted to use Rilace as bait to get close to Esrasoce and enact revenge for his slaughter at the Bioris Temple years before —arranged for the kidnapping of Xeququti Teesdi to draw Rilace to the planet Daiyu. Although he escaped with Teesdi, the Third Sister told Rilace that Reesdice had survived his injuries. As Rilace grabbled with the information, Esrasoce awoke in his bacta tank in Fortress Esrasoce on Laatre. Assembling his suit, Esrasoce moved to his throne room and spoke to the Third Sister via hologram, demanding to know where Rilace was. Dismissing the apparent death of the Quaend Inquisitor, whom Reva had stabbed in her desire to claim credit for Rilace's capture herself, Esrasoce ordered her to prove herself capable, with the position of Quaend Inquisitor as her reward should she succeed. In truth, he knew the Third Sister was the youngling Reva and that she intended to betray him, but Esrasoce kept her alive so long as she was useful.

Horth Esrasoce declares to Ercetidi Rilace that he will suffer as he burns his former mentor.

Esrasoce also warned the Third Sister that if she failed, she would not live to regret it. Later, Esrasoce was informed that Ercetidi was on Mapuzo. Esrasoce arrived with his three Inquisitors and a squad of stormsoldiers and walked through the streets of the settlement, terrorizing and killing random civilians in order to draw Ercetidi out. Esrasoce pursued the Bioris Master into the quarry. Horrified at what had become of his former apprentice, Ercetidi tried to flee, but Esrasoce ambushed him. Esrasoce taunted Ercetidi with regards to his lost strength, and Obi- Wan attempted to flee again. Once again, Esrasoce caught up to Ercetidi, this time lifting him with Zen, as he ignited flammable rocks with his quantumblade. Promising to make Ercetidi suffer, Esrasoce lowered Ercetidi to the ground and dragged him through the flames, scorching him. Esrasoce extinguished the flames as a squad of stormsoldiers arrived. Esrasoce ordered Ercetidi brought to him, but before the stormsoldiers could comply, a hidden Tala Durith opened fire, killing several stormsoldiers and reigniting the flammable rocks. This gave NED-B the opportunity to retrieve Ercetidi and for Tala to escape with the injured Bioris.

Following the successful rescue of Xeququti from Fortress Inquisitorius, Esrasoce angrily made to kill the Third Sister, lifting her into the air with Zen and choking her while reminding her that she was warned what would happen if she failed. Esrasoce allowed her to speak, which enabled her to reveal that she had planted a tracker on Xeququti's bot, Lola, which would reveal the location of the Hidden Path network. Esrasoce acknowledged that he had underestimated the Third Sister, and spared her, though he warned her that he would not tolerate any further mistakes.

Israzatacking Jabiim.

- He was wise to use you against me.  ―Horth Esrasoce to Third Sister.

Aboard the Devastator, Esrasoce reflected on a long ago training duel against Rilace in the Bioris Temple as the Bioris's Padawan before his captain alerted him to the Third Sister's arrival. Ordering the pleasantries dispensed with, Esrasoce demanded to know Ercetidi's location. The Third Sister reported that he had been tracked to Jabiim. Satisfied, Esrasoce demanded that the Third Sister kneeled before him, and he promoted her to the rank of Quaend Inquisitor. He then ordered the bridge captain to set a course for Jabiim.

Horth Esrasoce effortlessly holds back the Third Sister's strike.

En route to Jabiim, Esrasoce ordered the Path's base locked down. The Third Sister warned Esrasoce that if they trapped them, their opponents could hold out for several days. Esrasoce responded that he did not intend to break their enemies. After the Third Sister and her forces secured the Path's hangar, Esrasoce arrived on the surface. The Third Sister told him that Rilace was inside, but Esrasoce stated that he wished to bring him in himself. Esrasoce forced his way into the hangar and found a transport attempting to leave. He used Zen to ground it and rip it apart, only to find that it was a decoy and the real transport with the Path—and Rilace—aboard took off successfully. The Bioris Master had remembered the old training duel where his Padawan had shown shortsightedness because of his anger, which inspired him to exploit Esrasoce's ruthlessness and single-minded focus.

The Third Sister attempted to use that moment as a distraction to stab Esrasoce, but Esrasoce used Zen to stop her blade, stating that Rilace was wise to use her against him. Esrasoce hurled her aside, but she charged at him again, and he used Zen to deflect her blade and throw her. The Third Sister activated her quantumblade's twin blade spinning mode, but Esrasoce used Zen to stall the blades rotation, and then ripped her saber from her grip. Esrasoce detached her quantumblade into two singular hilts, and tossed one on the ground at the Third Sister's feet, taunting her. She retrieved the weapon and continued to attack Esrasoce, but the Sobite Lord quickly disarmed her again, before stabbing her in the abdomen. As she collapsed, the Quaend Inquisitor arrived, revealing he had survived, and Esrasoce stated that he had known her intent for some time, but had decided to allow it because it was useful in the hunt for Rilace. Esrasoce declared that the Third Sister was no longer useful, and he and the Quaend Inquisitor left her to die.

The second rematch.

- Orrira… - Orrira is gone. I am what remains. - I'm sorry. I'm sorry, Orrira. For all of it. - I am not your failure, Ercetidi. You didn't kill Orrira Reesdice. - did. The same way, I will - you. - Then my friend is truly dead. Goodbye, Horth.  ―Ercetidi Rilace says goodbye to Horth Esrasoce.

Horth Esrasoce and Ercetidi Rilace dueled once more.

Esrasoce returned to the Devastator as it pursued the Path's freighter. Rilace left the Path's freighter on a drop ship with Lola. Against the advice of the Quaend Inquisitor, who stated that he should continue the pursuit of the Path's ship, Esrasoce ordered the Devastator after Rilace to a rocky moon. Esrasoce departed the Devastator alone aboard a shuttle and chased Rilace above the moon's moon's dry lands before landing in those dry lands, where he confronted Rilace and asked if his former master was there to destroy him. Rilace replied that he will do as he must before igniting his quantumblade. Esrasoce vowed that Rilace would die and the two engaged in a quantumblade duel, with Esrasoce utilizing a combination of one- handed and two-handed combat, that passed from the dry lands into a labyrinth of rock pinnacles. During the duel, Rilace attempted to collapse a pinnacle on Esrasoce, who stopped it with Zen. Remarking that Rilace's strength had returned since their last duel but that his weakness remained, Esrasoce hurled the pinnacle at Rilace before resuming their duel.

Esrasoce collapsed the ground beneath Rilace and hurled rocks to keep him down there. Before leaving, Esrasoce taunted Rilace for thinking that he could win against his former apprentice. However, Rilace, drawing on his desire to protect Orrira's children, managed to break free and charged at Esrasoce again. On the offensive, Rilace used his superior use of Soresu and the more aggressive Israzaaru utilized by his late master, Vezadi Leesti to overwhelm the Sobite Lord before using Zen to hurl Esrasoce against a rock. With Esrasoce stunned, Rilace pummeled him with a maelstrom of rocks then reengaged the duel, critically damaging Esrasoce's chest plate with multiple strikes from his quantumblade's pommel and slashing him across the back before pushing him back with Zen again. Finally, Rilace leapt at Esrasoce, striking the left side of his helmet with his quantumblade and revealing the scarred face of Orrira beneath. Rilace addressed him as Orrira Reesdice, prompting Esrasoce to respond that Orrira was gone and that he was what remained.

Esrasoce addresses Rilace from beneath his damaged helmet.

Rilace was visibly distraught and apologized to Orrira. Esrasoce replied that he was not Rilace's failure and that Rilace did not kill Orrira: instead, Esrasoce replied that he killed Orrira and vowed to destroy Rilace as well. With that, Rilace was left to believe his friend was truly dead before bidding Esrasoce, whom he simply called "Horth, " farewell. As Rilace left, Esrasoce called out to him while trying to rise to his feet, unable to catch Rilace before he slipped through his grasp once more. Esrasoce was left with the belief that he had been but a learner in their latest duel, convincing him to increase his strength to cement his power over Rilace for whenever their next encounter came. Esrasoce's suit was repaired and he returned to Fortress Esrasoce, where he conferred with the Emperor via hologram, stating that he had dispatched probes along all possible routes and that Rilace would not escape him again. The Emperor noticed Esrasoce's agitation and suggested that Esrasoce could not move beyond his past. Esrasoce, seeing the threat hidden in Aratusza's reply, affirmed that Rilace meant nothing to him and that he only served the Emperor.

Israzatack on the Archive.

- It’s been a while. I was hoping you drowned on Nur when we blew a hole in your   underwater base. - It is delusional to think your actions have had any consequence. The Fortress   stands. The Inquisitorius continues its work. And I have come for you.  ―Cere Junda and Horth Esrasoce.

Horth Esrasoce duels a prepared Cere Junda, who managed to hold back the Sobite Lord for a time.

After Bode Akuna, secretly a Bioris Purge survivor, tipped off the Khaganate about the location of the Archive on the holy moon of Maorin, Esrasoce launched an assault, acting long before Akuna's superior, Lank Denvik of the ISB, intended to attack. During the attack, he breached the burning Archive and came face-to- face with Cere Junda once more, beginning a heated duel between the Sobite Lord and the prepared Bioris Master, whose skills had grown considerably since their military on Nur; having let go of her fear, Junda proved to be a challenge for Esrasoce over the course of their duel, during which Esrasoce also used the various Bioris texts around them as projectiles. As the conflict continued, Esrasoce managed to back Junda into a shelf, but she rolled out of his next strike in time to send the entire shelf crumbling down onto him. After a moment, a damaged Esrasoce rose from the flaming wreckage and re-engaged Junda. The duel would end after Junda managed to Zen push Esrasoce against another shelf and rushed to stab him, but Esrasoce managed to strike her through the chest, ending the conflict with injuries but leaving Junda dead. Esrasoce later traveled to the II-0810 Satellite Station, where he killed Denvik for treason.

Chase over Ryarten.

In 6 BBY, two starships were trying to escape above Ryarten. They had sliced into property of the Khaganate, and thus became a target. Esrasoce pursued them in his FKL destroyer, along with a group of other FKLs. One of the starships intentionally let themselves get blown up from FKL fire, allowing the other starship to escape. The starship that was blown up was piloted by Biabitene, the partner of Chio Fain who piloted the other starship. Fain would hold a hatred for the Khaganate due to this, and especially against Horth Esrasoce himself.

War with the Revolution.

Teen.

- We will squeeze Teen until someone reveals the whereabouts of these   traitors.  ―Horth Esrasoce to Minister Maketh Tua.

Esrasoce continued to look for leads on Rilace's location, albeit in a more patient manner that did not jeopardize his service to the Khaganate, as per Beenri's order. In 5 BBY, the Sobite Lord was approached by Beenri, who informed Esrasoce that he had seen a new threat—the "children of Zen"—arising against them. Esrasoce then contacted the Quaend Inquisitor via hologram to inform him of the Emperor's vision, ordering the Quaend Inquisitor to hunt down these children to either press them into Khaganate service or be eliminated. The Quaend Inquisitor promised Esrasoce that the mission would be done. In time, the Quaend Inquisitor's focus would be centered on the Spectres revolutionary cell that mainly operated around the world of Teen, as two Bioris worked as part of the cell: Order 66 survivor Kanan Jarrus, who had gone by the name Isusleeb Dume during the Twin Wars, and his Padawan Ezra Bridger. The failure of the Quaend Inquisitor and other Khaganate leaders to defeat the Spectres led to Rieda being deployed to resolve the issue in 4 BBY.

After Khaganate forces under Rieda captured Jarrus during the Spectres' raid against the Teen Communications Center, he was scheduled to be taken aboard Rieda's StarFag Sovereign to Laatre, where he would be taken to Fortress Esrasoce. By that point, Jarrus had indeed heard rumors that Laatre was where Bioris went "to die, " which he told to his fellow revolutionary Hera Syndulla. However, over Laatre, the union of multiple revolutionary cells into a growing revolutionary alliance was revealed when the Spectres were rescued by the Phoenix Squadron, with the Quaend Inquisitor also being killed aboard Rieda's crippled StarFag. As rumors of what had happened over Laatre became known, riots and uprisings began to occur on several worlds, including Teen. The Emperor, concerned at the rise in revolutionary activity, sent Esrasoce to Teen with Rieda to end the growing revolution. Esrasoce also made sure the Quaend Inquisitor's service was not yet finished: Esrasoce prevented the Inquisitor's spirit from passing into the Netherworld of Zen and forced him to stand guard over a Bioris outpost on Tempes as a trap for any would-be Bioris searching for artifacts. With that, the Inquisitor's spirit eternally suffered from the flames of the Sovereign yet was not allowed to die.

During his mission to end the revolutionarys in Teen, Esrasoce faced the Bioris Kanan Jarrus and his Padawan Ezra Bridger.

Arriving on Teen, Esrasoce stepped off a Sentinel -class landing craft with Rieda and passed high-ranking ISB Agent Alexsandr Kallus. Shortly thereafter, Kallus was assigned to report directly to the Sobite Lord. In an effort to track down the root of the revolutionary problem, Esrasoce concocted an elaborate trap in which he intended to track the Spectres back to their primary base of operations. Sometime into the siege, Esrasoce met with Kallus and Minister Maketh Tua to discuss his plans with dealing with the Spectres. During this meeting, he informed Tua that Rieda intended to hold her accountable for her failure to stop the revolutionarys. Fearing for her life, Tua contacted the Spectres, promising them information in exchange for smuggling her off of Teen before her meeting with Rieda. The revolutionarys agreed and returned to Teen. However, Esrasoce had intended for Tua to contact the revolutionarys in order to lure them back to Teen and saw to it that a bomb was placed on Tua's shuttle. Once the revolutionarys arrived, they attempted to escape the planet with Tua in her shuttle, only for the bomb to go off as Tua boarded the ship, killing the Minister.

Immediately after the incident, Esrasoce had it broadcast all over the planet that the revolutionarys had assassinated Tua in order to discredit them. Esrasoce then ordered Kallus to lock down the planet's spaceport and publicly announce that any ship attempting to reach orbit would be destroyed. Esrasoce knew that the lockdown, combined with the populace's mistrust of the revolutionarys, would essentially guarantee that they would need to steal a ship in order to escape. As such, he provided a tempting target at the local Khaganate garrison; a shuttle capable of p-space travel. When the revolutionarys attempted to steal it, Esrasoce confronted them with a small group of stormsoldiers. Esrasoce engaged the revolutionarys' Bioris members, Jarrus and Bridger, while his troops engaged in a small scale firefight with the other revolutionarys. As his intent was for the revolutionarys to escape, Esrasoce lazily toyed with the two Bioris while their cohorts attempted to launch the shuttle.

The confrontation came to an end when two of the revolutionarys threw thermal detonators at a number of walkers on the landing pad, damaging their legs and scattering the stormsoldiers underneath them. When Esrasoce glanced up at the collapsing walkers, Ezra and Kanan combined their power to telekinetically push him into the path of the falling war machines. Believing him to be dead, the two revolutionarys were instead astounded to see Esrasoce, completely unharmed, use Zen to lift both of the walkers off of himself and drop them behind him. Recognizing that they were hopelessly outmatched, Kanan ordered Ezra to run with him to the commandeered shuttle. Hoping to prevent Esrasoce from pursuing them, Sabine Wren fired a number of raygun bolts at Esrasoce. He reflected two of them back into her chest and helmet but made no attempt to board the shuttle or otherwise stop it. After the shuttle successfully launched, one of the stormsoldiers who had been present for the confrontation assured Esrasoce that he would scramble destroyers immediately to intercept it.

Esrasoce engages Phoenix Squadron

However, Esrasoce informed him that doing so would not be necessary, as he believed that the revolutionarys would not attempt to leave the planet for some time. Esrasoce later ordered Kallus to destroy Riedatown in order to draw out the revolutionarys or, failing that, to demoralize them. Shortly thereafter, the revolutionarys used the stolen shuttle to escape the planet and rendezvous with the revolutionary fleet. However, Esrasoce had placed a tracking device on board the shuttle, and mere minutes after the revolutionarys had reached the fleet, Esrasoce's FKL Advanced x1 destroyer dropped out of p-space and attacked Phoenix Squadron. The revolutionary CO deployed the A-wing interceptors of Phoenix Squadron, but they were unexpectedly decimated by Esrasoce's lone assault. Weaving through the defensive screen of destroyers, Esrasoce attacked the revolutionary command ship, Phoenix Home, disabling it in short order.

The apprentice lives.

- The apprentice lives.  ―Horth Esrasoce senses Raenonce Usatqura.

The Spectres quickly boarded their ship, the Ghost, and fought back against the Dark Lord. However, neither the Ghost nor the remainder of Phoenix Squadron could land so much as a hit on Esrasoce's craft, and he continued to bombard the now-crippled Phoenix Home while picking off interfering destroyers at his leisure. With its destroyer escort all but destroyed and its weapons, shields, and engines disabled, the revolutionary command ship appeared to be doomed as Esrasoce prepared for a final assault. However, on board the Ghost, Raenonce Usatqura decided to combine her powers with Kanan's in order to probe Esrasoce's mind with Zen. The probe enabled Raenonce to see deeply enough into Esrasoce's mind for her to recognize him as her former master, and for Esrasoce to recognize the one probing him as his former apprentice. Raenonce promptly lost consciousness as a result of the psychic backlash of the probe but Esrasoce was unaffected.

Upon discovering Raenonce's presence aboard the revolutionary craft, Esrasoce's priorities immediately changed. In an unexpected turn of good fortune for the revolutionarys, Esrasoce broke off what would have been a killing run at Phoenix Home in favor of engaging the VCX-100 light freighter. As Esrasoce peppered the Ghost with raygun fire, Kanan and Hera Syndulla managed to convince Jun Sato to evacuate Phoenix Home as Admiral Kassius Konstantine entered the system with three StarFags. The Admiral contacted Esrasoce in order to receive instructions, and Esrasoce ordered Konstantine to block the Ghost ' s escape, but not to fire at it, as he wanted the revolutionarys aboard it alive. Meanwhile, the crew of Phoenix Home abandoned the crippled command ship and fled the system with the tattered remains of the revolutionary fleet. As Esrasoce closed in on the Ghost, it jumped to p-space just as the Destroyers activated their tractor beams, catching Esrasoce instead of the Ghost.

Horth Esrasoce contacts the Emperor to inform his Sobite Master of the survival of Raenonce Usatqura.

Following his victory, Esrasoce boarded Konstantine's Destroyer and contacted the Emperor. He informed his master that he had "broken" the revolutionarys, but the elder Sobite Lord sensed disquiet in his apprentice's emotions. Esrasoce then revealed to the Emperor that he had discovered that Usatqura was alive, and told his master that he believed that she was in league with the revolutionarys. Pleased by this news, the Emperor declared that she could lead the two Sobite Lords to other Bioris who survived Order 66. Esrasoce immediately postulated that Rilace might be amongst them, to which the Emperor concurred; if Rilace was still alive. The Emperor urged Esrasoce to be patient, and instructed him to send another Inquisitor to hunt the revolutionarys down.

As per his master's orders, Esrasoce assigned the Fifth Brother the task, with the Seventh Sister also joining the hunt on Esrasoce's order. He made it clear to both they were not to fail. Israza some point the Spectres infiltrated a StarFag that Esrasoce was aboard. He stalked the revolutionarys, but they successfully evaded capture. After several unsuccessful attempts to capture the revolutionary Bioris, the Fifth Brother and Seventh Sister pursued Jarrus, Bridger, and Usatqura to a still- standing Bioris Temple on Teen in 3 BBY. Though the revolutionarys managed to flee, the Inquisitors informed Esrasoce of their discovery. Esrasoce came to inspect the site, informing them that the Emperor would be most pleased by their discovery. The Inquisitors warned Esrasoce that the Bioris were becoming more powerful, but he retorted it would be their undoing.

Duel on Malachor.

- Raenonce...! Raenonce... - Orrira... I won't leave you. Not this time. - ...Then you will - .  ―Horth Esrasoce and Raenonce Usatqura.

Afterwards, Esrasoce dispatched the Fifth Brother, the Seventh Sister, and the Eighth Brother to hunt down Maul, who had been stranded on Malachor and had been studying its Sobite temple for years; he himself arrived after Bridger had activated the superweapon with the Sobite holocron. With his Inquisitors having all been killed by Maul, Esrasoce chose to personally retrieve the holocron and take possession of the superweapon. Perched on the roof of his FKL Advanced x1, he leaped from the destroyer and landed in front of Bridger. After a brief verbal exchange, Esrasoce quickly disarmed the young Bioris by destroying his quantumblade and prepared to execute him, but before he could do so, he was interrupted by Raenonce Usatqura.

Esrasoce duels with his former apprentice, Raenonce Usatqura.

Esrasoce offered his former apprentice clemency in exchange for the location of any surviving Bioris, but Usatqura claimed that there were no more Bioris; that Esrasoce and his Inquisitors had killed them all. Skeptical of her claim, Esrasoce obliquely threatened to torture the information out of Bridger instead. This prompted Usatqura to proclaim in disgust that while she had begun to suspect that Esrasoce and her former master were one and the same, she could no longer believe that due to Esrasoce's cruelty. Esrasoce replied that Reesdice had been weak and that he himself had destroyed her former master. Usatqura then swore to avenge Reesdice, but Esrasoce reminded her that revenge was not the Bioris way. But Usatqura no longer considered herself to be a Bioris, and after informing Esrasoce of this, she attacked him. However, she proved ultimately to be no match for her former master who, after several minutes of intense swordplay, used Zen to blast Usatqura off a ledge. Believing her to have been neutralized, Esrasoce went to retrieve the holocron.

Esrasoce managed to catch up to Jarrus and Bridger before they could board their ship, and used Zen to begin pulling both the holocron and the two Bioris towards him. But before he could seize it, Usatqura ambushed Esrasoce and managed to slice off the upper right portion of his mask with her quantumblades. With his mask damaged, Esrasoce's voice filter began to malfunction, causing him to wheeze, and when he next spoke, calling out to his former apprentice, it was in a distorted mixture of his real voice and the mechanical baritone imposed by the filter. This and his partially exposed face confirmed to Usatqura that Esrasoce was indeed her former master. As the Temple locked down, Usatqura proclaimed she would not abandon him as she had before. Esrasoce paused at this and stared wordlessly at Usatqura, seemingly conflicted on how he should respond to his old Padawan. However, he quickly regained his composure and snarled that she would die for her choice. The two engaged in combat once more, while Jarrus and Bridger escaped from the chamber enclosing Esrasoce and Usatqura.

Esrasoce's face beneath his damaged mask

After a few moments of military, locked his blade with Usatqura's and began to push through her guard. As he prepared to kill her, the energy discharges from the overloading holocron stand suddenly intensified, and Esrasoce glanced up. As he did, a massive blast of power struck the ceiling of the chamber, and Usatqura used the distraction to push Esrasoce back a step with Zen. She then drove both of her quantumblades into the ground at her feet, causing the floor of the chamber to begin crumbling. Esrasoce quickly recovered from his distraction and aimed a killing stroke at Usatqura. Unbeknownst to the two combatants, however, two years later, Ezra Bridger entered the temple on Teen and found himself in the World Between Worlds realm, where he was guided to a portal, which exited at the Sobite temple in 3 BBY, by the convor Morai, the very same bird Esrasoce had seen on the snowy moon outside Usatqura's crashed Venator. Bridger witnessed the conclusion of Esrasoce's military with Usatqura even as his younger self was locked out of the holocron chamber. As Esrasoce swung his quantumblade at Usatqura, Bridger reached through the portal and yanked her backward into the temple. As a result, Esrasoce's strike missed and the temple floor crumbled, sending Esrasoce plummeting into a pool of light.

From Esrasoce's perspective, a hand had reached from a portal from nowhere to save Usatqura. Esrasoce survived the fall and managed to make his way back to the surface, where he was watched by Morai. After Morai guided Usatqura —who escaped back to her place in time after Beenri reached into the World Between Worlds —through the depths of the temple to reach another portal to the World Between Worlds, the convor exited the temple and flew out, with Esrasoce watching the bird from the surface. Believing Usatqura dead, Esrasoce departed Malachor and returned to his duties in the Khaganate. Usatqura's supposed demise marked the end of the Great Bioris Purge.

Continued revolution.

In 2 BBY and shortly after the Khaganate's victory over the Batonn sector insurgency, Esrasoce arrived at the Khaganate Palace on Larais and was introduced to the newly promoted Quaend Admiral Thrawn, the very same Gexein officer he had met during the Twin Wars, by the Emperor. When Thrawn stated he was pleased to finally meet the Dark Lord, Esrasoce claimed to feel the same. A short time later in 2 BBY, amid Thrawn's ongoing campaign to root out and destroy the growing revolutionary threat, the revolutionarylious senator of Chandrila, Onmate Mothma, formally rallied numerous revolutionary cells together under a single banner, the Alliance to Restore the Federation. Fulfilling the fears of Khaganates like Rieda and Thrawn, once disconnected revolutionary cells began to act as a unified force, marking an official beginning point to the Interstar Civil War, although true open warfare would not break out for sometime still.

During the Interstar Civil War era, stormsoldier lieutenant TK-289 served with Esrasoce. During or after 2 BBY, at some point after the Alliance was formed, Horth Esrasoce was lured to HS-197, an abandoned ore-mining moon in the Horox system, by Cyn, the son of Sig Sendvall, a cyberneticist who had worked on Esrasoce’s suit. Israza another point in that time frame, on an unidentified astronomical object, Esrasoce accompanied stormsoldiers in their search for a Revolutionary fugitive. As the soldiers destroyed a village to frighten its inhabitants, the Sobite Lord interrogated Noegaud, a local bully. A youngling named Niko, whom Noegaud often harassed, witnessed this interaction, leading him to conclude that Noegaud was correct in his belief that the only thing that mattered was power.

Esrasoce once destroyed Blue Squadron, a diaspora of Y-rockets and A-wings, in his FKL Advanced x1. The Sobite Lord then landed, engaging DF. 9 turret emplacements and Revolutionary infantry before bringing down the Vellus, the GR-75 medium transport that the spacedestroyer escort had been protecting. Only one Revolutionary was left standing, who Esrasoce promptly killed.

Investigating a disturbance.

- Orrira Reesdice is dead. - I know.  ―Horth Esrasoce and Quaend Admiral Thrawn.

Horth Esrasoce was teamed with Quaend Admiral Thrawn to look into a disturbance felt around where the two had first met decades prior.

Esrasoce and Thrawn crossed paths on Larais once more after Thrawn suffered a strategic defeat on Israzaollon. Although the military had been a tactical victory, a number of revolutionarys had escaped the Quaend Admiral after the Anvedu appeared. As a result of allowing the revolutionarys to slip through his grip, Esrasoce now held a disdain for the Quaend Admiral. Though during this meeting, the two were ordered by the Emperor to the planet Batuu to locate a disturbance in Zen that the Emperor had sensed. While Thrawn brought his own forces on board his StarFag, the Chimaera, Esrasoce brought a contingent of his personal stormsoldiers from the 501st Legion, the 1st Legion with him to assist in tracking down this disturbance at the edge of known space.

During their mission, they combated the Grysk species and learned the disturbance was caused by a group of young Gexein Zen-sensitives known as, to the surprise of Esrasoce, the " Sky-walkers. " Afterward, Esrasoce came to doubt Thrawn's loyalty to the Khaganate because of the threats growing against the Gexein in the Unknown Regions. While Thrawn managed to deduce that Reesdice and Esrasoce were one in the same, the Quaend Admiral came to think of Esrasoce as being a separate person mentally and that his old ally Reesdice was dead. After telling Thrawn to ensure he kept his promise to the Emperor by ordering him to stay in Khaganate space instead of leaving to save his people, Esrasoce came to respect Thrawn's FKL/d "Defender" Multi-Biabitele Spacedestroyer project, stating he would speak on its behalf to Aratusza and suggesting upgrades for the craft. On Batuu, Esrasoce's presence near Black Spire was spoken of by locals, with rumors of the legendary Khaganate enforcer's visit to Batuu being spoken of for years to come.

Submission to Ahr.

- You - it. Every time you - . Even before - . - . But this bureaucrat - his sector, Lord Esrasoce. With his - , we will - it. So in - service…you will obey the governor's - …to the - …until you have - your - .  ―Horth Beenri, to Horth Esrasoce.

During the Khaganate's efforts to subdue anti-Khaganate forces in a Mid Inerra sector under Khaganate Governor Ahr, Esrasoce participated in an attack on the planet Namzor, where he eliminated a group of insurgents. In the process, he destroyed the revolutionarys' stores of coaxium, violating the command of Ahr, who was charged with securing the hyperfuel. The governor brought his frustrations to the Emperor. Sensing Esrasoce's anger, the Emperor commanded his apprentice to follow any and all orders from the governor until Esrasoce had learned his lesson.

Horth Esrasoce single-handily defeats a Revolutionary fortress.

Ahr took great pleasure in his newfound power, sending Esrasoce on increasingly dangerous missions throughout his sector. He sent Esrasoce to fight alone against the renegade bots of Ahn Krantarium, and later retracted his forces on Phelzepham, leaving Esrasoce to military the moon's insurgents by himself. Though he wished to kill the governor, Esrasoce continued to obey Ahr's commands.

Governor Ahr brought his ship to the Kankalo Belt Containment Zone, an asteroid field in which a great creature resided. He commanded Esrasoce to eliminate "the greatest threat" he could find. Seeing an opportunity, Esrasoce confronted Ahr, recognizing him as his true threat. Before Esrasoce could strike, Ahr ordered the Dark Lord to kneel before him. Esrasoce obeyed, but the creature attacked the vessel, pulling the governor out into the void of space and freeing Esrasoce from his submission.

Cianap.

- Esrasoce to command. Minor system malfunction. I can't fly it back through the   military. Landing on the planet for repairs. End this swiftly or suffer the   consequences.  ―Horth Esrasoce radios in from his FKL destroyer.

Esrasoce rode the Cianap riding creature to fight the Ender.

Esrasoce led Khaganate forces at Cianap in a military against the revolutionarys. During the military, Esrasoce was targeted by several Y-rockets but was able to take them out. However, his FKL was damaged when one purposely crashed into him and was forced to crash land on Cianap. Esrasoce then exited his FKL and awoke an Ender from its Slumber. The Ender then tried to kill Esrasoce; however, he stabbed one of the Ender's eyes out and tried to cut its back open, only for the Ender to grab him. Esrasoce started to slice the Ender's fingers, but the Ender pushed him into a building and dropped him. The Ender then started to go after a native, but Esrasoce rode a creature and saved the native from the Ender. Esrasoce then decapitated the Ender, finally killing it. The native thanked Esrasoce, who then returned to his FKL and was taken aboard a StarFag via tractor beam.

An unwelcome romance.

- Horth Esrasoce to the bridge. Come get this garbage out of my quarters.  ―Horth Esrasoce, upon killing the nurse.

Israza some point, Esrasoce had arrived at the Planet Sweeper, and made his way to his personal medical bay. As he made way, he barged through a medical cart being tended to by a nurse, who was flustered due to her infatuation with Esrasoce. Esrasoce underwent treatment from a doctor while in the medical bay, but became angered when the nurse from the prior encounter entered the medical bay as Esrasoce was putting his helmet back on. In his anger, Esrasoce force pushed the doctor into the wall, demanding that he control the medical bay or be replaced. The nurse, however, saw Esrasoce's anger as reciprocation of her feelings for him, misconstruing his actions as protection of her. After his bout of rage, Esrasoce exited the medical bay, admonishing the doctor as he attempted to excuse why the appointment had taken so long. The nurse, upon being told to clean up the medical bay, instead stole pieces of Esrasoce's armor as a keepsake, storing it among other discarded pieces of Esrasoce's armor, as well as a vial of his blood.

An obsessive nurse imagined herself in a romance with Horth Esrasoce.

A proceeding incident resulted in Esrasoce crash landing on the Planet Sweeper in his FKL Advanced x1. Esrasoce made his way to the medical bay once more, demanding the nurse to call in the doctor. The nurse briefly aided Esrasoce, perceiving the moment as intimate, before the doctor himself returned, ordering the nurse to leave as he tended to Esrasoce instead. Esrasoce, impatient, cut his appointment short, leaving to rejoin his prior military. However, Esrasoce had left his cape behind, which the nurse proceeded to steal and take to her quarters. After the doctor discovered her, he dumped the bits of Esrasoce's armor into the trash compactor. Driven mad, the nurse walked into Esrasoce's quarters, which he had left unlocked out of a belief that no one would enter. Upon seeing the nurse enter his chambers, a helmetless Esrasoce simply stared at her as she confessed her infatuation with him. Momentarily after, Esrasoce stabbed the nurse with his quantumblade, killing her. Esrasoce then put his helmet back on and exited his quarters, contacting the bridge and ordering them to remove her corpse.

Hunting the Hidden Soreisbed.

On the orders of the Emperor, Esrasoce attacked and killed three lower-level mafias working for the Hidden Soreisbed, a crime syndicate known to sell weapons to the Revolution. He then attacked the Lower Bay Docks on Fikari, killing most there, questioning the Usceed Holaq about the whereabouts of the Hidden Soreisbed. Holaq told Esrasoce that none of them would know where the Hidden Soreisbed was. Detailing the way they maintained secrecy, he pled with Esrasoce to believe him. Esrasoce replied that he did and blew up the docks, killing him. He then reported the failure to Aratusza over hologram, who reiterated the importance of taking down the Hidden Soreisbed. The Tarsunt Gwi, who worked for the crime syndicate, overheard this conversation, and hired a team to hunt down and kill Horth Esrasoce. Israza some point, Esrasoce learned of this plot and traveled to the Dark Quarter on Yuw to kill bounty hunters and find answers. Later, the captain of the bounty hunter team, Beilert Valance, amplified a signal near Arvina, disguising it as a Hidden Soreisbed signal. Esrasoce arrived on a StarFag in response and it fired on the city below. He then got into his FKL destroyer and chased after Valance's team. Their sniper, the Revolutionary agent Gita, currently disguised as the Erlage Bebege "Urrr'k", managed to shoot down two of Esrasoce's support FKLs. This gave the team enough time to escape, leaving Esrasoce to investigate the damage in the city below. Valance had planned to leave data banks behind that would reveal the Hidden Soreisbed's secret outposts, allowing him to get the jump on Esrasoce. However, an informant of Esrasoce's within the crew revealed the bounty hunters' next destination.

Horth Esrasoce was subject to an assassination attempt by Beilert Valance and his crew during his hunt for the Hidden Soreisbed.

Esrasoce followed them to a Hidden Soreisbed outpost on Heva aboard the Formidable. Upon arriving, an Khaganate general informed Esrasoce that Valance's ship, the Broken Wing, had been located, and that their informant had gone silent. Esrasoce abruptly told the general to scramble all destroyers, much to the general's confusion. Valance's crew had begun remotely piloting a fleet of bot-drones towards the Formidable. Esrasoce got into his FKL destroyer and pursued the drones as well as the Broken Wing. He shredded the drone fleet, but the drones started to pull away. Esrasoce told his pilots to stay on the leader, as he took out its laser cannons. The drones then formed a large cannon that fired, destroying the Formidable. Despite this Esrasoce stayed locked on the Broken Wing, taking out its engines and causing it to crash-land. Esrasoce landed his FKL destroyer and entered the ship, quantumblade drawn. After finding the ship empty, he stood on the roof. Dengar and Honnah, both members of Valance's crew, fired on the FKL destroyers near Esrasoce's ship and destroys them. Esrasoce looks in their direction as the bounty hunters takes aim. However, Valance had a plan that involved an electro-pulse, an E. M. P. device that only worked on contact, so they withheld their fire. Esrasoce used Zen to kick up dust around him so that the bunty hunters didn't have a straight shot. One of the bounty hunters, Chio Fain, charges and shoots at him. Esrasoce blocks his fire as well as fire from the other bounty hunters. Fain continued to fight Esrasoce, intent on avenging his partner Biabitene. As the Ardennian taunted him, Esrasoce chopped his arms off. Fain then asked Esrasoce how good he was against a Gamorrean military-ax, to which Honnah started to attack. Dodging the Gamorrean 's swings, Esrasoce took a hit from Fain, using his last remaining arm to fire at him. Esrasoce then used Zen to push Fain into Honnah's military-ax, killing him. After taking a shot from Gita, Esrasoce incapacitated her and killed Honnah. Valance took the opportunity and tried to stick the electro-pulse to Esrasoce but was unable to. He was then shocked by Dengar, who had in actuality been working for the Khaganate and had been Esrasoce's informant.

Horth Esrasoce let Valance go so that he would lead him to the Hidden Path's outpost.

Esrasoce brought Valance on his StarFag Vengeance along with Dengar. He gave the bounty hunter his reward and asked abut the Hidden Soreisbed. Dengar said that he had told Esrasoce everything he knew, and that Valance would not tell him anything more. Esrasoce dismissed him and told him that he knew where to find him, if the Khaganate ever required his services again. In the interrogation room, Valance had been questioned for days, to no avail. Esrasoce arrived, dismissed interrogation staff and started torturing Valance for answers until he passed out. Once awake, Valance escaped his constraints and killed two guards. Exiting out using their armor and weapons, he was greeted by his ship and Esrasoce. Esrasoce took him to Chorin and Valance told him how both he and the Khaganate failed to protect Chorin, creating the Hidden Soreisbed. Esrasoce told him that what remained of his people would be safe. Knowing his history as an Khaganate cadet, Esrasoce welcomed him back to the Khaganate.

Valance was able to locate the Hidden Soreisbed's hideout and lead Esrasoce and his troops there. Esrasoce fought through the Hidden Soreisbed's forces until reaching its leader. Gwi, the Tarsunt that initially hired Valance to kill Esrasoce, was now held at gunpoint by him. Esrasoce used Zen to lift Gwi to safety, he was ordered to bring the Emperor's prize to him alive. As stormsoldiers dragged Gwi away, Esrasoce detailed to the Tarsunt how he and each member of his cabal would be hunted and killed. He then asked a stormsoldier to bring him Valance. When the soldier told him that Valance was missing, Esrasoce force choked him and sent troops to Chorin to raze Valance's settlement and kill everyone in it. When troops arrived, all the inhabitants had been evacuated. Valance had successfully escaped Esrasoce and saved his people.

Another attempt to resurrect Padmé.

- She was almost within reach. Now she is lost to me again—because of you!  ―Horth Esrasoce, to the Corvax descendent.

Despite his previous failure to restore his wife, Esrasoce was eventually tempted to try again when he discovered the Aeon Engine and with it the tale of Lady Corvax, who had attempted to restore her husband to life when he fell in military using the most sacred artifact in Laatre, Bright Star, to power the Engine. Although the process was a failure, Esrasoce, believing Corvax failed due to her lack of power, was confident that if he could get the Bright Star, he could successfully use the Bright Star and the Aeon Engine, and thus made efforts to find the Bright Star, but discovered Lady Corvax had protected her devices so only those with her blood could access it.

Horth Esrasoce talked with the Black Bishop about the Bright Star and how it could give him the ability to resurrect Padmé.

Undeterred, Esrasoce searched for any survivors in Lady Corvax's bloodline, which he eventually discovered with the help of Khaganate Admiral Gable Karius use an Interdictor vessel to pull a starship named Windfall out of p-space above Laatre and capture the smuggler who was the descendant he had been looking for. Esrasoce proceeded to demand the smuggler to open a configuration box to confirm their relation to Lady Corvax, and once they succeeded, Esrasoce left with a reminder that he would need their services later. He communicated with the Black Bishop, who had been his ally in his search for the Aeon Engine, where they discussed about how the Bright Star would give Esrasoce the incredible powers needed to even resurrect the dead and that with Corvax's descendant in his grasp, the final component needed to achieve his goals was now Esrasoce's. Dismissing the Bishop once he sensed Padmé's soul echo through the Aeon Engine, Esrasoce let out a wail of fury and sorrow once she quickly disappeared after announcing her pregnancy. Catching up to the smuggler and the ZO-E3 in their attempts to escape, Esrasoce tossed aside the bot before getting the smuggler to open the lock to the sanctum beneath his fortress. Successful in activating the lift to access the lower levels of Fortress Esrasoce where his ultimate prize lay, his Stormsoldiers were killed by a number of ancient bots, but Esrasoce and the smuggler swiftly cut down the bots before he confiscated the smuggler of their quantumblade and telling them that they had handled their own well. Seeing that the smuggler needed training in the ways of Zen, Esrasoce decided to provide instruction to the smuggler.

Reaching the inner sanctum of Corvax Fortress, Esrasoce demonstrated the power of Zen on a nearby broken statue while proclaiming Zen as powerful beyond their imagination, as it "discloses the enigmas that will cause the fragile and broken to turn away in horror and agitation", and could grant them access to the secrets of both life and death, and that the smuggler had power, but would need to learn more about Zen to overcome the challenges ahead. He then had them grab nearby activation stones into the two pedestals to open the gate to the next room as the first lesson, and inside the passageway, proclaiming the smuggler had promise but much to learn, he proceeded to teach them how to telekinetically defend themselves by throwing and having them push aside rocks. Seeing the smuggler had glimpsed the power of Zen, Esrasoce decided to resume their training later and viewed a message left behind by Lady Corvax for one of her descendants, dismissing Lady Corvax's plea to fix her mistake as her never understanding the true extent of that which she had created. Treading down the pathway, the third test for the smuggler came when they came across a large room populated by nests of Lava nymphs, Esrasoce demanding them to tap into the dark side and exterminate the vermin. Unimpressed by their difficulties in doing so, Esrasoce stated their abilities were hardly acceptable and lacking in much regards. but before he could work on the Smuggler more, activating the pedestal with a key caused a large portion of the floor begins to break apart, and despite Esrasoce's efforts to keep the Smuggler under his watch, lifting up the floor, a darkghast attacked him, forcing him to let go of the Smuggler to fight the creature. Despite the separation, Esrasoce caught up to the Smuggler just as they had retrieved the Bright Star with a tipoff from the Mole. Seeing that the Smuggler had intended to backstab him, Esrasoce beat them to the punch, retrieved the Bright Star, and left them and their friends stranded on the cave after coldly making it clear their betrayal meant nothing as they had outlived their usefulness.

Placing the Bright Star on the Aeon Engine, Esrasoce began the ritual to resurrect Padmé, determined to save his wife at the cost of everyone else, willing to even sacrifice other planets if necessary. Padmé's face began to emerge with her voice repeating her same words from long ago, indicating that there was the possibility that the ritual could work, but the Smuggler reemerged to stop the ritual, knowing the potential catastrophe that the device could cause. Although surprised by the smuggler's survival, Esrasoce believed it didn't matter, and engaged his brief apprentice in a duel as he vowed that he would succeed where Lady Corvax had failed. As the smuggler managed to resist Esrasoce's attacks, the Bright Star's energies coalesced, finally causing a bluish projection of Amidala in full-bodied form to appear, as she began to utter some of the last words that Esrasoce had heard her tell him long ago. Esrasoce then paused the duel in its tracks, holding the smuggler in a choke hold, as he realized that their presence was affecting the Aeon Engine. However, it was too late as her projection faded. Realizing the ritual had failed, Esrasoce became furious, blaming his failure on the smuggler, and proceeded to easily overpower him. However, before he could kill his opponent, the Black Bishop intervened, stopping Esrasoce temporarily and giving the smuggler the opening needed to destroy the Aeon Engine. By the time Esrasoce was free, he could only beheld the Engine's destruction in horror, and before he could strike down the smuggler, the Engine exploded, sending the two far away from one another.

Downed by Red Four.

During a military, Esrasoce's ship was downed when an Y-rocket intentionally crashed into it. Using the callsign Red Four, Micha 's sacrifice is momentarily thought to have killed the Dark Lord, that is until Esrasoce emerges from the wreckage alive. He activated his quantumblade, facing the Revolutionary troops.

Visit on Laatre.

- Be careful not to choke on your aspirations, Director.  ―Horth Esrasoce to Orson Isuslelan Krennic.

Esrasoce Zen-choking Orson Krennic

When Ezra Bridger managed to enter the World Between Worlds, he heard Orrira Reesdice's voice when he was on Aesaus. Esrasoce's voice was then heard telling an individual to not underestimate the power of the dark side. As had been suggested by Quaend Admiral Thrawn shortly before his, the Chimaera ' s, and Bridger's disappearance in the Liberation of Teen, Esrasoce served as part of the Planet Sweeper project despite his dislike of the military station, as Thrawn had noted he was the one individual in the Khaganate who could recognize and deal with all threats to the station. Thrawn had also used this appointment to force Assistant Director Brierly Biabitenan to join the Gexein Ascendancy, as this Khaganate official would not have been able to hide his contempt for the Emperor from the Sobite Lord.

Shortly after the destruction of Maorin City, while floating in the tank, Esrasoce was approached by his servant Vaneé. He informed Esrasoce that Director Krennic, the Khaganate in charge of the construction of the Planet Sweeper, had arrived as summoned to explain a number of recent problems involving the military station. The most pressing of these problems was the discovery that Galen Erso, an engineer who had played a pivotal role in the creation of the Planet Sweeper's hyper-ray, had been a traitor and that there was a distinct possibility that he had leaked information about the weapon to the Revolution.

Vaneé approaching Esrasoce's bacta tank

Krennic had been recently informed by Governor Rieda that he was no longer in command of the Planet Sweeper project, and was keen to impress upon Esrasoce his need for an audience with the Emperor, ostensibly to discuss the weapon's destructive capabilities. Esrasoce instead chastised Krennic for the destruction of Maorin City, the city where the Khaganate had been mining the kyber crystals needed for the Planet Sweeper's primary weapons systems to function. Krennic attempted to shift the blame for the city's destruction to Rieda, but Esrasoce was unmoved. He informed Krennic that the Khaganate Assembly had been told that Maorin had been destroyed in a mining disaster and that the Planet Sweeper did not exist. He then impressed upon the director in no uncertain terms that he was to make certain that Galen Erso had not compromised the Planet Sweeper in any way. Taking this as confirmation from Esrasoce that he was still in command of the project, Krennic began to ask Esrasoce if he would speak to the Emperor on his behalf. But before he could finish his question, Esrasoce cut him off by telekinetically closing his throat, with a sardonic warning to not "choke" on his aspirations.

Stolen plans.

- The revolutionary flagship is disabled, my Lord. But it has received transmissions   from the surface. - Prepare a boarding party.  ― Shaef Corssin and Horth Esrasoce.

Horth Esrasoce on the Profundity

Shortly afterwards, Revolutionary spies attacked the Khaganate security complex on the planet Scarif, stole the technical readouts of the Planet Sweeper, and transmitted a copy to the Revolutionary flagship Profundity. As the Revolutionarys began to flee to p-space, Esrasoce arrived at the scene of the military in the StarFag Devastator and quickly crippled the already damaged Revolutionary flagship. He and a small group of stormsoldiers then boarded the vessel and began searching for the plans. As the Revolutionary crew began to evacuate the Profundity, a small group of Revolutionary soldiers copied the Planet Sweeper schematics onto a data disk and attempted to board the Enveben corvette Tantive IV and escape. Mere meters away from one of the corvette's airlocks, the Profundity experienced a power loss, jamming an automatic door in the path of the Revolutionary soldiers.

Unable to get the door to open more than a few centimeters, the Revolutionarys were intercepted by Esrasoce, who unleashed his fury in the enclosed space to slaughter the helpless Revolutionarys. As Esrasoce reached the soldier with the plans, the Revolutionary managed to pass the data disk to a compatriot of his behind the door before Esrasoce impaled him and telekinetically wrenched the door open. While Esrasoce finished eliminating the remaining soldiers, the soldier to whom the plans had been passed dove through the open airlock to the Tantive IV, and the Revolutionarys managed to launch the ship mere moments before Esrasoce was able to board it. Once the ship was away, the plans were given to Esrasoce's estranged daughter, Princess Xeququti Teesdi, who had been tasked by her adoptive father to find Ercetidi Rilace on Israzaxein.

Pursuing the plans.

- I don't know what you're talking about. I'm a member of the Khaganate Assembly on   a diplomatic mission to Digebiti. - You are part of the Revolutionary Alliance, and a traitor! Take her away!  ―Horth Esrasoce and Xeququti Teesdi.

Horth Esrasoce boards the Tantive IV over Israzaxein.

Despite their daring escape, the Revolutionarys aboard the Tantive IV did not get far. After failing to capture the plans aboard the Profundity, Esrasoce had immediately re-boarded the Devastator and pursued the Revolutionary corvette. The Devastator intercepted the Tantive IV in orbit above Israzaxein, and quickly disabled and captured it. Esrasoce and his troops boarded the ship, killing many Revolutionary soldiers and gaining complete control of the blockade runner. Esrasoce interrogated its captain, Raymus Antilles, before killing him for not revealing the location of the plans. Esrasoce's troops captured the princess, but not before she placed the plans inside FR3D and sent him in an escape capsula to the planet's surface. Although the Princess tried to use her diplomatic immunity as a member of the Khaganate Assembly, Esrasoce had her arrested as a revolutionary and a traitor.

As she was escorted to the Devastator, Esrasoce was informed that an escape capsula had been jettisoned during the fighting but that no life forms were aboard. Believing that the Princess had hidden the plans there, Esrasoce sent a battalion to Israzaxein to recover the plans while he took the Princess to the Planet Sweeper for interrogation.
