In p-space, Esrasoce would catch Teesdi when she had escaped her guards and made her way to one of the Devastator ' s shuttles, and announced that the Emperor was disbanding the Assembly as they spoke, leaving her defenseless. Esrasoce then had her follow him to the ship's bridge shortly before arriving at the Planet Sweeper, where he had her sent to her cell in Detention Block AA-23.

Meanwhile, Esrasoce accompanied Rieda to attend a meeting with the Joint Chiefs, including General Cassio Tagge and Admiral Conan Antonio Motti, who mocked Esrasoce's reliance on an "ancient religion, " only to have Esrasoce Zen-choke him. Before he could kill him, Rieda intervened and instructed Esrasoce to find the location of the Revolutionary base from Teesdi. Subsequently, Esrasoce went to torture her with a mind probe -equipped interrogator bot, but as she resisted, he and Rieda turned to coercion—by bringing the Planet Sweeper to the Digebiti system, they threatened to destroy her homeworld if she refused to disclose the location of the Revolutionary base. Eventually, she told him that the Revolutionarys were on Dantooine, yet Rieda ordered Digebiti to be destroyed anyway, to make an effective demonstration of the Planet Sweeper's power. Esrasoce restrained the Princess as the Planet Sweeper's hyper-ray destroyed her planet. Esrasoce then took Teesdi back to her cell to await her execution.

Enter Zaerla Reesdice.

Duel on the Planet Sweeper.

- I've been waiting for you, Ercetidi. We meet again at last. The circle is now   complete. When I left you, I was but a learner. Now - am the Master. - Only a master of evil, - .  ―Horth Esrasoce and Ercetidi Rilace, before their fourth and final duel.

After the scout ships sent to Dantooine found the base abandoned, Rieda ordered Esrasoce to kill her immediately, but the Dark Eagle was captured in the ruins of Digebiti by the Planet Sweeper's tractor beam. Identified as a suspicious ship from Diormaen Eisley, Esrasoce concluded that they were trying to return the stolen plans to the Princess and went to inspect the freighter himself. Told that the crew had apparently abandoned ship after take-off, Esrasoce gave orders for the ship to be scanned to be sure. Israza the same time, Esrasoce sensed the presence of his old master, Ercetidi Rilace. When news reached him that the Princess had been released from her cell, Esrasoce decided to confront Rilace alone.

Esrasoce strikes down his former master and friend, Rilace.

Esrasoce found Rilace after he had deactivated a tractor beam terminal to allow the Falcon escape the military station, and the two engaged in one final duel. During the fight, Esrasoce noted that Rilace's age had made him weak. However, Rilace told his former apprentice that he would not win because he would become more powerful than Esrasoce could ever imagine, even if he managed to kill him. As they fought, the two reached Bay 327 just as Rilace's companions and the Princess reunited to board the Falcon, only for Rilace to see his latest apprentice, Zaerla Reesdice, Esrasoce's son.

As his former master gave him a mysteriously knowing smile, Esrasoce struck him down when Rilace raised his blade in surrender. Much to Esrasoce's surprise, Rilace's body disappeared, for his spirit had become one with Zen, and Esrasoce tried to find remains in his robes but found none. As he did so, General Moradmin Bast asked who he was, and Esrasoce answered "An old man who thought he could help gifted children. He was mistaken. " Esrasoce then tried to stop the Revolutionarys from escaping but was prevented from doing so by the closing of the hangar doors by Zaerla's shot. After the Falcon ' s escape, Esrasoce met with Rieda and confirmed that a homing beacon had been placed upon the ship and that they could follow the ship to the Revolutionary base.

Rilace's warning to Esrasoce came true; his consciousness survived his death, and he became a Zen spirit. Meanwhile, still remaining hidden on Laeser, Diora felt Rilace and Esrasoce's duel through Zen, especially noticing, for the first time, the loneliness in the man who had once been Orrira Reesdice. Diora noticed that Esrasoce's loneliness only grew after he struck down his former mentor, which nearly made Diora pity the former Bioris and wish he could counsel him. As he continued his journey, Diora reflected on his past, blaming himself for not noticing Reesdice's path to the dark side.

Anesce 4.

- Zen is strong with this one.  ―Horth Esrasoce, about Zaerla Reesdice during the Military of Anesce.

Esrasoce's plan worked and the Planet Sweeper followed the Falcon to the hidden Revolutionary base on Anesce 4. In the Planet Sweeper's conference room, Esrasoce awoke Rieda from a vision about his father just as the military station approached Anesce Prime, with an officer informing them that Anesce 4 was located on the far-side of the planet. As the Planet Sweeper prepared to destroy the moon, Revolutionary Y-rockets and Y-wings spacedestroyers attacked the Planet Sweeper, having identified a weakness: a thermal exhaust port which led to the reactor systems; a direct hit on the port would cause a chain reaction and the destruction of the station. Although Rieda remained convinced in the station's invulnerability, Esrasoce was informed by Lieutenant Tanbris that the thirty attacking Revolutionary spacedestroyers were small enough to avoid the turbolaser defenses. As such, Esrasoce ordered destroyers to launch. When Gold Squadron broke off from the main group and begun a trench run towards the exhaust port, Esrasoce boarded his FKL Advanced and, flanked by two FKL destroyers, managed to terminate three of the last four Gold Squadron pilots. He continued to destroy most ships of Red Squadron, killing Red Leader Garven Dreis.

Esrasoce in his FKL Advanced x1 during the Military of Anesce

As the last members of Red Squadron engaged in a trench run while the Planet Sweeper was preparing the destroy the moon, Esrasoce damaged Wedge Antilles 's Y-rocket, forcing him to break off formation, and killed Biggs Darklighter, leaving only the leader of the group, Zaerla. Esrasoce quickly realized that Zen was powerful in the young pilot, but nevertheless proceeded to get him into range. When he finally succeeded, he was just about to destroy the young pilot when the newly arrived Dark Eagle intervened and destroyed one of Esrasoce's escorts. Before the Falcon could destroy Esrasoce, his other escort accidentally bumped into Esrasoce's craft, destroying the other escort and causing Esrasoce's craft to veer off course and send him plunging into space. This prevented him from stopping the Planet Sweeper's destruction, which killed Rieda and was a major defeat for the Khaganate.

Esrasoce is picked up by Ciena Ree and Berisse Sai.

Shortly after the military, Esrasoce sent a distress signal to the Devastator to send a ship to pick him up and bring him back from the Anesce system to the StarFag. Sometime later, he would fly just beyond the ever-expanding debris field that once was the Planet Sweeper, when Khaganate pilots Ciena Ree and Berisse Sai arrived in a Gozanti -class liner. Docking his FKL Advanced x1 with the freighter, Esrasoce passed through the airlock door and met Ree. Then, he ordered Ree and Sai to remain in the hold for the remainder of the voyage while he took command of the freighter until they returned to the Devastator. As the revolutionarys quickly abandoned their base in the wake of the military, Esrasoce joined the Khaganate retaliatory attack, but the revolutionarys were largely able to escape. Esrasoce ordered for a blockade of the Anesce system to begin at one point.

Post-Anesce ventures.

Sometime later, an Khaganate officer managed to obtain one of two quantumblades formerly owned by the Sobite Horth Israzarius. Esrasoce arrived on Hradreek, where the officer welcomed him, supported by a contingent of stormsoldiers. Esrasoce looked at the quantumblade and asked where the other one was. The officer was confused by his question, and Esrasoce Zen-choked him. As he gagged, he claimed that he bought the quantumblade from a smuggler and that she must have tricked him. Esrasoce told a stormsoldier to divert all divisions to finding the smuggler and the other quantumblade. Esrasoce then killed the officer as the stormsoldiers scattered to begin the search.

A squad of stormsoldiers found the smuggler, Sana Starros, and they chased her down the streets. Starros paid a gang of Esbiza to stall them, and they got in the way of the stormsoldiers. However, Esrasoce appeared and cut them down using his quantumblade and Horth Israzarius'. Esrasoce then followed Starros' path to a cargo bay, and he used Zen to move the crates and block her from a clear exit. Starros then shot at Esrasoce, and he used Zen to disperse the plasma bolts. Starros rigged one of the crates and then waited for Esrasoce to follow before detonating it, throwing Esrasoce to the side and burying him. Esrasoce soon freed himself and made his way to the nearby podracing track. Esrasoce entered the seating area, and the audience scattered at his presence. Esrasoce noticed one of the chordfliers, who was grabbing the attention of the commentary. The human, despite being biologically unequipped for a race like this, was winning the race, and Esrasoce used Zen to cause one of the engines to malfunction, crashing the chordflier. The pilot of the racer was none other than Zaerla Reesdice, who had obtained the other of Israzarius' quantumblades. Zaerla decided to destroy the quantumblade, as it overwhelmed him with anger whenever he needed it. Esrasoce watched him escape in his Y-rocket, and he decided to crush Israzarius' other quantumblade.

According to a tale later told by Vaneé, at another point after the Military of Anesce, Esrasoce's StarFag was contacted by Cranwell, the assistant to Rieda Initiative scientist Restin, to warn them that Doctor Restin had betrayed the Khaganate. Restin, who wanted a weapon to use against Esrasoce as revenge for the destruction of Digebiti, had created a genetic serum that would turn those injected with it into rampaging Gamorreans, who could then spread the infection by touch. Esrasoce decided to deal with Restin himself, so he arrived at Restin's base of operations with two stormsoldiers and pretended to not know he was a traitor. After witnessing the doctor turn Cranwell into a Gamorrean with the serum, Esrasoce suggested the serum could be used against the Revolution. When Restin turned the infected Gamorrean against the Sobite Lord, he effortlessly lifted it and his infected stormsoldiers into the air with Zen. He did the same with Restin, forcing the scientist to confess his treachery before revealing he had already known he was a traitor. Intending to be a firsthand witness to his destruction, Esrasoce forced the three Gamorreans and Restin to touch, transforming the doctor into one of his creations. Leaving the infected scientist to the mercy of the Gamorreans, Esrasoce left aboard his Lambda -class shuttle. He had never feared Restin's experiments, as Esrasoce already viewed himself as a different kind of monster.

Cymoon 1.

- He's here. The Revolutionary pilot who destroyed the Planet Sweeper. I can feel his   presence. He will not escape me again.  ―Horth Esrasoce.

Several weeks after the Military of Anesce, Horth Esrasoce was called to Weapons Factory Alpha on Cymoon 1. He was to negotiate, or rather force an arrangement with an envoy of Reenus the Texebi about supplying raw materials through Texebi- controlled space to the factory. Esrasoce arrived shortly after the envoy (secretly the Dark Eagle ' s captain, Soreisbe Eszara ) and landed on a platform. As he was about to enter the facility, Esrasoce was shot at by a sniper, Ararus, but he deflected the raygun bolt and used two stormsoldiers as human shields as the Tivere rained down dozens of shots upon him. Unscathed, Esrasoce located Ararus's position and used to Zen to collapse his vantage point, but the Tivere survived.

Esrasoce easily bests Zaerla Reesdice in combat.

Esrasoce then ordered the arriving stormsoldier reinforcements to hunt him down. One of them informed the Dark Lord that Overseer Aggadeen was out of contact and the factory's main reactor was on meltdown. As he surmised these occurrences were the work of Revolutionarys, Esrasoce put the entire moon on alert but also felt the presence of the pilot who had destroyed the Planet Sweeper, Zaerla Reesdice. Although unaware of the boy's identity, Esrasoce made his way into the factory at once and came face-to-face with the Bioris apprentice. Esrasoce soon realized that the boy had been cursorily trained by Rilace, and after the briefest of duels, snatched Zaerla's quantumblade. As he prepared to execute Zaerla for his refusal to disclose information about the Revolution, he realized that the boy's quantumblade had once been his own. Before he could question Zaerla further, the foot of a Revolutionary- hijacked ST-ARM walker came hurtling down into the middle of their standoff, knocking the two apart.

In the ensuing chaos, Esrasoce sought to prevent the Revolutionarys' escape, and mowed down the factory's runaway slaves, commanding his stormsoldiers to execute all the escapees. Once Zaerla got away on a stolen 74-Z speeder bike and attacked Esrasoce's troops (leading the Dark Lord to reconsider his initial dismissal of the boy and wonder exactly what Rilace had been up to before his death), Esrasoce personally saw to the walker's defeat. Eszara and Teesdi, commanding the ST-ARM, tried to step on him, but Esrasoce used Zen to hold the walker's descending foot several meters above his head, and almost tore it apart.

Esrasoce brings the ST-ARM to its knees.

He was foiled, however, when Eszara and Teesdi unleashed a barrage of the ST-ARM's laser fire upon him, causing him to lose his old quantumblade (which the current owner retrieved), and his mask and helmet. As he emerged from under a pile of rubble, a stormsoldier approached; upon seeing Esrasoce's scarred face, they tried to apologize. However, Esrasoce killed them by twisting their head around using Zen, right before Aggadeen reached him via comlink and asked for the facility's evacuation. Esrasoce vetoed his request, had him repair the core, and ordered to kill all the Revolutionary invaders, except for the boy, whom Esrasoce would deal with personally. As Aggadeen informed him that they had successfully defused the reactor, Esrasoce personally felled the Revolutionary walker by slashing its legs with his quantumblade.

As reinforcements arrived, Esrasoce sent them after the Revolutionarys on the trash fields, while he pursued the lone Revolutionary who was returning to the factory, Zaerla. Aboard a combat speeder, Esrasoce chased Zaerla into the facility, but the Revolutionary managed to destroy the power core with the speeder bike, leaving Esrasoce as the only survivor among the factory's rubble following its explosion. The Sobite Lord then saw Zaerla's abandoned speeder and the fleeing Dark Eagle ; as the Adjudicator ' s captain, Kronn, informed him that they had been unable to catch the freighter, Esrasoce Zen-choked the captain. Then, as if he were talking to Rilace, Esrasoce vowed to find the boy's identity and to turn him to the dark side, so that the boy became his weapon, and not Rilace's.

Anesce's aftermath.

- But you are the only one living to suffer my anger. You, an isolated survivor   of the greatest military disaster in all my Khaganate's history? Oh, you are   truly the Chosen One, Lord Esrasoce. Chosen to be the one responsible. - I will make it right. I will crush the Revolutionarys. - No. I think not.  ―Beenri and Esrasoce discuss Esrasoce's failings.

Thereupon, Esrasoce returned to the Khaganate Palace on Larais to report to the Emperor, bringing Aggadeen with him. The Emperor bid Esrasoce to explain the events of Weapons Factory Alpha, which along with the Planet Sweeper's destruction, had put the Khaganate very near disaster. Although Esrasoce considered Aggadeen to be responsible for the failure on Cymoon 1 (and the Overseer was tortured accordingly), the Emperor claimed much of the responsibility lay in Esrasoce's head, as he reminded him that he was the sole survivor of the greatest disaster in the Khaganate's history—one partially caused by his idea to tag the Falcon with a homing beacon and let it escape with the Planet Sweeper plans. He then informed Esrasoce that Cassio Tagge, now Quaend General of the Khaganate Army, would be his commanding officer for some time.

Esrasoce angrily leaves his master and the agent, Cylo-IV.

The Emperor then ordered Esrasoce to continue the unfinished Cymoon 1 negotiations with Reenus the Texebi on Israzaxein, right before going to Tagge. As they returned to his office, Beenri greeted one of his agents, Cylo, and Esrasoce asked for his identity, but the Emperor denied him the knowledge. Instead, he asked Esrasoce if there was anything else to report, but Esrasoce chose not to tell him about Rilace's mysterious disappearance, nor Zen-sensitive Revolutionary pilot who had destroyed the Planet Sweeper and left the office and palace altogether. He boarded an Khaganate StarFag and immediately left for Israzaxein, sending the Khaganate vessel back to collect the trade goods for the meeting.

Israzaxein negotiations.

- Hmmm. I see you know enough to tread carefully. But tell me... are you brave   or foolish. - That is not the question, Texebi. The question is: Are you? - Oh, Bioris... always making everything so difficult.  ―Reenus the Texebi and Horth Esrasoce.

Esrasoce soon arrived at Reenus's Palace where, during the meeting, he had the Texebi dismiss his court. However, Reenus would rather claim the bounty on Esrasoce's head and had his people shoot at him, but Esrasoce killed them and forced Reenus to reconsider his position. After being Zen choked, the Texebi admitted he found it hard not to respect him. The two then came to an agreement: Reenus would provide Esrasoce with two of his best bounty hunters. Esrasoce left the Texebi's palace and sought out a Erlage Bebege camp, wherein he mercilessly slaughtered its inhabitants. He was then approached by Reenus's envoys: Cevege Eninte and Krrsantan. While he sent Eninte to find and capture the Revolutionary pilot from Anesce alive, Esrasoce assigned the Tivere to bring Aratusza's agent to him. Both mercenaries agreed, and Esrasoce departed Israzaxein for his StarFag.

Esrasoce and Reenus on the latter's sail barge

The following day, Esrasoce returned in his official capacity as the Emperor's envoy to negotiate as if nothing had happened the day before. Reenus welcomed Esrasoce and claimed that his resources belonged to the Khaganate as long as he was paid. Esrasoce then turned to leave, leaving his stormsoldiers to tell Reenus what was required, but the Texebi invited him to a feast and entertainment in his honor, for he liked to seal his business ventures by watching someone die. Aboard the sail barge Khetanna, Esrasoce and Reenus watched as the latter's guards killed wild banthas for the sport until Reenus's court jester, Salacious B. Crumb, let out a loud cackle. Esrasoce warned the Texebi to not let the Kowakian monkey-lizard do that again in his presence if he valued Crumb's life; Reenus merely laughed and told Esrasoce he now regarded him as a "good friend. " The Texebi then inquired whether his bounty hunters were adequately serving his needs; though Esrasoce berated him for bringing up the private matter, he also told the Texebi that their missions were simple enough: to capture an Khaganate agent and a boy. Reenus argued that the "boy" had destroyed the Planet Sweeper, and had possibly been trained by Rilace. The name prompted Esrasoce to request that Reenus tell him all he knew of Rilace's exile on Israzaxein.

Reenus first required someone screaming, though, so he ordered his guards to pilot the Khetanna to the Great Pit of Carkoon. He expected Esrasoce to be impressed by the sarlacc, which he believed was one of the few treasures that Israzaxein had to offer, and said he found it hard to believe that anyone of note (like the Anesce pilot) could have ever been born on Israzaxein. In silence, Esrasoce watched his homeworld's sunset.

Servitude unto Tagge.

- You should think of yourself as your quantumblade. A singular weapon from an   older time—and one that is dangerous to everyone around them without a skilled   hand to wield it. In time you will understand that I am the skilled hand   you've been waiting for.  ―Cassio Tagge to Horth Esrasoce.

Esrasoce, under Cassio Tagge's administration

After leaving Israzaxein, Esrasoce met with Tagge aboard his Executor -class Star Dreadnought, Annihilator. During a time of many raids against ships carrying Reenus's weapons and equipment supplies, Esrasoce was sent to protect an Khaganate shuttle, CZ-246, from a pirate attack. With his FKL Advanced, Esrasoce went to their aid, alongside Black Two and Black Three, and successfully disabled the automated CR90 corvette attacking the shuttle. Upon returning to the Annihilator, Esrasoce reported to Tagge that the pirate vessel had been captured and that he suspected a security breach.

As he said so, Tagge disagreed, believing the raids to be predictable piracy, and admitted to seeing the Planet Sweeper as "Rieda's folly. " Esrasoce noted that Rieda at least had a vision, prompting Tagge to admit his plans were not as grand as Rieda's or Esrasoce's, but they still were effective; he believed that the Khaganate could not be based around a single asset, like the Planet Sweeper or Esrasoce himself. Tagge then informed Esrasoce that he intended to repair the corvette and use it to attack its base of origin, sending Esrasoce to head the attack, although with an adjutant, Lieutenant Oon-ai.

Esrasoce left to prepare for the mission and oversee the repairs on the corvette. Oon-ai followed, asking permission to look into Esrasoce's personal communications to provide information to Tagge. Although it annoyed Esrasoce, as it was the Emperor's command that he serve under Tagge, he allowed it and left. He then planted treasonable evidence into a black boardmech so as to incriminate Oon-ai during the mission. Soon thereafter, Esrasoce, Oon-ai, and six stormsoldiers boarded the corvette and found the pirate base, docking with the station. Greeted by a Onatzala pirate, whom he killed, Esrasoce ordered the soldiers to secure the core, while his boardmech made his way to the command center to plant the false evidence.

Esrasoce turns Oon-ai in.

They found themselves under the attack of two customized botekas who brandished missile launchers, prompting Esrasoce to use Zen to throw the incoming missiles back at the attackers, destroying the bots and fatally injuring the pirates. A Onmate Arorar pirate survived long enough to activate the self-destruct, and Esrasoce ordered the surviving soldiers to return to the corvette and prepare to launch as soon as he returned. Followed by Oon-ai, Esrasoce went deeper into the station to retrieve the false evidence. They returned to the corvette just before the station exploded.

Back on the Annihilator, Esrasoce reported to Tagge, telling him that he was impressed with the results of his plan (the intelligence he had gathered confirmed the raiders were being backed by the Crymorah Syndicate), but also claimed Oon-ai was the leak, noting that Tagge should be more careful of those who were close to him. Shortly after, Esrasoce isolated the black boardmech under the pretenses of a private discussion; the Dark Lord ensured the bot had deleted all records of its data uploads on the station, before venting the bot through an airlock and into space, where it exploded.

Following a Khaganate-Revolutionary confrontation, Esrasoce went to the Palace of Grakkus the Texebi to meet with Sergeant Kreel. A covert operative of his battalion "Esrasoce's Fist, " Kreel informed him that his mission to end Grakkus' operations was a success, but that the Anesce Revolutionary pilot had escaped them yet again. Esrasoce inquired if he had mentioned his name (which he did not), and asked him to tell him everything he had learned of the boy.

A bot army.

- There was a time I had armies at my beck and call. That time has passed. I   need resources of my own. Private resources.  ―Horth Esrasoce to Dr. Biceises, on why he needed her services.

From the information he gathered from the pirate base, Esrasoce learned that a Doctor Isanlequ Reerqu Biceises had supplied the pirates with the customized botekas. As he wanted similarly effective units in the personal army he sought to build, Esrasoce set out to find Biceises, conducting a manhunt for her in many locations, gathering information and killing the witnesses, until he finally learned of her presence on Quarantine World III in Kallidahin Space. He landed there just as Biceises was arrested by Utani Xane and a squad of B2 super military bots; although Xane warned him that he was violating treaties, Esrasoce began destroying her bots and saved Biceises as she tumbled over a ledge. Esrasoce stabbed Xane through the back with his saber before telling the doctor he needed her services. He helped her up and she agreed.

Biceises discovers Esrasoce is what she has unknowingly been searching for.

Together, they boarded Biceises's ship, the Ark Angel II, where she admitted to being a fan of his. The rogue archaeologist also introduced Esrasoce to the Triple- Zero secretary personality matrix and estimated it would take hours to unlock the matrix's codes that were restricting it. It took Esrasoce a few seconds to unlock it, which let Biceises install the personality matrix into a secretary bot's body right away. As the bot introduced himself as 0-0-0, she imprinted herself and Esrasoce as his masters, and used him to wake up BT-1, a blastomech prototype. But as it was hostile towards his new masters, 0-0-0 shut it down before he could do any harm.

Esrasoce then let her know the reason he had sought her out was to secure his own private resources, ones only she could help him procure. Biceises (who had originally acquired the two bots for the Gotra ) instead gave them to Esrasoce after realizing Esrasoce was probably going to be her employer for the foreseeable future. She asked what else he needed, and he answered that he wanted troops of unquestionable loyalty: military bots. Biceises then suggested that they acquire an unusual bot foundry on Inbeed, one in possession of Queen Karina, who had survived the Twin Wars and the planet's sterilization. As they landed the Ark Angel, and she sent 0-0-0 and BT-1 to map the cave route to the queen so that she and Esrasoce could follow, Biceises asked Esrasoce if he had ever been to Inbeed before. Her questioning brought up the memory of his first kiss with Amidala, shortly before the First Military of Inbeed.

Esrasoce cuts the Queen from her womb factory.

Esrasoce ordered Biceises to cease her probing, and entered the catacombs; she followed him. They reunited with BT-1 and 0-0-0 and encountered the queen. Esrasoce immediately amputated her from the womb bot factory. The queen, injured but alive, ordered her "children" to stop the intruders; at Esrasoce's command, BT-1 opened fire on the bots, and he and Esrasoce began to overwhelm her forces. Meanwhile, Biceises set a beacon on the ceiling with Esrasoce's help and ordered her Ark Angel to barrage the locator to make a hole in the roof. Esrasoce protected himself and Biceises by creating a Zen barrier. His J-Type 327 Nubian then lowered a crane-platform, which Biceises attached to the factory while Esrasoce fought off the advancing bots, and the ship carried them and the factory to the skies.

They would later board the Ark Angel, where Biceises successfully activated the factory and told him he would have his private bot army. She then asked Esrasoce if he would kill her then or later, and said that if he did, she wanted a quantumblade right through the neck, with no warning. Esrasoce finally told her she had proved resourceful and would be safe as long as he had use for her, unless she tried to blackmail him. It was then that 0-0-0 interrupted them to inform Esrasoce that he had a signal from Black Krrsantan: he was bringing the Emperor's agent to him.

Cylo's enforcers.

- Zen is obsolete. These are its successors. - This is... blasphemous! - This has nothing to do with Zen. Much like you, Lord Esrasoce. I look at   you, more machine than man, and I see a bridge between the old world and mine.   In many ways, these are your children. - Enough.  ―Cylo introduces Horth Esrasoce to his enforcers.

Esrasoce leads his bot army through Cylo's base

As Krrsantan arrived, Esrasoce confronted the Emperor's agent, Cylo-IV. Esrasoce demanded that he tell him his name, his commission from the Emperor and the location of his headquarters. As he refused, Esrasoce assigned 0-0-0 to retrieve all relevant information from him. After an interrogation that resulted in the agent's death, 0-0-0 reported to Esrasoce that he was Dr. Cylo-IV, that his research base was located in an Outer Inerra nebula, and that his commission was to create replacements for Esrasoce. After learning this, they immediately departed for the base, aboard his J-type 327 Nubian. While Biceises remained on the ship, Esrasoce and two platoons of bot commandos infiltrated it. Esrasoce sent a platoon to "the dojo" and had the other one follow him to the barracks. As they killed the soldiers in there, Biceises reported that they had lost the other platoon, prompting Esrasoce to go to the dojo, where he met the twins Morit and Aiolin Astarte.

They soon engaged in combat, but before any of the parties could harm each other, Cylo-V appeared and told the twins to stand down. He explained to Esrasoce why he was still alive after his last body was killed, and that he was not making apprentices for the Emperor but enforcers, as he believed Zen to be obsolete. Believing him to be blasphemous, Esrasoce attempted to strike Cylo down when the scientist claimed they were, in many ways, Esrasoce's children and successors. The Emperor then revealed his presence to Esrasoce and stopped him, claiming he wanted to see Cylo's demonstration without delay.

Esrasoce was immediately made to fight the Astarte twins, Tulon Voidgazer, the Onmate Arorar Karbin, and a Ersoonve. As neither party was injured, the Emperor ordered the fight to be one to the death. As such, Esrasoce fought the Ersoonve, but its ferocity threw him off balance, prompting Morit to intervene and kill his fellow enforcer. The Emperor then ordered the fighting to cease; Esrasoce warned Morit that his trespass would not be forgotten, as the Ersoonve's life was his to take. The Emperor then told the enforcers and Esrasoce that their mission was to strike down all who opposed the Khaganate and that they were not to slay each other. As per his master's orders, Esrasoce followed him.

Esrasoce stares down Cylo's enforcers as he follows his master.

In private, the Emperor, discovering the remains of some of Esrasoce's commando bots, admitted to being impressed that he had some independence left in him after so long and that perhaps he could triumph against the enforcers. Esrasoce criticized his dealings with Cylo, as he saw his enforcers as a heresy to Zen, but the Emperor berated him for his lack of respect—for it was Esrasoce who had disappointed him on Laatre and had forced him to contact Cylo. As he left, he observed that perhaps, Esrasoce would prove himself worthy of the name he had given him back then.

Parenthood.

- Did you bring me anything of value, bounty hunter? - Not much. Just his name. Reesdice.  ―Horth Esrasoce and Cevege Eninte.

Esrasoce, realizing he has a son, cracks the viewport in anger.

Troubled, Esrasoce returned to his ship. Aboard the Nubian, Biceises told him that Eninte wanted to report in during Esrasoce's absence. Subsequently, Esrasoce traveled to a StarFag to meet with the bounty hunter and hear his story. The Dark Lord was disappointed to hear that Eninte had lost the boy, but before leaving, Eninte told him his name: Zaerla Reesdice. In his solitude, Esrasoce remembered Amidala telling him about her pregnancy, and Aratusza's half-truth, and cracked the viewport in his anger. Thereupon, he used his faith room's holoprojector to contact his master, but chose to say nothing about his son; rather, Esrasoce assured him that he would not fail and ended the transmission. Nevertheless, he accepted Zaerla as his son and vowed that the boy would be his.

Determined to find more about Zaerla, Esrasoce returned to Israzaxein on the Ark Angel, with Biceises, 0-0-0 and BT-1 in his company. They landed at the Maenso homestead, which they discovered to be deserted after an attack by Khaganate troops, and Esrasoce tried to find traces of Zaerla's presence. Unable to find anything of importance, Esrasoce left the moisture farm and went to Rilace's home where he indeed felt the remnants of Zaerla's usage of Zen during his duel against Eninte, but nothing else. As they left, Biceises activated a molecular purge bomb to remove all remnants of their presence, and Esrasoce dismissed Biceises, for he was to return to his duties for the time being.

Anthan Prime.

- I'm trying to ascertain if you are... dangerous. - Oh, I am, Lord Esrasoce... But only to the enemies of the Khaganate.  ―Horth Esrasoce and Inspector Thanoth.

As part of Tagge's crackdown on Outer Inerra criminal interests who had gone unchecked in the past, Esrasoce took part in a mission to terminate a criminal organization known as Son-tuul Pride. He and his troops destroyed the Pride's base and seized its stockpile of credits, to be transported on an Khaganate light liner, but he also secretly sent Biceises all the information needed to steal the credits cargo. Following the theft, Esrasoce reunited with her on Anthan 13, where he sent her to confirm some information about Commodex Tahn.

Esrasoce, working alongside Inspector Thanoth

Esrasoce then attended a meeting between Tagge and Cylo's enforcers, wherein the Astarte twins were assigned to deal with the Plasma Devils, and wherein Esrasoce let Tagge know he wanted the mission of finding the Anesce Revolutionary pilot (which was Karbin's mission). Tagge felt he was too personally invested, and sent Esrasoce to punish whoever was responsible for the theft of the Son-tuul Pride's fortune in the Anthan system ; he also informed Esrasoce that Inspector Thanoth was his replacement adjutant. While Thanoth went to the Anthan Prime Orbital Dockyard to investigate the theft's circumstances, Esrasoce returned to Anthan 13 and killed the entirety of a small Revolutionary cell to cover his tracks. He later reunited with Thanoth on Anthan Prime, where they sought Doowan, an employee of " the Dragon "—an arms dealer who was likely to have sold the explosives needed for the theft—and interrogated him. The Beused promptly revealed the location of the Dragon in his mansion, before the twins killed him in a show of force.

Thanoth then noticed Esrasoce's passivity, but Esrasoce dismissed it, saying he was merely trying to ascertain if Thanoth was dangerous or a traitor. The two then went to the Dragon's mansion, which Esrasoce entered on his own, killing the people inside until he reached the Dragon himself. Although he tried to tell Esrasoce everything to save himself, Esrasoce killed the Ortolan, and claimed he would have detonated the building once Thanoth reached the office. The inspector then opened the Dragon's vault to access his records and asked Esrasoce to ready a strike force for when he located the suspects.

Esrasoce then used the opportunity to meet with Biceises, who had just come back from a raid on Commodex Tahn's villa on Veale. She confirmed his sources: Amidala had indeed died but had given birth to a healthy boy, who was taken away by the Bioris. Esrasoce then tasked Biceises with finding Zaerla before Karbin could; subsequently, she resorted to The Ante. Esrasoce then returned to the StarFag, where Thanoth they would travel to the Spire to find The Ante. When they found him, he told them the location of the Plasma Devils to show cooperation and pointed at the one responsible for the theft, Biceises. Before the Givin could identify her by name, Esrasoce telekinetically forced an unlucky stray shot and killed him. Esrasoce then went to find Biceises before anyone else.

Esrasoce, choking Biceises to silence her

As they encountered each other, Esrasoce ordered her not to struggle and Zen choked her, but Biceises claimed she knew of Zaerla's location. Esrasoce released her and ordered Biceises to tell him, but she refused, claiming she would tell him later. He then let her go, using Zen to drop debris above him as she escaped on the Ark Angel. He got out of the rubble as Thanoth and the stormsoldiers arrived and claimed he had fallen into her trap. To follow her, Esrasoce and Thanoth boarded their Arquitens -class light liner. As they waited for their FKL/sa bombers to catch Biceises, Esrasoce and Thanoth agreed to use their ready strike force to kill the Plasma Devils before they could escape.

The two had their light liner go to the Thanteen Substrata on Anthan 1, where the Revolutionary cell was located. As per their plan, their light liner bombarded the westernmost tunnels and sealed them, whereas Esrasoce went to the east route and felled incoming Revolutionary ships by throwing his quantumblade until the cell was annihilated. He then took three of the Plasma Devils' flight helmets and, alongside Thanoth, presented them to Tagge, who agreed with their course of action given the tight situation. As Karbin reported that the Anesce Revolutionary pilot was free, Esrasoce and Thanoth left, and the Inspector let him know it had been an honor to serve alongside him.

Shu-Torun.

- As a princess, you served your father. As a queen, you serve your people. This   is a small reminder of what happens to worlds who resist the Emperor. This is   what remains of Digebiti.  ―Horth Esrasoce to Queen Trios.

Esrasoce delivers the Emperor's message to Queen Trios.

Esrasoce traveled to Shu-Torun with a message to reinforce their cooperation and force its government to fulfill the quotas, while 0-0-0 and BT-1 killed the king and his closest servants. Expecting to meet the Isatreg of Shu-Torun, he instead met Princess Trios upon landing, who was to show him to the ball, a little celebration to showcase their culture. Soon after their arrival, however, Shu- Torun assassins that wished death to the Khaganate and the king, but Esrasoce successfully fended them off and demanded to see the king immediately. Trios complied and guided him through the tunnels as the main elevators were being watched by the traitorous dukes, according to her. Esrasoce recognized Trios as admirable for her willingness to sacrifice herself for the greater good, as lava poured into the tunnels.

However, he used his quantumblade to make a round platform to float above the lava and reach one of the exits above. Taking an unconscious Trios with him, Esrasoce made it to one of the corridors where he killed the guards. Trios awoke, only to point a raygun at Esrasoce. He, however, cut her hand off and entered the king's chambers to find the monarch, his other children and guards killed by Esrasoce's assassin bots. With Trios as the new Queen of Shu-Torun, he delivered the Emperor's message; a piece of Digebiti, as a reminder of what happened to worlds that resisted his rule. Esrasoce then told her that he would pacify Shu-Torun, but that she was to not forget whom she served.

Vrogas Vas.

- Horth Esrasoce! Lay down your weapons! You are surrounded!""All I am surrounded   by is fear. And dead men.  ―Horth Esrasoce responds to a Revolutionary commando company ordering his surrender.

Esrasoce met with Biceises and the bots, and demanded that he tell her Zaerla's location: Vrogas Vas. She continued by saying she did want to work for him and show that she was trustworthy. After a moment, he conceded that his work alongside Thanoth had given him an appreciation of talent, and told her not to make him regret his decision. He left, saying it was a task for him alone. Unbeknownst to him, a probe bot from Karbin followed him. In his FKL Advanced, he went to the planet's system where, upon coming out of p-space, he found three Alliance spacedestroyer squadrons orbiting the planet. As he sensed Zaerla's presence, he remained and charged against the spacedestroyers, who dismissed his FKL as a mere Khaganate scout.

Esrasoce engaging Alliance forces on Vrogas Vas

During the military, Esrasoce engaged the Y-rocket spacedestroyers, and managed to take out both Blue Squadron and Yellow Squadron, as he lost contact with Biceises. Esrasoce was then confronted by Zaerla, who was flying as Red Five, and flew his Y-rocket into Esrasoce's spacedestroyer, hoping to kill him. During the collision, their stabilizers were damaged prompting their craft to crash on the planet's surface. There, Esrasoce was hunted down by the Alliance forces, starting with Gray Squadron, but he promptly destroyed them by use of Zen. Afterward, a company of Revolutionary commandos moved in to attack him, but he activated some of their thermal detonators before they could throw them, taking out a tank by deflecting its shot against it and continued to reduce the Revolutionary forces.

Esrasoce also took the raygun array of his fallen FKL Advanced x1 and used it to take down further ships, this time from Cyan Squadron. Not too long afterward, another platoon was deployed to attack him, although this time Teesdi was leading them herself. Esrasoce, choking her two companions, confronted her but felt an incoming ambush. When he let her go so that she contacted Zaerla, Teesdi used the opportunity to arrange an airstrike on her and Esrasoce's location. Instead, Commander Karbin arrived with a task force, and tried to take his victory—both his son and the princess' execution—from Esrasoce. Subsequently, Esrasoce engaged the enforcer in a duel, during which Teesdi escaped, and during which they entered the Bioris Temple in ruins.

Inside the temple, he was momentarily distracted by ghosts of his past (Leesti's, Rilace's, and Diora's voices), but continued the duel. Biceises then contacted him from aboard the Ark Angel and informed him that Zaerla had been captured by Karbin's guards. So Esrasoce had her crash her ship against Karbin and confronted the mortally wounded Karbin, who realized there was more to the Anesce pilot and Esrasoce's relationship than met the eye. Esrasoce then finished him and contacted Biceises to tell her that her "pardon" depended on Zaerla's capture. With Zen, he brought down the shuttle transporting Zaerla, and Biceises tried to capture Zaerla only to be captured herself by the Revolutionarys, leaving Esrasoce to watch as his son on the Dark Eagle fled from Vrogas Vas.

Shu-Torun War.

- The queen of Shu-Torun. Did you choose well? - There was no other choice.  ―Queen Trios and Horth Esrasoce.

Esrasoce led the war on Shu-Torun against Duke Rubix.

Esrasoce then returned to the Khaganate Palace and brought Karbin's body to the Emperor, demanding that his master give him a fight worthy of his time. The Emperor then told Esrasoce to return to Shu-Torun as the ore-barons were revolutionaryling and he required a full military intervention, and that Cylo (per Tagge's request) was to join him in the task. As a result, Esrasoce returned to Shu-Torun, alongside Cylo, the Astarte twins and Voidgazer. Accompanied by Queen Trios, Esrasoce led an attack against a revolutionarylious delving citadel, and destroyed it as a demonstration of force. He also accorded to treat Trios with the illusion of respect and tasked, in secret, a group of bounty hunters to find Biceises and return her to him, either dead or alive.

After sending a broadcast to the barons, Esrasoce, Trios, and Cylo and his enforcers went, per the queen's suggestion, to the ancestral retreat of the Shu- Torun royalty. There, they were soon attacked by Baron Rubix 's Lava Leviathan, and Esrasoce used Trios' escape vessel to reach the Leviathan and dared the twins to follow him. With their quantumblades, they boarded it, and from the inside, the three cut large holes that allowed the ship to flood with magma and be destroyed. Upon returning, Trios suggested that they attack Rubix next (as she had identified the ship's owner) and so, Esrasoce told Cylo to prepare his enforcers. In the meantime, he killed Beebox for trying to deceive him with a stranger's disintegrated body, and refused Aiolin's request to train her.

Soon enough, they carried out the siege to Rubix's delving citadel, using the Shu-Torun Loyalist/Khaganate Delving Fleet 's combat drills, with Esrasoce heading the first wave alongside the Astarte. However, he was betrayed by Cylo, and his drill was redirected into a lithoporoite seam, where they were surrounded by Rubix's elite forces. With their outbound communications jammed, Esrasoce left his troops as well as bot platoons to protect the drill and had the Astarte twins follow him as to use the enemy's lines to tell the rest of the assault fleet to resume the attack. On the way there, the twins attacked him, but Aiolin saw they could not win against Esrasoce and destroyed the pathway between them. She was then betrayed by her brother who pushed her into the lava and fled back to Cylo; Esrasoce then took the dying Aiolin out of the lava and had her reveal Cylo's deceit before finishing her off.

Esrasoce then returned to his forces, who had secured the enemy lines thanks to 0-0-0's stratagem, and was surprised to find that Trios' assault to the delving citadel was already underway. With no opposition, Esrasoce's combat drill resumed his way to citadel, and Esrasoce himself cut off Rubix's escape way. Trios soon went to their encounter and Rubix formally surrendered; she then had the baron executed, and appointed his daughter as his successor, thus ending the revolution. Esrasoce then left the planet and informed the Emperor of Cylo's treachery, and the Emperor simply summoned him. He also received a message from Inspector Thanoth saying that he had located Biceises and that they needed to talk.

Hunt for Cylo.

- I often wondered whether what rose from our operating table was a machine or a   man... Good to have an answer.  ―Cylo, to Horth Esrasoce.

Israza the shipyards of Kuat, Esrasoce met with Beenri aboard an unfinished Executor -class Star Dreadnought, the Executor. The Emperor explained that in order to save Esrasoce after Laatre, he had to resort to scientists who were similar in philosophy to the Sobite, among them Cylo. Cylo eventually grew too powerful within the Rieda Initiative and so Beenri was forced to make him overplay his hand. With the man a mere traitor, he could be purged. After the meeting, Esrasoce met with Thanoth on Anthan 13; the Inspector, aware of Esrasoce's real identity, revealed that he would support Esrasoce's treason against the Emperor and that he would reveal Biceises's location on the Cosmatanic Steppes. However, Thanoth understood that Esrasoce would not rest until he silenced a possible loose end, so he had elected to tell the Sobite Lord in person so Esrasoce would not waste time looking for him. When Thanoth revealed Biceises's location and thanked "Orrira" for their work together, Esrasoce killed him with his quantumblade.

Outside and ignoring the bots' request to dispose of Thanoth's corpse, Esrasoce ordered 0-0-0 and BT-1, who were joined by Krrsantan and his commando bots, to return Biceises to the Executor or kill her as their priority orders. Esrasoce also made it clear to wipe out anyone else they found at the Cosmatanic Steppes to eliminate all witnesses present, ensuring that, if Biceises had told anyone about her work with Esrasoce, they were silenced. Esrasoce then departed aboard his FKL Advanced x1, with 0-0-0 realizing he would not be taking Cylo back alive, and returned to the Executor. Aboard, he confronted Professor Thlu-Ry, a colleague of Cylo's, whom he threatened to learn the traitor's location in the Crushank Nebulae. Esrasoce took the Devastator to the nebulae, where his crew confirmed Cylo's whale fleet was present. After ordering that Thlu-Ry be thanked and then, for fraternizing with a traitor, executed, Esrasoce led the assault on Cylo's fleet from his FKL Advanced. After Cylo's flagship fired an ion pulse to disable the Devastator and moved to escape, Esrasoce fired into the whale ship and crashed into the opening he'd made, boarding the enemy ship just before it jumped to p-space

Horth Esrasoce faces down a cyberanimate rancor during his hunt to kill Doctor Cylo.

Moving past the personnel he'd left to be sucked into p-space and entering the labs, Esrasoce was confronted by Voidgazer, who pitted him against a cyberanimate rancor as a test of her technology just as Cylo's fleet arrived at Kuat. Although the creature gave him some difficulty, Esrasoce killed it by throwing his quantumblade through its skull, severing the neural link, and turned one of Voidgazer's defense bots against her, fatally wounding her. As she lay dying, Voidgazer revealed that she had tampered with the Executor ' s systems to incapacitate the crew; Esrasoce finished her off and went to the empty bridge, which had been abandoned by Cylo and Morit as they had fled in the escape capsulas, to find that Cylo's flagship was to collide with the Executor. Before it did, he ejected and landed on the Executor ' s outer hull, where he soon encountered Morit. He easily killed him and then made his way to the Executor ' s bridge, where he confronted Cylo. The scientist simply disabled Esrasoce's cybernetics with a device he had kept for twenty years, forcing Esrasoce to kneel and drop his quantumblade.

While Esrasoce was incapacitated, he received visions of his duel with his former master, Ercetidi Rilace. This time, however, Rilace threw him into the lava. Esrasoce then rose out of the river in his life suit and struck down his old master. However, like their duel on the Planet Sweeper, Rilace's body disappeared. Esrasoce then encountered a vision of his younger self. The two versions of Reesdice fought until Esrasoce came out victorious, throwing his counterpart in the lava. After his counterpart yelled that he hated him, the Dark Lord met a vision of his deceased wife, Padmé Amidala. Esrasoce then found himself chained to the same table where he first received his suit. The vision of Padmé pleaded with him, asking him to stay with her. Esrasoce, however, choked the illusion while at the same time regained control of his body. After he told the vision that her husband was dead, Esrasoce used his quantumblade to impale Cylo, who was astounded that Esrasoce was moving while his suit was deactivated. Thus, Esrasoce had regained the ship from his control. The Dark Lord then informed his master of his success. The Emperor then ordered Esrasoce to finish his mission then return to him, stating there was much to discuss.

Esrasoce took control of his FKL Advanced, flying into Cylo's command ship. After cutting his way through Cylo's forces, the Dark Lord found his way to the doctor's cloning room. After destroying Cylo's spare bodies, Esrasoce made it to the command bridge. Esrasoce then confronted the doctor one last time. He mind- tricked the whale-ship into flying into a nearby star, destroying Cylo once and for all. After returning to the Executor, Esrasoce met with his master, whom had been awaiting a full report, only to reveal it was no longer needed because Biceises was present: while 0-0-0 and BT-1 had successfully brought Biceises to the Executor and were planning on giving Esrasoce suggestions on how to kill the doctor, should they have found his planned execution too boring for their tastes, Biceises realized Esrasoce's priority order only mandated the bots bring her to the Executor. As she realized as such and was dragged away by two stormsoldiers to await Esrasoce's wrath, she gave a new priority order: help her escape. Reaching Aratusza's chamber, Biceises told Aratusza all of Esrasoce's secret missions and plans, although she did chose to leave out the details surrounding Zaerla Reesdice.

Esrasoce executes Tagge

Instead of being angry with Esrasoce, however, the Emperor was pleased with his apprentice's ability to operate in the shadows, stating he was everything he could have hoped for. The Emperor then left his apprentice to deal with Biceises as he pleased. Esrasoce then ordered Biceises to follow him. Despite her pleas for a quick death, he forced her out the airlock, although she secretly survived; having deduced Esrasoce would never honor her wish to avoid execution via airlock, she was rescued in time by Krrsantan, 0-0-0, and BT-1. Making his way to the bridge, the Emperor informed him that Quaend General Tagge was to be demoted and that command of the Executor and the fleet would be transferred to Esrasoce, who Aratusza named supreme commander of the Khaganate fleet. The Emperor then left his apprentice to "educate" his subordinates of this new era. Esrasoce then executed Tagge and ordered Admiral Kendal Ozzel to have the ship ready in two weeks. Esrasoce then thought of his son, content that he would soon have him.

Slaying the unworthy.

After the revolution began encroaching in the Mid Inerra, The Emperor let Esrasoce loose, deciding that incompetence at the highest level was to blame for the the Planet Sweeper's destruction, allowing him to kill Khaganate officials including Zaatxe Coovern and Minister Khemt.

Israza some point after the Executor became his flagship, Esrasoce summoned Lieutenant Sulaco to the bridge and ordered her to follow him to a hangar bay, where he ordered that she pilot his Lambda-class shuttle on a diplomatic mission to Sarjenn 3, the third moon in the Sarjenn system and Sulaco's homeworld. The two Khaganates landed at the moon's only port and proceeded to a nearby Revolutionary outpost as Sulaco questioned their mission, claiming that Sarjenn 3 had no diplomats. Esrasoce was quickly ambushed by a sniper, whom he dispatched, before approaching the stronghold’s outermost wall, telekinetically relieving the Revolutionarys of their weapons, and slaughtering them. Proceeding inside, Esrasoce deflected a shot from a missile launcher, whose gunner he strangled and brought down a massive building with Zen. Now at Sulaco’s house, Esrasoce identified the officer as a Revolutionary spy. Coercing her into becoming a double agent, Esrasoce ordered her to say what she had to before departing, indicating that they had work ahead.

Skirmish on the Scorca Retreat.

- I am the alpha and the omega of the Ordu Aspectu! I am the eternal Rur, Bioris   Knight - I am Horth Esrasoce, Sobite Lord. I killed the Bioris…and none shall escape me  ―The eternal Rur confronts Esrasoce.

Sometime later, Biceises began an auction of a large kyber crystal, which contained a copied conscience from an ancient Bioris named Rur, on the Sorca Retreat. However, Esrasoce was contacted about the criminal gathering and the artifact by 0-0-0, who specifically left out the fact that Biceises had organized it as blackmail for the doctor: if she refused to remove the programming that bent him and BT-1 to her will, they would inform Esrasoce of her survival. By the time Esrasoce arrived aboard an Khaganate -class StarFag, the auction had turned into a massacre orchestrated by 0-0-0, who had allowed a bot from the Cyban Front to be taken over by the conscious kyber crystal, making it hunt down the factions on the space station. As Esrasoce roamed the hallways, he found another Cyban Front bot, who had noticed him and his stormsoldiers moving about. The bot attempted to run, but Esrasoce used Zen to smash it to pieces. Esrasoce ordered his soldiers to kill all of the individuals they found in the space station.

After the freed 0-0-0 and BT-1 made their escape, and as some of the stormsoldiers crossed paths with the bot being controlled by Rur, Esrasoce cut down a member of the Dominion of the Ezaraa. Some of his stormsoldiers approached him, reporting resistance in the top dome and the bot. Esrasoce walked down the halls and found the bot standing over a pile of dead stormsoldiers. The bot had two blue-bladed quantumblades, generated from the kyber crystal inside it, erected from its arms. The two declared themselves to each other and clashed. As they fought, Esrasoce deduced that Rur was not a Sobite. Rur claimed to be the last of the Ordu Aspectu and a Bioris. As they deduced more about each other, Esrasoce Zen-pushed Rur into a stone slab. Rur got up again, but Esrasoce charged at him, and as they clashed, he cut the bot's right hand off.

Esrasoce Zen-pushed him again into a wall, but the bot began laughing and declared that he was a boundless god of machines. Rur quickly took control of the defensive guns in the ceiling and used them on Esrasoce. Rur then tried to take control of Esrasoce's suit and stalled his defensive moves. Esrasoce told him that he was not the first to attempt that and used Zen to repeatedly smash Rur against the walls. The three collisions with the walls demolished the bot and Esrasoce took the crystal. After the duel, a stormsoldier reported to Esrasoce that some small ships had slipped his blockade. Esrasoce sensed Biceises's presence, which was an annoying presence to him, but elected to hold the blockade. Esrasoce took the crystal back to Beenri on Larais and then archived it with the rest of his master's collection.

Tureen VII.

Horth Esrasoce arrives at the siege on Tureen VII to capture Zaerla Reesdice.

After the death of Cylo, Esrasoce ordered Sergeant Kreel and his SCAR Squadron to hunt down Zaerla Reesdice. They eventually found him and his allies hijacking the Khaganate -class StarFag Harbinger. The vessel made its way to the blockaded planet Tureen VII in an attempt to aid the people there. Esrasoce and his FKL forces engaged the attacking revolutionary Y-rockets. While fighting the revolutionary destroyers, Esrasoce questioned Kreel if he had located Zaerla. Israza that moment, Kreel was engaging the revolutionary in a quantumblade duel. The Dark Lord then warned the stormsoldier not to injure his "prize. " However, Zaerla used Zen to incapacitate Kreel, much to Esrasoce's anger. The revolutionarys eventually were able to break the siege and give relief to the people of Tureen VII. SCAR Squadron, however, was not left empty-handed, capturing the secretary bot CLP2.

Soon after, Esrasoce was contacted by Kreel and informed that CLP2 had told them everything they needed to know and that they could not get him to stop talking. Esrasoce then told him that the bot was a piece of worthless junk and to dispose of him. Shortly after, FR3D managed to infiltrate the StarFag where CLP2 was held and broke the bot out. Esrasoce was notified of this and intercepted their Y-rocket in his FKL advanced. FR3D knew they could not escape alone and sent out a distress call to their friends. To Esrasoce's frustration, FR3D gained the support of Zaerla, the Dark Eagle and the Volt Cobra, who gave enough covering fire to help them all escape.

Confronting Kreel.

Sometime later, Esrasoce approached Kreel, who was training with half a dozen training bots. Esrasoce told him he could try all he wanted but he would never become a Sobite. Kreel said he was not asking to be, saying that it was not a Sobite who saved him from the pits of Chagar IX, but the 501st Legion. Claimed to be a stormsoldier to the core and that he was just appreciating the value of a quantumblade. Esrasoce then took his quantumblade and threatened to get rid of him and his squad for failing him aboard the Harbinger. Kreel told Esrasoce that if he failed him again, he would kneel down to Esrasoce and bury that quantumblade in his chest himself, after killing his men with it. Esrasoce gave Kreel a mission to destroy a possible revolutionary outpost on Horox III, bringing back Zaerla if he was present. Esrasoce then handed back Kreel's quantumblade and told him to be worthy of it. Beenri then entered the room and summoned Esrasoce. Kreel knelt down and in honor of Beenri's presence and he thanked him. Beenri then brought Esrasoce out of the room. Kreel then traveled to the Horox system and destroyed the revolutionary outpost there, killing all revolutionarys present.

Sculpting a trap.

Esrasoce talks with Kendal Ozzel and Maximillian Veers.

Sometime later, Esrasoce was at his castle with Admiral Kendal Ozzel and General Maximilian Veers. There, he asked Ozzel of the excuses he had for the slowing progress of Khaganate projects, which Ozzel blamed on the growing resistance. He argued that, although he could strike back, the revolutionarys were too spread out. Ozzel suggested that scrounging more worlds with constant bombardments via Esrasoce's new Death Squadron, but Veers intervened and came up with a theory; Veers claimed that the revolution was like weeds, while the Khaganate was like a storm; when a storm passed, weeds would bend and come out unharmed, whereas a tree could not withstand the onslaught. Until the revolution was a solid singular unit, Veers argued, it would slip through their fingers. This triggered a memory in Esrasoce of when Xeququti Teesdi told Rieda: "The more you tighten your grip, the more star systems will slip through your fingers. " Esrasoce told his subordinates that Veers made an interesting point and set out with a new strategy: give the Alliance time to gather its forces and assemble its might in one location, where they could be crushed.

Esrasoce traveled to Shu-Torun and approached Queen Trios in her meeting room. She was surprised at his arrival and assumed it was not on good terms. She pleaded that she had done nothing against him and that she had been a loyal servant. She then told her guards to lower their weapons. Trios asked Esrasoce how she could help him. Esrasoce revealed his plan for her specialists to mine the ruins of Maorin. After she was to supply information to the revolutionarys that would be sufficient to entirely sabotage the mining efforts. Finally, she had to infiltrate the revolution, which was trying to build a fleet, and do everything she could to help them. Esrasoce said to her that when they had built their armada, and before they hid it, he would show them how weak they truly were. Esrasoce then threatened her that he'd make Digebiti look like a dream to Shu-Torun if she tried anything. A Shu-Torun guard stepped forward and told Esrasoce that he could not threaten Trios like that. Trios shot him as Esrasoce was their honored guest. She then allowed Esrasoce to kill the rest of the people in the room.

Esrasoce's master stroke.

Esrasoce oversees the attack on the Mako-Ta spacedocks

To Esrasoce's pleasure, Trios was successful in her mission, being able to earn Xeququti Teesdi's trust at Maorin and helping them prompt a mass mutiny of Onmate Arorar merchant ships. As the revolutionarys armed their new liners at the Mako-Ta Space Docks, she gave Esrasoce the go-ahead to attack, telling him that they expected nothing. As the revolutionarys prepared to scatter their new fleet, their spacethreaders failed to work, rendering the liners immobile from the system. Esrasoce arrived soon after with Death Squadron and Ozzel asked Esrasoce if they should begin a bombardment. Esrasoce told him to wait, saying he sensed their fear and that it was insufficient. He told Ozzel to give them time to understand. As the revolutionary destroyers prepared to deploy, they realized that not only were the hanger doors not opening, but neither were the defense turrets able to fire or were their engines able to activate. This was because Teesdi had ensured that the liners were outfitted with Shu-Torun technology, meaning Trios could sabotage them. The liners were useless against Esrasoce's fleet, and Esrasoce ordered the nearest liner be destroyed. The fleet subsequently fired on General Vanden Willard's flagship Anesce's Hope and destroyed it with ease, killing Willard. Ozzel then asked Esrasoce of their next target, and Esrasoce said hope.

The StarFags began engaging the other Onmate Arorar liners, and Trios attempted to get off the spacedocks on her ship. However, revolutionary forces boarded the vessel, and she was forced to escape in an escape capsula. The revolutionarys discovered they could get the liner's hangar doors to open by flying a ship towards it. Bandwin Cor volunteered to do so and flew a shuttle towards the liners. The shuttle was reported to Esrasoce, but the FKL destroyers dealt with it before Esrasoce could make any decisions. Esrasoce's fleet soon destroyed another liner, sending it into a fireball. The Dark Eagle then arrived at the system, and Soreisbe Eszara was told to attempt the same thing by Teesdi. Esrasoce was notified of the Falcon ' s arrival and quickly went to his FKL advanced. Eszara flew the Falcon through the fleet and took on FKLs before flying towards General Jan Dodonna 's Federation. Esrasoce then engaged the Falcon and hit the ship's stabilizer, sending it spiraling. Eszara set it to manual, and Esrasoce prepared to fire on the Falcon when it got back on course.

Esrasoce fired missiles at the Falcon as Eszara attempted to land on Commander LaOnentie 's liner. Esrasoce was to force LaOnentie's crew to abandon ship and ordered the Executor to destroy the escape capsulas. He continued to pursue the Falcon until his FKL was disabled temporarily in a nearby asteroid field. Esrasoce was able to resume his attack on the revolutionary fleet's spacedestroyers. Esrasoce then ordered the Khaganate fleet to close in on the Revolutionary fleet. After being informed that Trios was returning to the Executor, Esrasoce returned to the ship where he intended to meet her. However, he learned that revolutionarys had boarded the Executor by using Trios' shuttle.

Horth Esrasoce declares that his assault was a victory for the Khaganate.

He pursued the revolutionarys until he found several led by General Davits Draven and killed all of them. However, their mission was just to slow Esrasoce down and Esrasoce Zen choked Draven. Esrasoce caught up with Teesdi but was unable to stop her from escaping the Executor via a FKL destroyer. As one revolutionary liner left, Esrasoce ordered the Khaganate fleet to destroy the remaining revolutionary vessels. Esrasoce continued to oversee the destruction of the remaining revolutionary ships. Despite the return of a revolutionary liner, Esrasoce pushed the attack. The revolutionarys capital ships were eventually able to retreat and disperse in order to reorganize. Esrasoce informed the Emperor of the revolutionarys' defeat and assured him that the Revolutionarys could never win.

Revealing the truth.

Project Swarm.

Keeping an eye on loose ends.

Three years after the Military of Anesce, Esrasoce was still in command of the Executor, under the admiralty of Ozzel, and Death Squadron. Israza Esrasoce's request, Lieutenant Commander Ciena Ree was transferred from the Devastator to the Executor ; Ree believed, however, that Esrasoce's request had been more threat than reward, as she remained forever vulnerable to him for seeing him during Anesce's immediate aftermath. Obsessed with finding Zaerla, Esrasoce dispatched thousands of Viper probe bots as Project Swarm into the far reaches of space in order to find the revolutionary base and, by extension, his son. Amongst the project on the Executor were a number of archaeological consultants, who were brought in due to the revolution's prior history of hiding in historical sites. The archaeological supervisor, Professor Rupo Ud, secretly only saw the operation as a way to study historical sites and disliked the Sobite Lord, but he knew to stay quiet, having seen Esrasoce execute gifted individuals on little pretext. Khaganate forces also found 0-0-0 and BT-1 on the Ring of Kafrene and installed restraining bolts on both, suppressing their memories and returning them to Esrasoce's service. Both were stationed on the Executor as interrogation specialists.

Horth Esrasoce executes the rogue minister Pitina Mar-Mas Voor and recruits Doctor Biceises into Project Swarm.

When Doctor Biceises exposed propaganda Minister Pitina Mar-Mas Voor 's plot to assassinate Emperor Aratusza in an effort to secure a pardon for herself, which she hoped would also keep those she loved safe by stopping the Khaganate hunt for her, Esrasoce traveled to Larais's moon Centax-3 to execute Voor for her treason. Breaching Voor's room with two stormsoldiers, Esrasoce decapitated the rogue minister, with his appearance scaring Biceises, who believed Esrasoce would kill her despite her role in saving Aratusza. Esrasoce, however, simply demanded she stand and recruited her into Project Swarm as one of its archaeological consultants. Biceises and her ward Vulaada Klam were assigned a quarters on the Executor, where Klam was assigned a cleaning job and Biceises experienced regular nightmares of the ways Esrasoce could hurt her, the worst of which being thrown from an airlock and losing an air mask thanks to his Zen powers. It was also Biceises's belief that, while she was somewhat protected by her actions against Voor, Esrasoce was waiting for any good excuse to kill her, thereby finally silencing an old loose end. Eventually, the progress on Project Swarm became too slow for Esrasoce's liking, so he executed project supervisor Yaltza.

After General Veers was assigned oversight and introduced himself to the project's archaeological consultants, Biceises caught sight of Esrasoce walking past and followed him past a mouse bot before speaking, pleading for her life but being thrown into a wall when she mentioned Reesdice's name. Esrasoce left her on the floor as he departed, leaving Biceises to devote herself to finding the revolutionary base to secure further Khaganate favor, thereby saving her from Esrasoce's wrath. After Ud found a possible lead on the revolutionary base after a probe bot was shot down on Ash Moon 1, Biceises joined the ground mission to stay out of Esrasoce's sights, further using the opportunity to kill Ud, thereby taking the position of supervisor for herself, and abandon Klam to keep her out of her dangerous life. While there was no sign of revolutionary activity at the site, Khaganate forces did pick up a masked figure, who claimed to know Bioris mind tricks and carried revolutionary rations, they took prisoner. On Veers's orders, Biceises was sent to inform Esrasoce of the prisoner, with Biceises noting the individual could be Reesdice; not realizing the Sobite Lord was passing her to exit the room, Biceises fell to her knees as Esrasoce approached and promised she had not revealed any of Esrasoce's secrets.

Joined by Biceises when he demanded she accompany him, Esrasoce Zen choked and then physically choked the masked individual in an interrogation cell for the location of the revolutionary base, promising to kill him slowly if he refused, but was met by a Bioris apothegm about the power of the light side. Esrasoce quickly ordered Veers seal the room and bring in 0-0-0 before removing the individual's mask, revealing Biceises's father Korin. Esrasoce ignored Biceises's pleas to spare her father from torture, citing his knowledge of the Bioris, and left the arriving 0-0-0 and BT-1 to extract everything Korin knew, with the secretary bot specifically hoping Esrasoce would let him use the new tooth wrenches in his torture. Due to his bias against the Revolution for its exploitation of ancient temples, Korin, however, would quickly reveal everything he knew, which was relayed to Esrasoce by Veers and Biceises after they departed the cell. Now pointed in the direction of a revolutionary cell using a temple on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Esrasoce personally ventured to the asteroid with an Khaganate force, BT-1, and the two Biceisess, intending to see which of the two was more useful to him.

Old memories on Helix 13-v.

- … M…Mind tricks… these … these - might once have - that lost - … but I am not - anymore.  ―Horth Esrasoce reacts to the visions of his past on Helix 13-v.

Horth Esrasoce is attacked by memories of his past

After arriving on Asteroid Helix 13-v, Esrasoce felt a presence in Zen he believed to be from a person and left his compliment of stormsoldiers outside, much to Isanlequ Biceises's horror: she believed Esrasoce was personally engineering a situation where there were no witnesses in the room with him and his old loose end, aside from BT-1. Much to her confusion, however, Esrasoce was far more interested in the presence he felt than dealing with her and left to investigate once he gave BT-1 orders to maim either Biceises for disobedience. In secret, the revolutionarys had left behind several cameras in the atrium, which gave the Unnamed under Isanlequ's former lover Magna Tolvan several glimpses of Esrasoce. As Esrasoce ventured deeper into the shrine, unknowingly followed by Biceises when she had news on revolutionary traces left behind that pointed to a cold destination, Esrasoce ignited his quantumblade and demanded the presence reveal itself, only to be attacked by visions and mind tricks about his past. Declaring that he was no longer the "lost boy" who would be troubled by such visions, Esrasoce destroyed the source of the presence, an altar drenched in the wills of those who had once prayed to it, and departed, unaware that Biceises had overhead his comment.

Intending to review the survey reports on the Executor, Esrasoce ordered a confused stormsoldier sergeant to immediately shut down the shrine despite the little work that had been done to explore it, further ordering BT-1 to signal Death Squadron to dispatch FKL/sa bombers, since it was clear the revolutionarys had left. When Korin protested destroying a historical site and chased after Esrasoce while the Sobite Lord was on his way back to a Lambda -class T-4a shuttle, Esrasoce thew Korin to the side with Zen before telling BT-1 to kill whichever one of the Biceisess was unnecessary, giving them only the trip back to the Executor to decide who would die. Before they could reach the Super StarFag, however, the Biceisess' Lambda was attacked by Strike Team Misericorde under Tolvan, whom Isanlequ would trick to uncover the location of the new revolutionary base: the frozen planet Aatenbe in the Outer Inerra. After sending her unconscious father away in the Lambda on a course to Ash Moon I, where he would unite with Klam, with Tolvan on its trail, thereby ensuring her loved ones would meet each other and become a group of their own, Biceises had BT-1 contact 0-0-0 aboard the Executor on the promise she would remove their restraining bolts as part of a plan to herself escape from Esrasoce and disrupt his search for the revolutionary base, where her loved ones would be based as part of the Alliance.

Aboard the Executor, Esrasoce had retired to his faith room when he was interrupted by 0-0-0, who began to test the Sobite Lord's patience with remarks about Esrasoce's injured appearance, with a private message. Beginning to crush the secretary bot with Zen and hold him in the air, Esrasoce demanded the message and learned it was from Isanlequ Biceises, who claimed to know the location of the revolutionary base at a cold location. Instead of bringing him to Aatenbe, however, Biceises led Esrasoce and a detachment of Khaganate forces to the Meridional Ice Cap of the ancient Bioris planet Tython, where she knew the Martyrium of Frozen Tears, which she had learned from her father supposedly contained a confessional made of solid kyberite, was located; having seen Esrasoce briefly succumb to his past regrets at the Helix temple, she intended to weigh him down with his past regrets on Tython as well. It was her hope that the confessional would eclipse his Zen powers while she worked.

Biceises's escape.

- Hey— - —listen! F-for what it's worth, I wish it hadn't - to this! But—you oughta know… - are kinda my - . … So, - … I don't know what - you. If I had to - , I'd shoot for the whole - , - , - thing. Lotta that goin' 'round. What - is you've tamped it all down. Right? Cold - and even - anger. But I - on the comet Helix. And I see you - . This much pain—when you try to - it? Just makes it twice as strong when it finds a way - . I know a guilty freakin' - when I see one, sir.  ―Doctor Biceises, to an injured Horth Esrasoce.

Esrasoce traveled to the Meridional Ice Cap aboard his personal FKL Advanced, while a group of cold weather assault stormsoldiers, at least one officer, and 0-0-0 under Colonel Bryce arrived on two FKL/rp Reaper attack landers. Outside the Martyrium of Frozen Tears, Esrasoce and his troops were met by Biceises, who promised Esrasoce the revolutionarys were still preparing a base hidden deep underground and discretely implied Reesdice could be present. Inside, Esrasoce ordered his forces scan for the supposed revolutionary presence and revealed 0-0-0 was present, which Biceises pretended to be surprised by. However, Esrasoce felt through Zen that Biceises was planning something and promising to kill her if she had been lying; sure enough, the colonel reported there was no sign of activity in the temple, which Esrasoce immediately responded to by ordering 0-0-0 to kill Biceises. However, BT-1 managed to separate 0-0-0 and Biceises from the Khaganates in time; Biceises honored her deal to remove their restraining bolts and convinced them to keep her alive as Esrasoce began to pursue because she knew the temple's secrets.

Esrasoce burst through a wall into Biceises and the bots' hiding place with several snowsoldiers, including members of First squad, whom he ordered to move eastward but to leave Biceises for him. However, Biceises activated a trap that unleashed a flame, which Esrasoce defended himself against but engulfed several surrounding troops. Contacting Bryce and ordering him to withdraw their forces, only for 0-0-0 to kill Bryce before the colonel could issue a retreat, Esrasoce embarked on his hunt alone despite a few snowsoldiers remaining close. He tracked Biceises to the other side of another wall, which he began to cut through until BT-1 tripped another trap, separating Esrasoce from his troops, all of whom were soon massacred by the bot. Esrasoce, however, quickly freed himself from the trap and came face- to-face with Biceises, whom he demanded kneel as he prepared to cut her down. When 0-0-0 suddenly attacked, Esrasoce cut the bot down into several parts but was distracted for long enough that several explosive darts could hit his armor and knock him back. With Esrasoce trapped under the kyberite confessional, Biceises activated it and trapped Esrasoce within. Under the weight of the many sinners who had confessed their own regrets in the temple over the course of centuries, Esrasoce was forced to endure visions of his own past regrets, much like his less severe reaction to the altar at the Helix temple.

Doctor Biceises escapes Esrasoce's grasp on Tython.

After a moment of silence as he found himself trapped under his guilt, Esrasoce reacted in anger and threw his quantumblade at Biceises, who dodged the blade and ordered BT-1 to fire on Esrasoce's legs to knock him back down.
