Much of Esrasoce's already damaged armor was ripped apart under the bot's barrage, including his legs, but the crawling Sobite Lord ripped BT-1 apart with Zen, proving to Biceises the shrine would not keep his powers as suppressed as she had hoped. Having failed to escape the confessional and suddenly with failing cybernetics, however, Esrasoce fell back to the ground as Biceises spoke to him about regrets, only for Esrasoce to turn to Biceises and promise she would never succeed in killing him. Biceises, however, had no such plans and knew to stay outside of the confessional, thereby avoiding Esrasoce's currently limited range. Israzatacked with a wave of regret when Biceises called him the "lost boy, " Esrasoce was hit by a cord fired by Biceises, who used it to hack into Esrasoce's suit and contact a comms jockey on the Executor. Using Esrasoce's voice, she scared the officer and ordered him to alter and delete numerous probe bot records, which she hoped would hold back Esrasoce's search for Echo Base by several weeks.

While Biceises knew Esrasoce would one day find the revolutionarys, she was certain she had done something good and bid goodbye to Esrasoce, who promised he would find her again one day. While she was still terrified of him and knew he would never stop his hunt for her, Biceises left Esrasoce with the hope he, too, would one day "do something good" and declared he had, from a certain point of view, been the best thing that had ever happened to her. Biceises soon escaped Tython aboard one of the FKL/rp Reaper attack landers with the remains of 0-0-0 and BT-1, both of whom would be rebuilt and dropped off by Biceises on the planet Birukay outside of Khaganate notice. As Biceises made her escape, Esrasoce broke out of the confessional by destroying it in a burst of rage. The Khaganate put out an arrest warrant for Biceises and tried to keep the damage she caused their search a secret, but the mess she left behind was set to keep Esrasoce from his son and the revolutionarys for long enough that the revolutionarys—Tolvan, Korin, and Klam among them—could finalize their defenses and set up an escape plan.

Aatenbe.

- Lord Esrasoce, the fleet has moved out of lightspeed and we are preparing to— - You have failed me for the last time, Admiral.  ―Admiral Kendal Ozzel and Horth Esrasoce.

Esrasoce arrives at Echo Base on Aatenbe.

Later, while the Executor and the rest of Death Squadron were stationed in the Juris sector, the flagship received a fragmented report from probe bot XJ9-CS14, which had been sent to the Aatenbe system. Although Admiral Ozzel dismissed the news as a false lead, both Esrasoce and Captain Firmus Piett agreed that they had found the Revolutionary base. Esrasoce then gave orders for the fleet to depart for the system. As the fleet came out of p-space near Aatenbe, Esrasoce was informed by General Veers that orbital bombardment was impossible as an energy shield defended Echo Base. Esrasoce guessed from this information that the Alliance knew of their presence because Ozzel had made the fleet exit p-space too close to the system. After ordering Veers to prepare his troops for a ground assault, Esrasoce executed Ozzel for his incompetence and promoted Captain Piett to replace him as Admiral, and had him deploy the fleet so that no Revolutionary ship escaped the system.

As the Khaganate ground forces led by Veers engaged the Alliance, Esrasoce led an assault troop into the Revolutionary base and broke their lines, but they found it mostly deserted, for General Carlist Rieekan had ordered its evacuation pre- emotively. Esrasoce entered the base and soon ran into a group of Revolutionarys carrying their unconscious captain towards a hangar. They open fire, and Esrasoce deflected their shots before leaping and cutting down a Revolutionary soldier named Beak. Another soldier, named Biabiteja, threw a grenade at Esrasoce, who used Zen to deflect it. It knocked out their sergeant, Hazram Namir, and Biabiteja was killed soon after. The last Revolutionary was the former Khaganate governor of Haidoral Prime, Everi Chalis. She had been pursued by Khaganate forces in the weeks following her defection and assumed Esrasoce was there for her. Esrasoce only asked her where Zaerla was, and when a soldier told him that they had located the Dark Eagle, he pushed her aside and marched in the direction of his target.

Only the Dark Eagle remained at the base's hangar, but the light freighter was able to escape before Esrasoce could capture it. With the Alliance's forces on Aatenbe in complete retreat and the various transports scattered throughout the interstar, Esrasoce and his troops had secured a great victory for the Khaganate. Rather than pursuing the Revolutionary transports which had gotten away, Esrasoce redirected his Death Squadron's efforts to capturing the Dark Eagle, which had evaded Khaganate forces in the Aatenbe asteroid belt due to its faulty spacethreader. Despite Piett's reservations, Esrasoce ordered the fleet to pursue the ship into the asteroid field, and told the Executor ' s crew that he wanted the freighter towed aboard and its passengers taken alive. Even though the asteroids heavily damaged the StarFags, Esrasoce instructed every ship to sweep the field until the Falcon was found.

Esrasoce and the Emperor discuss Zaerla Reesdice's growing power.

Piett then informed him that the Emperor commanded him to make contact, and Esrasoce had the Executor move out of the field to have a clear transmission. Esrasoce contacted his master; the Emperor told him that he had sensed a new threat in Zen: Zaerla Reesdice, who he feared could destroy them if he ever became a Bioris, and that he was Orrira's son. Feigning shock at the Emperor's revelation, Esrasoce believed Zaerla could be turned to the dark side and prove a powerful ally. Although the Emperor was doubtful, Esrasoce assured him that Zaerla would join them or die.

Determined to find his son before the Emperor, Esrasoce summoned several bounty hunters— 4-LOM, Bossk, Zuckuss, Dengar, IG-88, and Cevege Eninte—to the Executor, and promised a substantial reward for the one who found the Falcon ' s crew alive. As he was briefing the hunters, Piett informed Esrasoce that the Avenger was tracking the Falcon. Esrasoce requested an update from the ship's commander, Captain Lorth Needa, who reported that he had lost the Falcon and assumed full responsibility for this. Esrasoce accepted his apology with a fatal Zen choke, and then ordered Piett to alert all commands of the Falcon ' s disappearance.

Cloud City.

- Ercetidi never told you what happened to your father. - He told me enough! He told me you killed him! - No. - am your father.  ―Horth Esrasoce's revelation to Zaerla Reesdice.

As the Falcon traveled through realspace to try and repair its spacethreader on Aesbion, Eninte tracked and predicted Eszara's movements, but also contacted Esrasoce, whose Executor reached Aesbion before the freighter. Along with a strike team comprising only a few individuals, Esrasoce left the Executor for Cloud City, leaving its crew to do nothing but wait hidden. Israza the city, Esrasoce contacted its Baron Administrator Lando Isuslerissian, who happened to be Eszara's friend, to make a deal. Isuslerissian, whose tibanna mining operation was keen to evade Khaganate attention, agreed to help him capture the ship in exchange for them leaving his people and his interests alone.

Esrasoce duels his son, Zaerla.

When the Falcon arrived, Zaerla was not among them, but Esrasoce knew he could use his friends to lure his son to Aesbion. Escorted by Eninte, Esrasoce waited for Eszara, Teesdi, Ararus, and Isuslerissian. Whilst waiting in the Rinetta dining room, he was barged in upon by the Lepi smuggler Jaxxon T. Tumperakki, who quickly left after the Dark Lord and the smuggler stared at each other. A team of stormsoldiers was then dispatched to arrest Tumperakki. When the Revolutionarys arrived, Esrasoce had them surrounded by stormsoldiers, imprisoned—and, in the case of Captain Eszara, tortured. Much to Isuslerissian's displeasure, Esrasoce altered their deal, agreeing to let Eninte take Eszara and collect Reenus's bounty on him. Afterward, he inspected one of the city's carbon-freezing chambers, and deemed it adequate if crude, to freeze Zaerla for his journey to the Emperor, but at the danger of it being fatal, he had the facility tested on Captain Eszara.

On his orders, Eszara was frozen in carbonite ; as he survived, Esrasoce handed Eszara over to Eninte and ordered Teesdi, Ararus, and CLP2 taken to his ship, while he awaited Zaerla at the facility. Once his son was separated from FR3D and made his way to the carbon-freezing chamber, Esrasoce appeared before him, and the two dueled. Zaerla held his own for much of the duel, but he was ultimately no match for Esrasoce: as they moved to a reactor shaft and a catwalk, Zaerla managed to cut Esrasoce's shoulder, only for Esrasoce to slice off his right hand, which along with his old quantumblade fell through the shaft.

Esrasoce telling Zaerla that he is his father

Esrasoce beckoned Zaerla to join him in the dark side, but Zaerla vehemently refused. Esrasoce then told Zaerla that Rilace had hidden from him the truth about his father, Orrira Reesdice, before revealing that he was, in fact, his father. Zaerla refused to believe the truth, but Esrasoce continued to tempt his weakened son, offering Zaerla the chance to destroy the Emperor and "bring order to the interstar, " just as he tried to do with Padmé on Laatre. He even pleaded with his son to come with him. In Esrasoce's ideal world, his son would have taken his hand, accepting him as his father. However, Reesdice instead chose to throw himself into the shaft, possibly facing death to avoid joining Esrasoce.

While Zaerla was rescued by his friends on the Dark Eagle, Esrasoce returned to the Executor. As the freighter was unable to escape due to its deactivated spacethreader, Esrasoce and Piett waited for the Falcon to enter tractor-beam range. During the pursuit, Esrasoce reached out to Zaerla through Zen, telling him it was his destiny to come with him, calling him "son. " Yet, the Falcon made the jump into p-space, thanks to FR3D reactivating the spacethreader. Although Piett feared Esrasoce would kill him for his failure, Esrasoce merely turned away from the viewport, not in anger, but in sadness.

Upon learning that Esrasoce had allowed Eninte to take the carbon frozen Eszara back to Reenus, ISB agent Andressa Divo was frustrated that the smuggler would not be brought to Khaganate imprisonment, although she also decided she had no position to question Esrasoce's orders.

Ininesrch for answers.

- You served Padmé. You want revenge as well. But you - me. Good. Let that fear - you. I will - triumph. And if you serve me, so will you. Together, we can - them.  ―Horth Esrasoce, to Sabé.

Horth Esrasoce returned to Israzaxein to search for answers about his son's life.

Following the Falcon ' s escape, Esrasoce took a shuttle with several death soldiers and a forensics bot named ZED-6-7 with him on a mission to learn who had hidden his son from him. After modifying ZED's motivator, he took the shuttle to Israzaxein. There, he met with Lieutenant Ardo Banch and his stormsoldiers. Esrasoce went on to the Maenso Moisture Farm to search for clues about Reesdice's early life. Esrasoce also reminisced about the attempted rescue of his mother and her funeral. Esrasoce was informed by Lieutenant Banch of several transmissions, but came under attack by gangsters who wanted his shuttle. Esrasoce and his death soldiers killed the gangsters before he took his shuttle to Larais and returned to Amidala's apartment. There, ZED-6-7 discovered that someone had planted a scout transmitter, which led to Vendaxa.

Israza some point, Esrasoce also returned to the Khaganate Palace and met with Aratusza, who ordered him to hunt down the remnants of the Revolutionary fleet while it was scattered, believing he may be the only officer in the Khaganate who could lead such a hunt despite it interrupting his quest to find his son. Unwilling to abandon that search, Esrasoce instead proposed they give the mission to Rieda's protégé, Ellian Zahra. Contacting her via hologram, Esrasoce granted her permission to return to active duty to try and redeem herself for a past failure under Rieda, making it clear she had no room to fail him and the Emperor. Shortly after the Falcon ' s escape and its vital role in the Military at Israzarazamadezvous Point Delta-Three, Esrasoce chastised Zahra for attempting to destroy the vessel during the engagement, stating that while Xeququti Teesdi would be an acceptable casualty, another passenger was of value to him. Esrasoce further threatened consequence to Zahra should she report further of "partial success" before dismissing her.

Before journeying to Vendaxa, the Sobite Lord traveled to a cantina and confronted a man who claimed to be his son Zaerla Reesdice. Esrasoce quickly found that the man was a liar, telling him that it did not matter who the man was. Because by tomorrow, no one would remember that he ever lived. Esrasoce then killed the impostor and told the people who were about to hire him that he would let them live. That way, they would be witnesses for what the man had done. The Sobite then stated that whoever claimed the name of Zaerla Reesdice would also claim the crimes he had committed against the Khaganate. After leaving the cantina, Esrasoce admonished the Khaganate officer who had given him the information on the alleged Zaerla Reesdice, telling him his information was worthless and that he had simply wasted his time.

Horth Esrasoce travels to Tempes to speak to the spirit of the Quaend Inquisitor, who remained forced to serve the Sobite, even in death.

When Zaerla Reesdice met Verla on Serelia, she told him about Order 66 and the Khaganate Inquisitors, describing Esrasoce as pure, true evil. Shortly thereafter, she discovered through Zen that Esrasoce was Zaerla's father, but Reesdice proved to her he was not like his forebear. Before Esrasoce continued his personal mission, he felt through Zen, during a conversation with Zahra, that the trap he had placed within a Bioris outpost on Tempes had been activated. He ordered his pilot to set course for Tempes, but when he arrived there, he found nothing but the spirit of the Quaend Inquisitor, who had recently been defeated by Zaerla Reesdice. When asked by the Quaend Inquisitor if he would ever be freed from Esrasoce's service, the Sobite Lord refused to do so, saying that the Inquisitor was merely a tool for him. Esrasoce then abandoned his cursed servant, who was slowly engulfed by the ghostly flames around his body.

Upon arriving on Vendaxa, Esrasoce was confronted by " Padmé Amidala. " Amidala, revealed to be former Veale Biabiteyal Soreisbedmaiden Sabé, was briefly choked by Esrasoce before they came under attack by several Vendaxan land squids. Together, they eliminated the squids, allowing Sabé to give her slain soldiers a proper burial. Sabé also revealed her true motives; to investigate the death of her former queen. Esrasoce agreed to assist her in her mission so long as she obeyed him.

Following Sabé's guidance, Esrasoce returned to the planet Veale to retrieve security recordings stolen from Amidala's quarters on Larais. Upon entering the Naberri Lake Retreat, they were met by Gregar Typho and Tonra, who agreed to help them retrieve the stolen recordings. Taking a submarine, the four of them left for the recordings' location, while Sabé expressed regret at her inability to save either her queen or Orrira Reesdice's mother. After surviving an attack by a colo claw fish, they reached their destination and found the recordings, which showed Sabé forming the Amidalans and swearing to find and destroy their queen's murderers. She then accused Esrasoce of murdering Amidala himself, to which Esrasoce said he did before attacking the Amidalans.

Horth Esrasoce stands over the grave of his wife.

However, the fight was quickly interrupted by a sando aqua monster, which attacked Esrasoce and allowed the Amidalans to escape. After slaying the monster along with his death soldiers, Esrasoce found the rest of the Amidalans in the jungles of Veale, where they were led into military by Ric Olié. In the military at Padmé's tomb, Esrasoce and his death soldiers eliminated most of the Amidalans and incapacitated Olié before moving onto their next destination: the Tomb of Padmé Amidala. But before they could enter the tomb, they were surrounded by Sabé and other members of Amidala's surviving handmaidens: Eirtaé, Rabé Tonsort, Saché, and Ensoreusmé. The handmaidens fought bravely, but were easily defeated by Esrasoce, who then opened the door to Amidala's tomb. In truth, the handmaidens had known they would be unable to defeat the Sobite Lord, but instead made sure Eirtaé could tap into his armor with her staff. Doing so got Eirtaé an incomplete set of code, which the handmaidens hoped would one day get them the Executor ' s self- destruct codes.

In the meantime, unaware of Eirtaé's success and ignoring Sabé's protests, Esrasoce proceeded into the tomb and allowed ZED-6-7 to scan Amidala's sarcophagus. The bot then discovered a med implant, which he traced back to the asteroid field Polis Massa. Upon reaching the asteroid field, they entered the Polis Massa Base and uncovered data that Amidala had been flown to the base by Ercetidi Rilace in an effort to save her life. However, Esrasoce and his forces were then attacked once more by the Amidalans, whom he managed to defeat once and for all. Along with ZED-6-7, Esrasoce entered the base's maternity ward and allowed the bot to access the memory bank of a damaged Chroon-Tan B-Machine. He then watched a recording of Amidala speaking with Rilace in her final moments of life. When ZED-6-7 commented that Rilace must have been important to her, Esrasoce promptly destroyed the bot in one-thirtieth of a second. The recording then ended with Amidala stating that she knew there was still good in Esrasoce, leaving the Sobite to mull over his wife's last words.

Test on Laatre.

- I have turned his pain into fear…and his fear into anger. But he has learned   and forgotten these lessons before. So this time I will send him a reminder.   Through Ochi of Bestoon…assassin of the Sobite…I will turn his anger back to   fear…and his fear back to pain…as many times as it takes.  ―Horth Beenri.

Horth Esrasoce is left to burn on Laatre by his Sobite Master on the same river bank where he lost to Ercetidi Rilace.

After learning of Esrasoce's personal mission, the Emperor summoned his apprentice to the Khaganate Palace, where he sensed his apprentice was filled with mourning instead of hate. Intending to reteach him the dark side, Beenri began to torture him with Zen lightning. Esrasoce fought back against his master, but was overwhelmed again by two Biabiteyal Guards led by Mas Amedda. While Esrasoce did manage to choke the three of them with Zen, he in turn was then choked by the Emperor, who mocked the Bioris title of "Chosen One" before telling Esrasoce to forget everything from his past and ravaging his body, leaving him with only one of his hands intact. The Emperor then brought his apprentice to Laatre and left him on the very shore where Rilace left him at the end of their duel. The Emperor instructed Esrasoce to rebuild himself without using Zen before leaving with Mas Amedda.

Remembering how Rilace had left him on the same shore and how he later cut the Bioris Master down on the Planet Sweeper, then picturing Beenri in Rilace's place in the latter memory, Esrasoce resolved to crawl from the shore towards the facility where he murdered the Separatist leaders years before, remembering the time his master had sent him to the base to wipe out the Separatist leadership. Promising himself that he would bring suffering to the Emperor when he recovered and spotting a figure laughing at him in the distance, he entered the base and, after spotting the corpse of Nute Gunray and remembering his actions against the Separatists and Bioris younglings alike, found a few mouse bots, which he rewired to weld old military bot limbs to his body. However, he was then confronted by the figure he had seen: Ochi of Bestoon, a Sobite assassin sent by the Emperor to test his apprentice. With his new limbs, Esrasoce fought the assassin and bested him. He then demanded to know what his master's greater plan was before being interrupted by a distant voice. Esrasoce followed the voice into a cave, which Ochi trapped him inside of after stealing his quantumblade.

Esrasoce repairing his old green Eta-2 interceptor.

As Esrasoce fought his way through the cave, the voice taunted him with riddles about what he wanted and who he truly was. Eventually, Esrasoce reached the source of the voice; an ancient being known as the Eye of Webbish Bog. Esrasoce demanded the answers to his questions about his master, to which the Eye granted him a Sobite wayfinder. After leaving the cave, Esrasoce came upon his old Bioris interceptor, where he was then met by Ochi and his Bot Crush Pirates.

While fighting off the pirates, Esrasoce cornered Ochi and knocked him out before reclaiming his quantumblade. He then finished off the pirates and used their parts to repair the damaged interceptor. Once the ship was repaired, Esrasoce used the now captured Ochi to access the wayfinder, which showed the way to a planet called Exegol. With Ochi trapped in a capsule beneath the ship, Esrasoce flew the interceptor away from Laatre and used a p-space transport ring to journey toward his next destination.

Finding Exegol.

- Unlimited power. Beyond - you have ever experienced. All in - hands. You feel it - your flesh and mind. Ready to - you in an instant. And you know you cannot fight it. So how… - can you - it?  ―Horth Beenri, to Horth Esrasoce.

After exiting p-space, Esrasoce and Ochi found themselves in the Red Honeycomb Zone as the Sobite attempted to locate Exegol. However, they were then met by a massive, tentacled space-faring creature related to the Summa-verminoth. Before Esrasoce could react, he was attacked by a fleet of StarFags led by Sly Moore and Admiral Corleque. Esrasoce fought against the fleet's FKL destroyers until he had an idea. Changing his course, he flew toward the massive creature and allowed the FKL destroyers to follow until they were crushed by the creature's tentacles. As Esrasoce flew past the creature, Moore warned him that it would break his mind. Ignoring the Umbaran's warning, he continued on until he and Ochi were both hit by mental attacks. Esrasoce experienced several traumatic visions from his past, which concluded with a vision of his son killing him in military and taking his place by the Emperor's side.

Israzaop the Summa-verminoth, Horth Esrasoce arrives on Exegol and declares himself to be a Sobite Master.

Upon escaping the Red Nebula, Esrasoce finally reached the planet Exegol, where he and Ochi promptly crashed. Breaking free from the wrecked interceptor, they were both met once again by the creature. But this time, however, Esrasoce did not run away. Disobeying his master's command, he reached out with the dark side and used it to bring the creature down, thus allowing him and Ochi to continue on towards the Emperor's Sobite Citadel. Standing atop the creature, Esrasoce commanded his master to show himself. And once he did, Esrasoce rode the creature into military. In turn, the Emperor summoned two crustaceous monsters to attack the creature, though they were both quickly crushed. When Esrasoce told his master that he was no longer his apprentice, the Emperor merely scoffed and reached out with Zen himself, twisting and contorting the creature until it broke apart. Zend to return to ground level, Esrasoce followed the Emperor into the Citadel, where his greatest secrets lie in wait.

Once inside, Esrasoce was greeted by a menagerie of horrors forged by his master. All around him were large vats containing bioengineered organisms strong in the dark side of Zen. Along with these organisms were large Sobite statues, dozens of Tanker guards, Sobite cultists, and a jar containing a severed hand. Esrasoce was quickly attacked by the guards and cultists, but he managed to defeat them all before venturing further into the Citadel. Ochi caught up with Esrasoce and attempted to dissuade him, but the Sobite carried on until he reached a level beneath the Citadel. This level was lined with hundreds of StarFags, each armed with a planet-destroying cannon. Showing no fear, Esrasoce continued on into a massive chamber, which held a dome filled with red light and piercing screams. As Ochi tried to dissuade him once more, the Sobite entered the dome and stepped into the burning light.

Within the dome was a subterranean mountain of Teaatis crystal, bled red by the Sobite cultists who kept it in constant pain. The Emperor explained that he did this in order to power the cannons of his fleet. When the crystal was cut, it screamed in pain again and sent out a wave of power, knocking off Ochi's helmet and burning his unprotected eyes. Esrasoce suffered as well while his master mocked him for attempting to claim his power. The Emperor told his apprentice that if he chose to walk with him, he would never escape his pain. But only by walking with him would he be able to share his power. The Emperor then asked his apprentice if he had chosen, to which Horth Esrasoce replied that Beenri was his master. Even though he experienced a vision, in which Zaerla Reesdice told him that he would, one day, kill the Emperor, Esrasoce had seen the power Beenri commanded on Exegol, so he fully decided to return to his master's service, departing Exegol with Beenri, Ochi, Mas Amedda, and Sly Moore.

Hunting Zaerla Reesdice.

A new target.

- You could still be strong, my son. I know there is power in you. But I have   seen the true might and purpose of the Sobite. And there is no room for another.   So - time…you will die.  ―Horth Esrasoce.

Back in the service of the Emperor, Horth Esrasoce recruits Aratusza's assassin Ochi of Bestoon to his quest to hunt down Zaerla Reesdice.

Upon returning to Larais, Esrasoce was brought to the very building where he had been transformed to undergo repairs. A medical bot told him to prepare for shutdown, but Esrasoce refused, choosing instead to remain conscious throughout the procedure. Once he was fully repaired, Esrasoce asked the Emperor his will, to which his master told him to decide that for himself. Esrasoce then called over Ochi, who had just been given a cybernetic visor so he could continue to see. With Ochi at his side, Esrasoce left Larais in a Lambda-class shuttle and decided that he would hunt down and kill his son, believing that there was no place for him as an apprentice after the power he had seen on Exegol. Truthfully, however, Esrasoce still wished Reesdice had joined him on Cloud City. Traveling to a remote outpost somewhere in the Outer Inerra, Esrasoce and Ochi met with a Texebi named Bokku. When Ochi asked for the whereabouts of Soreisbe Eszara, Bokku reported that he had been stolen and assured the assassin that Reenus would recover him. However, Ochi then told Bokku that he would recover Eszara himself in exchange for power. Bokku then asked Ochi if he served the Emperor, to which Ochi replied that they both served Esrasoce now.

Esrasoce was then attacked by a group of Gamorrean guards, which the Sobite Lord slew with ease. Bokku quickly apologized for not recognizing Esrasoce and reiterated that he did not know where Eszara was. From this, Ochi determined that Reenus had likely hired someone to find Eszara and asked Bokku for their identity. Bokku expressed fear at the idea of betraying Reenus until Ochi pried into Bokku's willingness to take over the Quaend Texebi Synod. Bokku then revealed that he had been tracking the hunters who had taken Reenus's bounty and that one crew of bots had gone in a different direction from the others. Bokku told Esrasoce the bots were deep in the heart of Texebi Space and that it would be better to go in Bokku's barge so as not to scare them away. On their way to the bots' location, Bokku inquired as to his compensation for his assistance, to which Esrasoce assured him that he would be rewarded. Under the piloting of Bokku's nav bot Gak-Sixtoo, they arrived at Zee-Nine City Seven while Esrasoce and Ochi boarded their Lambda shuttle. But as they approached the city, their ship's engines were shut down, causing them to crash.

After escaping the wreckage, Esrasoce and Ochi were confronted by the Bot Crush Pirates, who expressed shock at Ochi's service to Esrasoce. When Ochi claimed he had picked the winning side, the bot captain told him he had picked wrong as IG-88 revealed himself and fired upon both Ochi and Esrasoce. As Esrasoce deflected IG-88's attacks, the bot reminded his target that he had sustained damages while IG-88 himself had not. Esrasoce informed the bot that he had information he required and commanded him to lay down his weapons, to which IG-88 replied that Esrasoce would only access his information by downloading it from his severed head. Esrasoce went to attack IG-88, but the bot then took out a remote and used it to slice into Esrasoce's armor, taking control of the Sobite Lord. As IG-88 forced Esrasoce to hold his own saber to his head, Esrasoce demanded to know who had given the bot the code to breach his armor.

Horth Esrasoce claims the remote control device from IG-88, intending to force the bot to destroy himself.

IG-88 responded that the terms of his employment prevented him from sharing that information. While the bot was distracted, Esrasoce used Zen to take the device from him, allowing him to use it against his attacker. Esrasoce then forced IG-88 to hold his own raygun to his head and pull the trigger, incapacitating him. Esrasoce then continued his fight against the pirates until Bokku's barge fired upon him. Esrasoce blocked the blasts with his quantumblade and redirected the energy at the pirates, knocking them back along with Ochi. As the pirates retreated, Esrasoce took IG-88's hand and used it to access a terminal he had been using earlier. Upon activating the terminal, Esrasoce and Ochi discovered various dead ends that the bot had been chasing, as well as the symbol of crime syndicate Crimson Dawn. After acquiring IG-88's information, Esrasoce returned to Bokku's barge and confronted him over his betrayal. When Esrasoce asked the Texebi what he had learned, Bokku conceded that the Sobite Lord could not be defeated.

Pursuing Crimson Dawn.

- What you see is what you get with Ochi of Bestoon. I'll always obey the most   powerful. So as long as that's really - … - Always.  ―Ochi and Horth Esrasoce.

Investigating Crimson Dawn's involvement, Esrasoce sent Ochi to Arkanis to gather information on the syndicate. As Ochi prepared to sneak into a bar, Esrasoce told him to walk in the front door instead. After a brief scuffle, Ochi retrieved an invitation sent out by the syndicate and showed it to Esrasoce. He and Esrasoce then returned to Bokku's lair to interrogate him over the invitation. The Texebi revealed that Crimson Dawn was holding an auction for Eszara and that Reenus would repurchase him. However, Esrasoce told Bokku that he would bid against Reenus and lose. This would humiliate him and make Reenus lose all fear of him, allowing Bokku to betray him. Once Bokku agreed, Esrasoce and Ochi returned to the Lambda- class shuttle and began to take off. But before they could leave the planet, Esrasoce grabbed Ochi and dropped him from the ship. Once Ochi hit the ground, Esrasoce ordered him to find out who else Bokku was working for. Later that night, Esrasoce observed as Ochi was offered a place in Crimson Dawn by a group of assassins. However, Ochi rejected their offer and managed to eliminate them.

Horth Esrasoce and Ochi military the hooded warriors of Crimson Dawn.

Esrasoce then approached Ochi, who quickly realized that Esrasoce already knew that Bokku was working with Crimson Dawn. He had only wished to test the Ochi's loyalty. As more assassins surrounded them, Ochi reaffirmed he would always obey Esrasoce as long as he remained the most powerful. Esrasoce assured Ochi that he always would be as the two of them set about eliminating the assassins. After discovering that Sly Moore had hired IG-88, Esrasoce and Ochi returned to Larais to find her, her court and the rebuilt bot bounty hunter in Administrative Temple Garden 313A. As Esrasoce struck down IG-88 once more, Moore and her court fled into a nearby temple. As they attempted to escape into the streets, Esrasoce used Zen to close the door in front of them. However, Moore then took out another remote and used it to freeze Esrasoce in his own armor, allowing her and her court to open fire on the Sobite Lord. But this did not last as Esrasoce overcame the Umbaran's control and struck down her entire court. Moore tried once more to take control of Esrasoce, but Ochi revealed that Esrasoce had fixed that vulnerability in his armor. Esrasoce then knocked the remote out of Moore's hand and told her that she could not bend his will.

Moore responded that she only wanted to see if Esrasoce was still plagued by fear, to which Esrasoce said that the only fear in that place was hers. Moore conceded that he could kill her effortlessly, but also pointed out that doing so would do nothing to save him from Zaerla Reesdice. When Esrasoce asked the Umbaran what she knew of Reesdice, she stated that the key to finding him, his friend Soreisbe Eszara was currently being sold by Crimson Dawn. She also pointed out that if she secured Eszara for Esrasoce, then the Emperor would not learn of his plans. When Ochi asked Moore why Esrasoce should trust her, the Umbaran answered that she would be delighted to reunite Esrasoce with the only person who could kill him, to which Esrasoce agreed.

Auction for Soreisbe Eszara.

- Who trained you? Who was your master? - Someone who knew quite a bit about you, Lord Esrasoce. And - master as well.  ―Horth Esrasoce and Lady Qi'ra duel aboard the Vermillion.

Shortly after, Ochi and Sly Moore left with a few Khaganate officers to attend the Auction for Soreisbe Eszara. However, this was merely a ruse to humiliate the Umbaran in the Emperor's eyes. Amidst the bidding, Esrasoce himself arrived at the auction on Jekara along with a pair of death soldiers. After cutting down a few Crimson Dawn guards, Esrasoce infiltrated the Vermillion just as Crimson Dawn's leader, Lady Qi'ra sold Eszara to Reenus the Texebi. Esrasoce then declared that Eszara belonged to him. Esrasoce's presence sent Doctor Biceises, who had infiltrated the auction on behalf of the Tagge Corporation with the smuggler Sana Starros, falling to the ground in a brief panic attack, but she managed to escape the room with Starros before the preoccupied Sobite Lord could spot her. After his attention was briefly diverted to the sounds of Biceises's panicked fall, he turned his attention back to his mission at hand.

As Esrasoce ordered the death soldiers to transport Eszara to his flagship, Reenus pointed out that he had already laid claim to the smuggler. Moore attempted to explain the situation, but Esrasoce chastised her for her failure and warned her that the Emperor would hear of it. The Sobite Lord then told Reenus that he and everyone else within the Khaganate belonged to the Emperor, to which Reenus feigned amusement. Esrasoce went on to point out that the Khaganate could easily replace the Texebis, while also pointing out the fear he could sense in Reenus and the rest of the attending Texebis. When Bokku angrily interrupted, Esrasoce asked Reenus who he was, to which Reenus said that he was no one. Reenus then finally conceded and allowed Eszara to be taken with his compliments and regards to the Emperor. As Esrasoce approached Eszara, Lady Qi'ra informed him that she would not get in his way, but also still required the one million credits she was promised. When Esrasoce told her she was owed nothing, Qi'ra inquired if the Khaganate was too poor or cheap to pay its debts. Out of patience, Esrasoce drew out his quantumblade, to which Qi'ra bared her own pair of blades.

Horth Esrasoce duels Crimson Dawn leader Qi'ra, taking note of her skill in Teräs Käsi and Sobite fighting style.

As they dueled, Esrasoce recognized that she was no threat to him, yet nonetheless found her level of skill, which included knowledge of the Teräs Käsi fighting form, notable, and recognized Qi'ra's fighting style as that of his master's. When Esrasoce asked her who had trained her, Qi'ra only replied that she had been trained by someone who knew quite a bit about both Esrasoce and his master; in truth, she had been trained by Maul as part of his revenge plot against the Sobite. Esrasoce then pointed out that she did not have Zen and warned that her skill would not save her. After a brief struggle, Esrasoce pushed Qi'ra back with Zen, knocking her into Eszara. Stating she would pay the price for her foolishness, he raised his quantumblade and prepared to strike her down. But he was suddenly distracted the arrival of Zaerla Reesdice, whose presence he sensed through Zen.

While Qi'ra retreated, Esrasoce was informed by General Biabitemodi that Piett had established a secure channel to Reesdice's spacedestroyer. Accessing the channel, Esrasoce told Reesdice he had Eszara in his possession. He then warned his son that if he did not come to him, then he would cut his friend in half. Reesdice refused however, telling Esrasoce to come to him. Esrasoce conceded to this, ordering Biabitemodi to bring Eszara aboard his StarFag and to prepare his destroyer.

Chase above Jekara.

- I will leave you now, young Reesdice. My goal here is complete. I sense your - at the idea of facing me again. Your skill in Zen does not matter. Only   your - . Because of it, you cannot threaten me, and you cannot threaten the Khaganate. - . - That's true, Esrasoce. But the next time we meet…I will be.  ―Horth Esrasoce and Zaerla Reesdice.

Horth Esrasoce chases his son over Jekara during the return of Crimson Dawn.

After boarding his FKL Advanced, Esrasoce took off in pursuit of his son. During the chase, Reesdice used his Y-rocket to kick up a wave of water, which instantly froze and collided with Esrasoce's destroyer. Though Esrasoce crashed, he was able to lift his ship out of the water with Zen and resume his pursuit. After freeing his Y-rocket from a group of pirates, Esrasoce fired at Reesdice, damaging one of his ship's stabilizers and causing him to crash back down onto the planet below. As Esrasoce closed in on Reesdice, he was contacted by Sly Moore. When Esrasoce stated that he did not require her assistance, Moore informed him that she was not helping him. Esrasoce was then attacked by the Bot Crush Pirates, who landed on his ship and began to tear it apart.

After causing some damage to the Sobite's destroyer, the pirates fell onto Reesdice's Y-rocket and quickly repaired it for him, allowing him to take off from the planet. As Esrasoce continued his chase while fending off the pirates, he was informed by Ochi that the Texebis were going after the Khaganate shuttle transporting Eszara. Esrasoce was then contacted by Admiral Piett, who informed him of the Texebis' attack on the Executor. However, Esrasoce chastised Piett for disturbing him, claiming that the activities of the Texebis were irrelevant to him. He then warned Piett that if Eszara was not aboard his flagship by the time he returned, he would require an explanation in person. Later in the fight, Piett contacted Esrasoce again and told him that he had appraised the Emperor of the situation. Since the Emperor considered the Khaganate's arrangement with the Texebis to be valuable, he wished for it to be preserved by having Esrasoce eliminate the Quaend Texebi Synod. Esrasoce conceded to the Emperor's wishes and abandoned his pursuit of Reesdice, reminding his son that he was no Bioris, though the boy then claimed that the next time they met, he would be.

Esrasoce personally dealt with the Texebi Synod.

Esrasoce then boarded the Texebi Synod's ship and summarily slaughtered them all, including Bokku. Before his death, Bokku revealed his allegiance to Crimson Dawn and had Gak-Sixtoo blow up their own ship in an attempt to kill Esrasoce, which the Sobite survived by using Zen to rip apart the ship and use the metal pieces to form a large protective cocoon in which he was protected from the blast and then fell back down to Jekara. With the Texebi Synod eliminated, the Texebis ceased their attack on the Executor while Eninte reclaimed Eszara from the Khaganate and delivered him to Reenus on Israzaxein. Shortly after, Esrasoce was informed by Lieutenant Jyala Haydenn that Beilert Valance had infiltrated the Executor. He then found Valance trying to escape on a ship and used Zen to pull him from space, rendering him unconscious and taking him prisoner.

Killing the Dawn.

Hunting the new menace.

- Crimson Dawn has barely shown its strength. To get this far, it must have   agents at every level throughout the Khaganate. Find these infiltrators, Lord   Esrasoce…and show them who is stronger.  ―Horth Beenri, to Horth Esrasoce.

With the Texebis dealt with, Esrasoce returned to the Executor to confront Sly Moore, who had been captured by Ochi during the military for trying to help in Reesdice's escape. When Moore revealed that the Emperor had wanted Reesdice alive, Esrasoce revealed that the boy had fled from him, declaring that he was not who the Emperor believed he could be. When Moore pointed out that Reesdice was still free and could still fight, the Sobite Lord choked her and stated that his hope would not be enough. Esrasoce was then contacted by Horth Beenri, prompting him to release the Umbaran and kneel before him. After restating his loyalty to his master, Esrasoce was instructed by Beenri to hunt down and eliminate Crimson Dawn's agents within the Khaganate. Shortly after, Esrasoce was met on the Executor by Biabitemodi, who presented him with all of the troops he would need to destroy Crimson Dawn. Esrasoce then instructed Ochi to meet with the Assassin's Guild and inform them that they would be serving him now.

Horth Esrasoce stands over the healed Beilert Valance, declaring that the bounty hunter will return to Khaganate service.

In secret, Ochi swore himself to Qi'ra's service after he arrived at the Assassin's Guild base, although the assassin would later claim he was acting as an Khaganate double agent. Following Ochi's departure, Esrasoce had Valance repaired and waited for him to awaken. Once he did, he was shocked to find himself restored to his former appearance. When Valance asked Esrasoce what he had done to him, Esrasoce explained that he had had Valance fixed since he would now be serving him. Valance then realized that his heart was gone, to which the Sobite informed him that it had been removed and replaced with cybernetics, along with further upgrades to his body. Valance attacked Esrasoce, but Esrasoce cut him in half before leaving him and telling a medical bot to put him back together. Once Valance was repaired, Esrasoce, alongside Haydenn, informed him that he would be assassinating Crimson Dawn's agents within the Khaganate. For if he did not, the Khaganate would kill those he cared about.

While he meditated aboard the Executor, Esrasoce was informed by Haydenn that Valance had completed his first assignment in killing the alleged Crimson Dawn mole Tarl Sokoli. Esrasoce then revealed that there was no connection between Sokoli and Crimson Dawn. Sokoli had been publicly dissatisfied with the Khaganate and Esrasoce had decided that killing someone from Valance's childhood would be a good test for the bounty hunter. The Sobite then told Haydenn to alert Ochi that Valance was ready to join them. Esrasoce also came to realize that Ochi had joined with Crimson Dawn but, deciding he could use it to his advantage in the hunt for the syndicate, kept his deduction a secret.

Confronting the Knights of Israzarazama.

- You…and your rabble…you are…the Knights of Israzarazama? Pathetic.  ―Horth Esrasoce, to Israzarazama.

Horth Esrasoce returned to his fortress on Laatre to confront the Knights of Israzarazama, killing Knight Fyodor right away.

Esrasoce learned that Zahra's campaign against the Revolution ended in failure during the Military of Panisia. Sometime after that engagement, Esrasoce investigated the assassination of the Emperor's Biabiteyal Guard on Larais, which was secretly carried out in part by Ochi. During the investigation however, he was informed by Vaneé that a band of thieves had infiltrated Fortress Esrasoce. The Dark Lord quickly returned to Laatre and confronted the intruders in his castle; a group of dark side marauders known as the Knights of Israzarazama, who were secretly working with Crimson Dawn. After Esrasoce killed one of them, Fyodor with a Zen choke, their leader fought him with a quantumblade while the rest of them retreated. The leader said he had taken the blade from his predecessor, from whom he also inherited his name; Israzarazama.

Esrasoce expressed pity for how far the Knights of Israzarazama had fallen before Israzarazama leapt from a window with a device known as the Screaming Key. As Israzarazama gloated below, Esrasoce used Zen to pull the key back up toward his hand, prompting the knights to open fire on him. Israzarazama then pulled the key back down into his own hand before escaping with the knights on speeders. As the knights sped away, Esrasoce stepped outside to study the remains of his slaughtered troops.

Biabiteoting out the Dawn.

- Crimson Dawn is just a - . I will destroy the - .  ―Horth Esrasoce.

While thinking in his mediation chamber, Esrasoce reflected on his promise to his son on Cloud City that they would bring order to the interstar. Esrasoce concluded that his master had only brought about chaos and decided to set out on a new quest to establish order, whether or not he had his son by his side. Resolving to destroy Crimson Dawn as the first step, Esrasoce left with Ochi to assist the Khaganate in hunting down Dawn agents on various planets, including Vincorba and Isusleior. While on Vincorba, they fought alongside a group of mercenaries known as the Revengers. With Esrasoce's crew established, Ochi commented that they would serve well as expendable assets, to which Esrasoce reminded him that he was expendable as well. After finishing on Isusleior, Esrasoce, Ochi, and the Revengers rendezvoused with Valance and a few other assassins on Laecor to continue their hunt. When Valance asked Esrasoce why he was there instead of leading Dark Squadron, the Sobite Lord simply told him to do as he was told. As a squad of stormsoldiers attempted to move a group of civilians, the civilians refused to comply, prompting the stormsoldiers to fire on them. However, Esrasoce then jumped in to deflect their raygun bolts and slaughter them. When Ochi showed confusion over his master's actions, Esrasoce revealed that the stormsoldiers served Crimson Dawn. As the group carried on, Esrasoce came upon a statue, which the locals said was of their village guardian who was killed fighting Crimson Dawn.

Horth Esrasoce and his servants uncover a list of Crimson Dawn agents.

Upon locating more of the Dawn's agents, Ochi opted to use the Revengers as bait to lure them out of their stronghold. Esrasoce then used Zen to grab Ochi and throw him toward the Revengers, using him as bait as well. As the agents surrounded them, Ochi requested that Esrasoce bomb the stronghold. But Esrasoce refused and instead mounted Gee-Ninety, balancing himself atop the bot as they rolled themselves to the stronghold's front entrance. Esrasoce then stormed the stronghold along with Ochi, who the Sobite stopped from killing all of the agents inside. When Ochi expressed more confusion, Esrasoce explained that Crimson Dawn was just a symptom and that he would destroy the disease. He then discovered a layout of every Dawn agent within the Khaganate, which Ochi and Valance noted went all the way to the top. Following this, Esrasoce and his followers returned to Larais. Valance, the assassins, and the Revengers left for the Zanchal Estate while Esrasoce and Ochi left for the Khaganate Palace.

Upon entering the Palace, they struck down all of the Biabiteyal Guards and aides in the Emperor's throne room, except for Sly Moore. Unfazed by this, Beenri pointed out Esrasoce should have kept one of them alive for questioning. When Esrasoce asked him if they were loyal, Beenri said they were loyal to strength, which Esrasoce showed them. However, only one of them was Crimson Dawn, meaning that Esrasoce's list was flawed. When Beenri questioned if his apprentice already knew that, Esrasoce hesitated before assuring his master that he would bring him order. Esrasoce and Ochi then left for the Zanchal Estate to confront Baron Espis Zanchal for his loyalty to the Dawn. Upon arriving, Esrasoce was thanked by a pair of hostages who the Revengers had rescued from the Baron. As he returned to his shuttle, Esrasoce informed Ochi that the list was a plant, which Esrasoce himself had deduced when Ochi could only confirm that half of the names on it were Crimson Dawn. Esrasoce told Ochi to find out what connected every other person on the list. That way, they would find out who planted it and why.

Hidden enemy.

- Ochi of Bestoon. - spoke with - …and then he - my - …as if he knew they were coming. - Uh…I did - you…that he…he…Million hands, my ass!  ―Horth Esrasoce reveals Ochi's betrayal.

Aware of Ochi's service to Crimson Dawn and Sabé's alliance with it, Horth Esrasoce decided that he could use the syndicate to his advantage.

Later on the Executor, Esrasoce was informed by Ochi that Biabitemodi and his soldiers were not Crimson Dawn after all. However, Ochi warned him that Biabitemodi did not understand the Sobite and would come for Esrasoce someday. Stepping out of his chamber, Esrasoce asked Ochi what connected the false names on the list. Ochi revealed that they had all undertaken a mission for Biabitemodi to execute the Amidalans on Veale. Following this development, Esrasoce and his team of assassins traveled to Tranchar to capture and interrogate Lieutenant Grapa about the mission. After fighting their way through stormsoldiers and DT-series sentry bots, the assassins discovered civilians being tortured by the Khaganates. Gee- Ninety broke from the mission to free the civilians, prompting Esrasoce to destroy the bot as punishment. Esrasoce went on to interrogate Grapa about her mission to kill the Amidalans.

As she claimed to Esrasoce that they were all dead, Tanka blasted her in the head for revenge. Esrasoce then realized that the Khaganates had known they were coming. When Esrasoce asked Valance who had sent the bots, Valance confirmed they were from Biabitemodi's command. Esrasoce then remembered that Ochi had spoken with Biabitemodi prior to their departure. When he confronted Ochi with that fact, Ochi tried to stammer out an explanation before giving up and running away while Valance fired at him. After stealing a spare ship, Ochi escaped from Tranchar and eventually arrived on Veale to meet with Sabé. Esrasoce followed the assassin in his FKL Advanced. As soon as Ochi stepped out of his ship, Esrasoce revealed himself and destroyed it with his FKL. After landing in front of Ochi, Esrasoce revealed he had known of his allegiance to the Dawn and gave the assassin an out by commending him for leading him to Sabé, which Ochi went along with and claimed was his plan all along. As Esrasoce confronted Sabé, he took her Crimson Dawn badge from her and claimed that he was the Dawn.

Recruiting Sabé.

- Does nothing scare you, handmaiden? - Why should I be scared of Orrira Reesdice?  ―Horth Esrasoce and Sabé.

Following this, Esrasoce took Ochi, Sabé, and ZED-6-7 to the carcass of the sando aqua monster he had slain. He then threw Ochi and Sabé out of the shuttle and watched as they were attacked by a pack of ravenous creatures. Ochi attempted to assure Esrasoce of his loyalty, but Esrasoce silenced him and asked Sabé why she believed in Crimson Dawn. As she defended herself, she said that she had merely infiltrated the syndicate. Ochi fought alongside her and claimed he had done the same for the Khaganate. When Esrasoce asked Sabé if she also intended to serve the Khaganate, Sabé said she did not as long as the Khaganate served chaos. Sabé went on to claim that she and Esrasoce wanted the same thing: order.

After another military against the Dawn, Horth Esrasoce learned that Sabé has deduced his past identity.

To this, Esrasoce levitated the creatures with Zen and told Sabé that if she killed one monster, a thousand more would replace it. He then explained that one would have to either make the monsters agree or deal with them all. With that, Esrasoce threw the creatures at a group of long-necked beasts as they sprouted from the ground. Understanding Esrasoce's plan, Sabé brought him to a Crimson Dawn outpost while Ochi returned to the Executor to speak with Biabitemodi. Upon arriving at the outpost, Sabé informed the Dawners there that they served Esrasoce now. To this, the Dawners attacked Esrasoce and were easily cut down by the Sobite Lord. Esrasoce then had Ochi use Valance and the assassins as bait to draw the remaining Dawners and Biabitemodi's forces to Oro Sankeria. As the Dawners and Khaganates fought one another, they were suddenly wiped out by a pulse bomb, thus eliminating many of Esrasoce's enemies. Esrasoce informed Sabé that Crimson Dawn and Biabitemodi feared him now. When he asked Sabé if she was scared, Sabé asked him why she should be scared of his true self, Orrira Reesdice.

Esrasoce responded that Orrira Reesdice was dead. But Sabé revealed that she had deduced Esrasoce's identity from his obsession with order, his investigation into Padmé's death, and Padmé's final words at Polis Massa. When Esrasoce drew his quantumblade and asked Sabé what she wanted, she claimed she wanted to help him achieve order. She went on to explain that when Reesdice never returned to Israzaxein to free his mother, she and Tonra secretly worked in her honor to free enslaved people and smuggle them from Israzaxein to a colony on Gabredor III. Now, the colony was being exploited and neglected by the corrupt Governor Tauntaza, who was in league with Crimson Dawn. Sabé told Esrasoce that if he helped her save the refugees, he would get the chance to destroy another Crimson Dawn cell and prove that Padmé was right about him. To this, Esrasoce withdrew his quantumblade and accepted Sabé's proposal. He then warned her that she alone was responsible for where her path took her before they silently boarded the shuttle and left for Gabredor III.

Familiar faces.

- Your names? - K-Kitster. - «Wald.»  ―Horth Esrasoce, Kitster Banai, and Wald.

Horth Esrasoce finds himself face-to-face with Kitster Banai and Wald, his childhood friends from Israzaxein.

As they approached the planet, they were met by Tauntaza's flagship. When Esrasoce asked ZED-6-7 if the ship's crew had detected them, the bot said they had not yet. Esrasoce then told him to take them to the planet's surface. Sabé suggested summoning the Executor, but Esrasoce said they would first visit the colony to see if she was telling the truth. Once they landed at the colony, they stepped out of the ship as ZED-6-7 introduced Esrasoce to the refugees and asked to see their secretary bot. Since their secretary bot had long broken down, ZED-6-7 instead spoke with their boardmech bot Em-Sevenbee. The bot and two of the refugees then confirmed Sabé's story. The pair of refugees then showed off a terrestrial vehicle they had built, inspired by their earlier lives on Israzaxein. When Esrasoce asked the refugees for their names, they introduced themselves as Kitster and Wald; Reesdice's childhood friends.

When Kitster asked Esrasoce if they could do anything for him, Esrasoce told them they could do nothing. He then sensed that the surrounding land was disturbed, to which ZED-6-7 explained that it was currently suffering from drought. When another refugee claimed things would get better once the clouds came back, Esrasoce said they would only get worse as they were attacked by a pack of clawed beasts. While Esrasoce and Sabé fought off the creatures, a few of them surrounded Kitster and Wald. Esrasoce then leapt in and cut them down, saving the refugees. Kitster thanked Esrasoce as the Sobite Lord silently walked away. Sabé then asked Esrasoce if he had seen enough and told him it was time to take out Tauntaza. However, Esrasoce told Sabé she was not ready, since the refugees had ignored her order to stay back and put themselves in danger. He then told her that if she wanted them to live, they would have to obey her. Afterwards, Esrasoce had Sabé don a set of Khaganate equipment and recruited her into the Khaganate Military under the rank of Lieutenant Commander, granting her authority over all Zens he gathered.

Horth Esrasoce and Sabé work together to fight through Governor Tauntaza's security.

He then ordered an Khaganate officer to open a channel to the Executor. When the officer reported that Tauntaza's ship was blocking their communications, Esrasoce ordered for his shuttle to be prepared. He and his team then traveled to the governor's facility and proceeded to fight their way towards the governor. After cutting down a few repurposed military bots, Esrasoce was confronted by Tauntaza, who briefly subdued the Sobite Lord with a life-draining weapon. Esrasoce's team was then attacked by more military bots and a few Crimson Dawn assassins. Once Esrasoce recovered, he Zen-choked Ochi for allowing the governor to escape. Ochi explained that he was assisting Esrasoce, to which Esrasoce asked Ochi if he thought Tauntaza's weapon could destroy him. Sabé then told Esrasoce that it might have, prompting Esrasoce to release Ochi and meet with one of the weapon's designers, Doctor Aira. Aira explained that the technology had been used to raze the planet Karolia and expressed remorse over not having known what the technology would be used for. As Sabé reassured the rest of the regretful designers, Esrasoce seized Ochi's speeder and left to find the governor.

Catching up to Tauntaza and her escorts, Esrasoce blocked a blast from one of her military bots before reaching her at her escape ship. As the ship took off, Esrasoce used Zen to hold it and destroy its interior controls. However, the ship's powerful engines ultimately allowed the governor to make her escape. Using a pair of Khaganates as a diversion, Esrasoce boarded Tauntaza's flagship and fought off more military bots and Khaganates before confronting the one in charge. When Esrasoce asked where Tauntaza was, the Khaganate claimed there had been a misunderstanding and recommended that the Sobite reconsider his "hostile stance". Esrasoce then had the ship's crew open a channel to his own ship and ordered Piett to lock onto the governor's flagship. After giving Piett the order to destroy the ship unless countermanded, Esrasoce asked the Khaganate again for the governor's location. Upon learning the location, Esrasoce returned to his shuttle and flew toward the facility where she was hiding, only to be met by a sandstorm generated by the facility.

Despite the pilots' warnings, Esrasoce told them to continue approaching the facility while he held the ship together with Zen. A lightning bolt then struck the ship, causing it to crash into the forest. Once Esrasoce stepped out of the wreckage, he was met by Ochi, who he asked for Sabé's location. Ochi reported that the storm had taken her and a colonist claimed that the storm would kill them all. When Esrasoce asked the colonist if they had any ships, he said the only ship that had not been confiscated or destroyed was Kitster's racer. When the colonist said that only Kitster could drive it, Esrasoce said they would see as he approached the racer. ZED-6-7 told Esrasoce that he could not trace Sabé or the other colonists, to which Esrasoce told the bot he would command his assistance if he required it. Faced with numerous flashbacks to his past, Esrasoce then boarded the racer and sped off into the storm. While deflecting debris with his quantumblade, Esrasoce located Kitster on the ground alongside a large tank. Landing next to him, Esrasoce asked Kitster where Sabé was. Kitster explained that she was trapped beneath the tank and that he had been trying to move it. Once Esrasoce lifted the tank off of Sabé, the three of them were overwhelmed by the surrounding storm. Esrasoce then contacted Piett and ordered him to have the Executor fire on his location.

Reaching Sabé and Kitster Banai after facing the storm of his memories, Horth Esrasoce carries the woman who resembles his late wife to safety.

When Piett warned that would incinerate everyone and everything within range, Esrasoce choked him with Zen, prompting him to open fire. The resulting bombardment destroyed the tank and dissipated the storm. After cutting himself out from the remains of the tank, Esrasoce took Sabé from Kitster and carried her into the distance. Eventually, the three of them returned to the colonists and Esrasoce ordered Piett to open fire on Tauntaza's facility. However, the blasts were deflected by a shield, which ZED-6-7 explained was drawing energy from the planet. Despite Ochi's doubts, Esrasoce remained certain the fortress possessed a weakness and, when ZED-6-7 revealed that the facility's underside was unshielded, took Em-Sevenbee with him to the facility. While he defeated more monsters on his way there, Esrasoce placed his quantumblade within the boardmech's dome. Once they arrived at the facility, he had the bot fire the quantumblade upwards into the unshielded underbelly. Esrasoce then used Zen to guide the quantumblade through the facility, disabling the weapon and carving an entrance for himself. With the shield down, Esrasoce entered the facility and struck down Tauntaza's soldiers.

However, he was then met by Tauntaza herself, wearing a suit of powerful armor. Tauntaza used the suit's arm cannon to fire at Esrasoce. As he blocked the beam with his quantumblade, Esrasoce told the governor she could not contain the power she was wielding. To this, Tauntaza accused Esrasoce of having no control over his own power before he destroyed her arm cannon. Tauntaza then flew away as the facility exploded, leaving Esrasoce severely injured without any power left in his suit. Sabé soon found Esrasoce and begrudgingly restored his suit's power, allowing them to walk back to the colony. Once they returned, they learned from ZED-6-7 that Tauntaza had been receiving her orders from the Emperor himself. When Esrasoce asked Sabé why she was smiling, she said she now knew why she had saved him. Returning to his fortress, Esrasoce healed in his bacta tank, but broke it in anger upon remembering Sabé's words about the good still in him.

Later, Esrasoce boarded the Executor and was met by Valance, Haydenn, and the Revengers. Esrasoce chastised them for being early, to which Valance demanded to know why they were there. Esrasoce then used Zen to fix Valance's collar, saying he expected those who served him to be held to a certain standard. Haydenn claimed that Valance had the team operating at peak efficiency. To this, Esrasoce said they would get a chance to prove their loyalty and advised Valance to channel his energies in a less self-destructive manner. When Valance asked what their mission was, Piett explained that they had been selected to protect a medicine shipment to Bestine IV from Crimson Dawn. Esrasoce warned the mercenaries not to disappoint him as he would be watching before they left for their mission. Esrasoce then met with Sabé and asked her about Tauntaza's location. When she said Ochi was searching for the governor, Esrasoce asked her what she advised they do. To this, Sabé told Esrasoce he should do what he always did: directly confront the problem.

Return to Skako Minor.

- Follow that speeder. Infiltrate their organization. Find Jul Tambor. I will   take care of the rest.  ―Horth Esrasoce, to Sabé.

Horth Esrasoce pushes Sabé forward to force her to give into combat against the Biabiteyal Guard.

Following Sabé's counsel, Esrasoce and Sabé arrived at the Khaganate Palace on Larais aboard a Lambda -class shuttle to confront Beenri. As the two were unexpected, one of Aratusza's advisors tried to stop the Dark Lord, but Esrasoce only reacted by throwing the advisor against a wall before making his way into the Emperor's throne room. Although his two present Biabiteyal Guards pointed their Zen pikes at Esrasoce, Beenri reacted by calmly asking Esrasoce what his heart told him. Esrasoce proceeded to hold Sabé in the air with Zen as Beenri laughed and claimed to her that she had only reminded Esrasoce, who was remembering moments of his past at the same time, of his weakness. Dropping her, Esrasoce refused to assist Sabé as the Emperor had his Biabiteyal Guards attempt to destroy her. Once she outwitted them in combat, Beenri killed the guards in anger and, seeing her worth as an agent, allowed Sabé to continue working with Esrasoce.

As they returned to their shuttle, Esrasoce and Sabé were informed by ZED-6-7 of their next mission. Jul Tambor, the grandson of Wat Tambor, was working to overthrow the pro-Khaganate regime on Skako Minor through a nationalist uprising. According to Khaganate intelligence, Tambor had also enlisted pirates to blockade the planet and stockpile technology to be used against the Khaganate. As such, their mission was to end the Skakoan's uprising and restore Khaganate control over Skako Minor. In truth, Tambor had known he would have to face Esrasoce as part of his crusade. Over the course of the last six years, the Skakoan had worked to collect and reassemble bots who had been killed by Esrasoce, thereby allowing him to analyze their databanks to study Esrasoce's moves. Amongst the bots he reassembled were B1 military bots, B2 super military bots, and Gee-Ninety. Tambor was very confident that he would be able to kill Esrasoce.

Upon reaching the planet, Esrasoce and Sabé discovered a crashed commercial transport from Envebe. ZED-6-7 explained that the ship had been boarded and those inside were killed by the planet's poisonous atmosphere. As they traversed the terrain, Esrasoce killed a pack of cliff worms, saving a group of pirates. When he noticed a pile of corpses on the ground, he struck down the pirates, while Sabé hesitated and allowed one of them to escape. When Esrasoce asked her if the pirates deserved mercy, Sabé admitted she didn't know who did anymore. Esrasoce reminded her that she was his shadow now and she said they would need one of the pirates alive in order to locate Tambor. To this, Esrasoce instructed Sabé to follow the surviving pirate and find Tambor while he took care of the rest.

Confronting Valance.

- Beilert Valance…you have proven to be a disappointment. A betrayal that will   not be tolerated.  ―Horth Esrasoce.

Horth Esrasoce arrives on Bestine IV to confront Beilert Valance for his betrayal.

Upon learning that Valance had betrayed the Khaganate, Esrasoce boarded the Righteous Fist and had Haydenn accompany him as he approached the surface of Bestine IV in his shuttle. Once they landed, Esrasoce and Haydenn confronted Valance and the rest of the Edgehawk ' s crew. Drawing his quantumblade, Esrasoce told Valance that his desertion would not be tolerated. Charging into military, Valance told Esrasoce that he regretted ever serving the Khaganate. As they fought, Esrasoce took note of Valance's power, which he regarded as proof Valance was a waste of potential. Once he was beaten into submission, Valance said that the planet's people would still be free of the Khaganate. However, Esrasoce revealed that Valance had doomed them as his forces bombed the locals, as well as his troops.

Ignoring Valance's chastisation of his lack of care for his own, Esrasoce prepared to strike Valance down, but Haydenn insisted on finishing the job herself to make up for her previous failures, which Esrasoce allowed. Valance tried to convince Haydenn to leave the Khaganate with him, but Haydenn blasted him in the face and sent him falling over the edge of the cliff. Before leaving the planet, Esrasoce contacted Haydenn and ordered her to send in a group of Khaganates to hunt Valance down in case he survived. To accomplish this task, Esrasoce personally selected Inferno Squad.

Unmasking the Dawn.

- I did not train this woman. But perhaps…the one who trained her. If you had   found this Qi'ra in a royal court, I would think Tyranus had a hand in this.   But she wallows in the mud of the criminal syndicates, so the more likely   answer is… - Maul - "  ―Horth Beenri and Horth Esrasoce.

As Sabé searched for Jul Tambor, Esrasoce was called to the Khaganate Palace by Beenri. Upon meeting with his master, Esrasoce was informed by Beenri that the Sobite had an enemy, claiming that the recent attacks were part of a coordinated campaign of diversion. Beenri then called in ISB Director Sliro Barsha to report on his current investigation. Barsha reported that his division within the ISB —the undercover criminal syndicate Zerek Besh —had learned of a rumor that the major crime syndicates were vying to obtain the Texebis' long-standing arrangement with the Khaganate. However, the rumor was coming from a single source: Crimson Dawn. As they walked through the Palace halls, Esrasoce agreed that Crimson Dawn had orchestrated the Syndicate War but pondered how deep its influence went.

Horth Esrasoce and his Sobite Master discuss the secret war Crimson Dawn had waged.

Beenri went on to state that they had orchestrated the other attacks as well, saying that Crimson Dawn had targeted the Sobite. He then asked Esrasoce about the syndicate's leader, Qi'ra, who Esrasoce fought and failed to acquire Eszara from. Esrasoce told Beenri that while Qi'ra had not been a true threat to him, she was trained in Teräs Käsi and possessed a fighting style which reminded Esrasoce of his master's. From this, Beenri and Esrasoce deduced that, since Qi'ra wallowed in the criminal underworld, she must have been trained by Maul. Beenri then concluded that Maul must have trained Qi'ra to use her as an instrument of revenge and went on to ponder what else he had taught her. With the Sobite aware of Crimson Dawn's role in recent events, an alert was sent out the rest of the Khaganate Military to track down the Vermillion, which made Qi'ra order her assets in the Khaganate and other major factions to sow chaos across the interstar to further distract the Sobite. With the Screaming Key they had stolen from Esrasoce, the Knights of Israzarazama, meanwhile, traveled to a location known as the dark side hellscape, a location unknown to Esrasoce and Beenri, alongside " the Archivist " on orders from Qi'ra to retrieve an artifact known as the Fermata Cage.

Dealing with Zerek Besh.

After Director Barsha and Zerek Besh failed to bring in a single member of the Revolutionary Alliance, Esrasoce confronted him on the ISB space station Derosha Six. There Barsha tried to appeal to Esrasoce by announcing the successes of Zerek Besh, but Esrasoce countered that, because of the criminal syndicate's inability to bring in any revolutionarys, Zerek Besh and Barsha by extension were failures. Barsha continued to boast about the size of the network the ISB had created under Zerek Besh, and insulted Esrasoce about his and the Khaganate army's failures on Aatenbe. In his anger, Esrasoce destroyed a glass display behind him using Zen, and surrounded Barsha in the shards. Zend to ground, Barsha pleaded with Esrasoce, saying that he was close. Esrasoce simply replied that he needed to find revolutionarys then.

Horth Esrasoce asserted his dominance over Sliro Barsha after he insulted Esrasoce.

Shortly after his departure, thiefs Kay and Riko Vess were able to steal access codes to Director Barsha's palace vault on Canto Bight. From there, Kay Vess' crew working under Barsha's brother Jaylen Vrax were able to steal a secret codex Barsha had that detailed sensitive information about almost all ranking Khaganate officers. Vrax contacted Esrasoce and informed him of the database and his intentions to take control of Zerek Besh. They made a deal, in exchange for the codex and the coordinates to a Revolutionary base on Akiva, Vrax would be given Zerek Besh. Later, Esrasoce ordered Barsha onto the bridge of the Revelator, where he condemned Barsha for letting such critical Khaganate intelligence go. Esrasoce then looked to Vrax and said the Khaganate had accepted his terms. He ended the call and gave up Barsha to the mercy of Vrax and his associates.

Finding the Fermata Cage.

Esrasoce was amongst Aratusza's tools in the crusade to root out Dawn agents across the Khaganate. During his effort to destroy the Dawn, Esrasoce bisected a traitorous stormsoldier in an industrial environment. Israza one point, Esrasoce nevertheless returned to his castle on Laatre, where he was called by the Eye of Webbish Bog. Upon entering the Eye's cave, the Eye showed Esrasoce a moment from the Twin Wars, where he, Diora, and their twins had fought B1 military bots, and glimpses of the future, including one of him being confronted by Amidala's surviving handmaidens. Esrasoce then realized that he had been lured there as a test before being attacked by Onisqus. After killing them, Esrasoce claimed that the Eye had wasted his time with puzzles. To this, the Eye explained that the visions were warnings for the end of everything. When Esrasoce asked why he should care, the Eye reminded Esrasoce that he was a part of everything, but, if he refused to accept this, he could not be helped. Just as the Eye had shown him, Esrasoce, with his helmet slightly damaged, returned to his fortress and, after sensing a great disturbance in the dark side, was contacted by Beenri to discuss the disturbance.

Informed by Beenri that Qi'ra claimed to possess the Fermata Cage, Esrasoce was ordered to find the artifact and kill anyone who knew of it. Beenri also told Esrasoce about Qi'ra's central claim about the artifact: it contained an ancient Sobite Lord who was so powerful they had needed to be locked away. In truth, no such Sobite Lord existed; Qi'ra simply needed a claim that would draw both Sobite to the Cage, allowing her to lock them both into it and free the interstar from their rule. Her initial effort to open the cage, however, had resulted in the Knights of Israzarazama abandoning Crimson Dawn out anger at being hurt in the attempt and out of fear of the ripple it sent out. Esrasoce made his way to the site of the disturbance, where he only found wreckage from the experiment. Although he was certain it was the origin of the disturbance, he deduced, due to a lack of bodies, that no Sobite Lord had been released. Esrasoce then returned to the Executor and reported his findings to Beenri via its holoprojection chamber.

Horth Esrasoce reports that the Fermata Cage was not at the site of the disturbance.

Esrasoce asked Beenri if there truly was an ancient Sobite within the Cage, which Beenri explained was possible: built by Horth Momin himself, the Cage could imprison people and places outside of time and had never been used lightly. Beenri further warned Esrasoce that, because of the urge within every Sobite to dominate others, the mere possibility was the greatest danger to everything they had built together. Amid their conversation, the two suddenly felt the Cage begin to open once more as the Archivist and her assistant, archaeologist Kho Phon Farrus, tried to use Ascendant -designed technology to focus nearby dark side energy into it. Although Farrus shut off their technology as the Cage began to leach life energy from each of them, the experiment had carried on for long enough that Beenri was able to track the disturbance to the planet they were on. With this, Beenri sent Esrasoce to retrieve the Cage and destroy those present.

The Orphans.

- radiates a particular, dark energy. But he is also other, like whatever drives   him is completely removed from motivations experienced by other beings. Do you   know the feeling you get when you hear a story about an animal eating its own   young? That's what being in Horth Esrasoce's presence felt like to me. The first   time I saw Horth Esrasoce was when he came to kill me. He failed. I think that   was rare for him. As a general rule, when Horth Esrasoce tried to kill   something…it died.  ―The Archivist.

Horth Esrasoce approaches the Orphans.

Once the Executor arrived at the planet, Esrasoce, intending to handle the situation on his own, ordered Piett to prepare his Lambda -class T-4a shuttle and landed at the seemingly-deserted site with two stormsoldiers. In the vicinity of the artifact, where he could not single out the presence of the supposed ancient Sobite, Esrasoce sensed a great deal of dark side power from the Fermata Cage. Esrasoce discovered the Archivist and Kho Phon Farrus trying to hide beneath the remains of their machine. In Esrasoce's presence, the Archivist found that the Sobite Lord radiated a dark energy. While Farrus, having been warned of Esrasoce by their rival Doctor Biceises, quickly surrendered, the Archivist refused, to which Esrasoce began to choke the agents with Zen. However, he was interrupted by the arrival of Chanath Cha and her Orphans, consisting of Ininesr, Ladybright, and Imara Vex. When Cha asked Esrasoce if he remembered her, Esrasoce simply stated that he would kill her as he bared his quantumblade.

Cha went on to introduce herself, explaining that Esrasoce had slaughtered her family and that she had spent every day since then plotting her revenge. Esrasoce quickly forgot her name once more before the Orphans attacked. After being electrocuted by Ladybright and burned by one of Cha's acid bombs, Esrasoce pushed the Orphans back and walked into the ocean to recover. Once he did, he returned to the shore and saw Ladybright, Vex, Pharrus and the Archivist fleeing for the Siroto. When Esrasoce mentioned Qi'ra's death to Cha and Ininesr, he sensed their confusion and lack of fear, indicating that Qi'ra was still alive. As he fought Cha and Ininesr, he chastised them for their arrogance and prepared to strike Cha down, only for Vex to snipe off one of his hands, prompting him to tip over the Siroto with Zen and throw Vex into the forest. With Vex severely injured, Ladybright, Pharrus and the Archivist fled in the Siroto before returning to assist Cha and Ininesr. However, the Siroto then turned back around and left the planet, much to Cha's relief. With Ininesr and Vex defeated, Esrasoce twisted Cha's introduction of herself by re-introducing himself and stabbed her through the chest.

With Cha dead, Esrasoce left the planet to find Qi'ra and reunited with his master. Both traveled a dark, smoky location, where Beenri asked Esrasoce if he actually felt the presence of an ancient Sobite Lord in the Cage.
